# Changelog

All notable changes to this project are documented here.
Format: [Keep a Changelog](https://keepachangelog.com/en/1.1.0/).

## [Unreleased]

### Fixed
- **App Store compliance audit 2026-10-30** (#971; #973 #974 #975): checked against the App Review Guidelines (privacy policy, labels and manifests, accounts and deletion, Sign in with Apple, HealthKit, camera and AI, subscriptions, UGC, purpose strings). **Privacy policy** gets an *AI features (Anthropic)* section listing what each feature sends: Ask Coach (incl. machine photos), Talk to Coach mid-set, Coach's week review (sleep, HRV, mood: health data), plan setup, What can I eat?, Save from a video, gym / fridge scans and the community checks; Anthropic keeps it 30 days at most and never trains on it; Ask Coach and scans no longer sit under *Kept on your device only*; last updated 2026-10-30 (test `legal.test.ts`). **Camera permission** text no longer says nothing is sent: scans send that photo to Coach's AI. **Privacy manifests**: the widgets extension gets its own (App Group UserDefaults, reason `1C8F.1`), the app adds `1C8F.1`. **Store listing** (`listing.json`): the privacy paragraph no longer says health data is never shared, Pro lists gym scan, fridge scan and Save from a video, review notes explain the AI features. `docs/app-store-review.md`: 16+ age rating override to match the Terms, 5.1.2(i) and purpose-string rows, Purchase History, widget manifest. In-app AI consent (5.1.2(i)) follows in #972.

### Security
- **Security alerts triage 2026-10-30** (#964): Dependabot (`npm audit` 0), CodeQL JS/python/actions (local run: only the known `hibp.ts` false positive) and secret scanning (clean) checked on `main`. Swift alerts could not be read from the session (alerts API 403, Swift needs macOS), so a new manual workflow **CodeQL export** (`codeql-export.yml`) runs the default-setup suite with `upload: never` and prints every result to the job log and summary; its first Swift run found 0 results (167 of 634 Swift files covered by autobuild). Docs `security-alerts.md`, `ci-cd.md`.

### Added
- **Film whole session, hands-free** (#957, designs A + B + C from `record-workout/session/proposals.png`): Film my workout needed a tap between exercises. Train's 🎥 now asks **Step by step** (as before) or **Whole session** (last pick remembered). Whole session: prop the phone once and train; each set is found on its own, its move named (a set after a rest is most likely the same move: it gets an edge; a different move starts a new exercise), reps and form counted, the set logged after 6 s without a rep. Weights: a number in view between sets (dumbbell, plate, machine label) is read for the next set, else last time's weight (marked * in the review). **A** live screen: camera, skeleton, NOW card (move, set, reps, kg) and a timeline of moves (dashed amber = not sure). **B** dark screen (🌙): display at its lowest, big move and set count, saves battery over a long session. **Coach voice** says each new move and *Squat, set 3, 8 reps* (🔊 to mute). **C** Apple Watch: each new move shows on the wrist with ✓ / ✗ (✗ takes the next guess), no walking back to the phone. Picking the phone up pauses everything (the set in progress is kept); set it down with you in frame and it goes on. **Stop · review** lists every move: amber ones offer the other guesses and **Right**, each move has its sets (reps, kg, remove) and **Watch last set**, then **Save workout** (history, Apple Health, Undo) or Discard. Form check (and Film my workout) now only count a rep that starts from the start position, so stepping into view half-way down, or the end of the move before, is no phantom rep. On the iPhone only; one clip per move (its last set) in the temp folder, deleted on Save, Discard and at launch. Engine `FilmSession` (`add`, `accept`, `read`, `fill`, `confirm`, `reject`, `pause`, `resume`, `end`), `FilmWorkout.guess(current:)`, `sameMoveBoost` (tests `FilmSessionTests`); app `FilmModeSheet`, `FilmSessionView`, `FilmMotion` (CoreMotion), `FormCamera.textWithPose`; Watch `WFilmGuess`, `WatchEvent.filmAnswer`, `FilmGuessView`, `WatchSync.sendFilmGuess`.
- **Skip just the climb on a workout + climb day** (#966, design C = A + B from `skip-day-part/proposals.png`): on a *1 Lift · 2 Climb* day, Skip only skipped the gym workout, so skipping climbing meant cancelling the workout first. Now the day line's sport part (*2 Climb*) menu ends with **Skip climb today** (*Skip <sport>* on coming days, this week or later), also on the card's long press: that day's sport goes (Undo bar, 5 s), the workout stays. On such a day the **Skip** tile asks which part: **Skip <workout>**, **Skip climb** or **Skip whole day** (workout and sport, one Undo bar); long press has *Skip whole day* too. Days with one part keep one-tap Skip. App `DayComboRow.onSportSkip`, `NextUpCard.skipSport` / `skipWholeDay`, `skipDay(title:alsoUndo:)`.
- **Place types for climbing, snow and every sport** (#960, design A + B + C from `place-types/proposals.png`): Log climb offered *Fitnesspark Puls 5*, a fitness gym, as a climbing place: places had no type. Now every place has one (climbing gym, crag, ski resort, snow park, nordic centre, trail, bike park, pool, beach / lake, dive site, court, pitch / track, studio, skate park, golf course, gym, other). **A** a place found on Apple Maps takes its type from the Maps category when it fits the sport; otherwise, and for typed or *You're here* places, **What is it?** asks once with bold type tiles. Each sport's **Where** chips and search only list places of its types (search hint *Climbing gym, crag…*), with the type under each name; places logged before keep showing for the sport they were logged for. **B** **Account → Your places**: a gradient section per group (Gyms, Climbing, Snow, Trails, Water, Courts & pitches, Studios, Other), tap a place for **★ home spot**, long press to change its type or remove it, + adds one, *Set a type* lists older places; a new log opens on its group's home spot, else where the sport was done last. **C** **Your gym → This gym also has**: Boulder wall, Pool, Courts, Studio, Ring tiles; a gym is offered for Hyrox, CrossFit and rowing, and for climbing, swimming, racket sports, yoga, martial arts or boxing only with the matching tile. Synced with the profile (`placeBook`, `SavedGym.extras`); API climbs / sports `place.kind`. Engine `PlaceKind`, `PlaceGroup`, `GymExtra`, `PlaceBook`, `SessionPlaces.start` (tests `SessionPlaceTests`); app `YourPlacesView`, `GymExtrasGrid`.
- **What's new 2026-10-30.3** (bundle 89, TestFlight 89): Every machine holds together (animation art fixes, #954); 2026-10-30.2 moved to history as build 88.
- **What's new 2026-10-30.2** (bundle 88, TestFlight 88): Moves are back (animations play again, #951), Film my workout (#953), Climb on a gym day (#948); 2026-10-30.1 moved to history as build 87.
- **Film my workout** (#952, design B + A's sets that log themselves, from `record-workout/proposals.png`): a member had to type every set, and the form check only watched one set of a known exercise. Now a red 🎥 button beside **Custom gym session** on Train opens **Film my workout**: **1 Show** the dumbbell, kettlebell, plate or machine label to the camera and the weight is read off its print (kg or lb, taken after three equal reads; ± and Bodyweight too); **2 Lift** with the phone propped side on: after 2 reps a card names the move (*Looks like · 92 %* plus two alternatives, one tap), after 4 the top guess is taken on its own, and the earlier reps still count; reps, weight and form score show live, with the form cue; a set **logs itself after 6 s without a rep** (Undo for 5 s); **3 Review**: sets × reps, kg and form, every set editable, **Watch last set** (optional), **Another set**, **Next exercise** or **Finish workout** → *Workout built* lists the moves, **Log workout** saves it to history (and Apple Health) as a custom gym session, optionally also as one of My workouts. The move is named on the iPhone by running every form check model over the frames (closeness to the guide 70 %, range 30 %, planned today and recent moves get an edge); only exercises with a form check model can be named. In a custom session the queue bar gets **Film** (the move is added with its sets logged), and the player's **Film this set** now fills reps and weight into the next sets. Nothing leaves the iPhone; the review clip is a temp file of the last set (2 min max) deleted on Next, on close and at launch. Engine `FilmWorkout` (`guess`, `setEnded`), `FilmedMove`, `FilmedSet`, `WeightReading` (tests `FilmWorkoutTests`); app `FilmWorkoutView`, `ClipRecorder`, `FormCamera.onText` / `clip`, `AppModel.recordFilmed`.
- **Smoother exercise animations: 60 fps clips** (#944, option A from `smooth-animations/compare.gif`): the movement played 9 light frames (or 17–49 HD frames) with quick dissolves between them, so it still stepped. Every catalog exercise now has a **movement clip**: the same art drawn with 61 eased in-betweens from start to end and played at 60 fps, at the same tempo, holds, reps, path dot and tempo chip as before. The light clip (360×270, HEVC) is bundled; the **HD pack** carries the full-size clip (720×540) inside each exercise's `.mwf` file (after the frames, so apps from before ignore it). Clips have no alpha channel: each picture is stacked, colour over its alpha as grey, and the app masks one with the other (Core Image) over the category gradient. At rest the still frames show as before; Reduce Motion keeps whole frames. Bundled light frames go from 9 to 3 (start, middle, end), so the app stays a little smaller overall (light frames 38 → 12.6 MB, light clips 23.5 MB). The HD pack grows from 171 to 235 MB (HD clips 64 MB). Art pipeline: new `video.py` (run by `generate.py`; needs ffmpeg with libx265) writes `ios/MonkeyWorkout/Resources/Motion/motion-<id>.mp4` and `ios/HDMotion/<id>.mp4`; `pack.py` appends the HD clip (`MCLP`); the HD animations pack workflow also runs on `ios/HDMotion/**`. Engine `MotionPlayback.clipPieces`, `clipFrames`, `clipFPS` (tests `ExerciseImageTests`); app `MotionClips`, `MotionClipPlayer`, `HDAnimations.clipURL`, `HDFrames.clip`.
- **Ask Coach and snap your machine from Add exercises** (#934, design A from `picker-coach/proposals.png`): the *Add exercises* picker had only search and filters, so a member couldn't ask Coach or take a picture to find the machine or exercise. Now the same lime **Ask Coach** bar as the freestyle screen sits under the title: tap to type, 🎙 to talk, 📷 to **snap the machine** (photo library where there's no camera). Coach answers with up to 4 exercises as cards with **+** (ticks it in the picker, numbered in tap order like the grid) and **Add all**; for a photo it names the machine and gives a setup tip. Hidden with the Ask Coach tile setting. Pro (Ask Coach). API: `POST /v1/me/coach-chat` screen `picker` answers `exercises` like `freestyle`. App `CoachFocus.picker`, `CoachFreestyle.start` optional (no ▶ in the picker).
- **Insights focus** (#939, design A + C from `insights-focus/proposal-a.png` / `proposal-c.png`): Insights showed the same board to everyone, whatever mattered to them. The first time a member opens Insights (after the app tour) a full-screen **What matters most to you?** grid offers **Strength**, **Muscle**, **Weight loss**, **Endurance**, **Mind & recovery** and **My sport**, with the plan goal's focus already picked (*YOUR GOAL*, so onboarding isn't asked twice), or *A bit of everything*. The focus's widgets move to the top of the board; nothing is removed and Edit Insights still adds and arranges every widget. The board opens on a **focus hero card** with its key number (PRs and best lift, sessions this week and most worked muscle, weight change, VO₂ max, Mind score, or the main sport's sessions in 30 days); tap it for the part's page, **Switch** to change focus any time. Synced with the profile. Engine `InsightsFocus`, `ProgressBoard.focused(on:sports:)` (tests `InsightsFocusTests`); app `InsightsFocusPicker`, `InsightsFocusHero`, `InsightsFocusSheet`, `InsightsFocusChip`, `SyncedProfile.insightsFocus`; demo screens `insights-focus`, `insights-focus-pick`.
- **Lighter app, HD animations on download** (#932, design A + B from `hd-animations/proposals.png`): exercise animations were 171 MB of the app (441 exercises, 10,361 frames). The app now bundles **9 frames per exercise at half size (~38 MB, ~133 MB less)**; they still play, slightly softer. Every frame at full size is the **HD pack**: after sign-up and the tour (or after updating), a one-time sheet shows the light and HD picture side by side with **Download HD (Wi-Fi)** / Later; **Settings → Look → HD animations** has the switch (off deletes the files), progress, *Download on Wi-Fi only* and Try again; *HD moves ready* toast when done. Downloads resume on app open and back on Wi-Fi; per exercise the HD frames show once their file matches the build, else the light ones. Art pipeline: `generate.py` writes HD frames to `ios/HDAnimations/<id>/<frame>.png` and the bundled light frames (`lite.py`); new workflow **HD animations pack** (`hd-animations.yml`, `pack.py`) publishes content-named `<id>-<sha>.mwf` files + `manifest.json` to the `hd-animations` GitHub release on merge, so art updates reach phones without a build (remote value `hdAnimationsURL` can move the host). iOS CI prints an app size report. Engine `ExerciseIllustrations.liteFrameCount/liteFrames`, `ExerciseMotion.sampled` (tests `ExerciseImageTests`); app `HDAnimations`, `HDFrames`, `HDAnimationsOffer`, `HDAnimationsSettingsCard`, `HDReadyToast`.
- **Staging testers list** (#924): who may use Pro test mode (fake purchases) on staging now lives in the database (`subscription_testers`), so it can be managed without a new secret and Fly setup. New manual workflow **Staging testers** (`staging-testers.yml`, staging only): `accounts` lists staging accounts (id, masked email, nickname, sign-up date, tester), `add` takes an email or account id (the account may sign up later), `remove`, `list`. `admin.ts tester-add / tester-remove / testers / accounts` refuse where test mode is off, so production never gets a list. The optional Fly secret `SUBSCRIPTION_TEST_EMAILS` still adds to it. No API route can add a tester. Tests `subscriptions.test.ts`.
- **Save a workout seen on TikTok or Instagram** (#909, design A + B with C as fallback from `save-from-social/proposals.png`, Pro): a member who saw a cool workout in a reel had to rebuild it by hand. Now **From a video** (Add workout square, and Workout library → My workouts → *Save one from TikTok or Instagram*) opens *Saw a cool workout?*: **Paste** the post's link (and caption), type the caption, **tell Coach what you saw** (keyboard dictation), and/or add up to **4 screenshots**. **Read it**: Coach maps every move onto the app's exercises and the check screen shows the workout name (editable), a **source chip** (*@fitwithlea · TikTok*, Open ↗) and the moves: matched ones in their exercise colours with **✓ matched**, near or unknown ones dashed yellow **check** with the closest exercise (tap to pick, long press to remove). **Save workout** / **Do it today** (from a day: **Save only** / **Add to Thu**) saves it to My workouts with the post as its source; the editor shows *From @creator · TikTok ↗*. The video is never fetched and screenshots are never kept; only the clean link (share tracking dropped) and the creator are stored, as credit. API `POST /v1/me/workout-import` (`{link?, caption?, text?, photos[≤4], catalog}` → `{name, minutes, items[{said, id, sets, reps, unit, sure}], source?{platform, url, author?}, remainingToday}`; no caption → TikTok / YouTube public oEmbed for caption and creator; Instagram needs the caption or screenshots; scan model, `tracked` as `workout-import`, 20/day per member + global AI budget, table `workout_import_usage`; tests `workoutImport.test.ts`). Engine `WorkoutSource`, `ImportedMove`, `CustomWorkout.source`, `CustomWorkout(importing:moves:source:)` (tests `VideoImportTests`); app `VideoImportSheet`, `AppModel.readVideoWorkout`, `ProGate.videoImport`; demo screens `video-import`, `video-import-check`. **Share → MonkeyFit** (A): in TikTok, Instagram or any app, Share → MonkeyFit takes the post's link, caption and up to 4 screenshots; a gradient card shows what it took (*♪ TikTok · caption · 2 screenshots*) and says it waits in MonkeyFit; the next time the app opens, *Save from a video* opens with it filled in (nothing is sent from the share sheet; it is kept in the App Group for a day at most). New app extension `MonkeyWorkoutShare` (`$(APP_BUNDLE_ID).ShareExtension`, App Group), `VideoInbox`, `VideoShareLaunch`.
- **Get ready for… and Skill tree in My plan** (#898, design A from `my-plan-sport-skills/proposals.png`): sport prep and the Skill tree were only in Explore → Ready-made → Plans. My plan → *Add to your plan* now has two more tiles next to Programme, Lift Focus, Run and HIIT: **Get ready for…** (opens the same sheet as Explore: Ski, Padel, Climb, Run, Surf, Football season blocks and the 15′ sport add-on; shows the add-on sport and times a week when one is on) and **Skill tree** (Pull / Push / Legs / Balance levels, opens the tree). Explore keeps both. App `SeasonPlanTile`, `SkillTreePlanTile`.
- **Ask Coach while building a workout as you go** (#856, A + B from `freestyle-coach/proposals.png`): the freestyle *What first?* screen had no Coach, so a member couldn't say what they want to hit, name the machine in front of them or ask for ideas. Now a lime **Ask Coach** bar sits under the header: tap to type, 🎙 to talk, 📷 to **snap the machine**. Coach answers with up to 4 exercises as cards with **▶** (start it now, lined-up ones after it) and **+** (line it up; the header becomes Start), plus **Line up all**; for a photo it names the machine and gives a setup tip. Coach picks and Lately also show **▶ beside +** so starting at once or building a list first is one tap either way. Pro (Ask Coach). API: `POST /v1/me/coach-chat` takes `context.exercises` (names Coach may use) and an optional `photo` (JPEG ≤ 1.5 MB), answers `exercises` (only names from the list). Engine `Freestyle.coachNames` (tests `FreestyleTests`); app `FreestyleCoachBar`, `FreestylePlayMark`, `CoachFreestyle`, `CoachFocus.freestyle`; demo screen `ask-coach-freestyle`.
- **Talk to Coach mid-set** (#874, design A + C from `ai-epic/b1.png`, epic #865): the player mic only took commands or opened the Ask Coach sheet over the workout. Now the mic sits first in the **Up next** bar: **hold it** (or tap, it stops when you stop talking) and ask about the set on screen, like *"too heavy?"*, *"felt it in my lower back"* or *"how long should I rest?"*. Coach's answer card appears over the exercise picture (nothing moves, no scrolling): what Coach heard, one or two sentences, and up to **two one-tap fixes**: *Use 92.5 kg* (the sets still to do), *Swap to leg press* (in this workout) or *Rest 2:30* (this rest or the next). Coach **says the answer out loud** (C) unless Ask Coach's voice is *Never* or the pill muted it. Answers come from rules on the iPhone for the common questions (instant, offline: lighter on pain or a hard set, heavier with reps in reserve, rest from the plan and the last set's effort), then the on-device model, then the server Coach (Pro): new `POST /v1/me/coach-set` (top model, low effort, cached prompt, `tracked` as `set-talk` in the AI usage report, 30 a day per member, table `coach_set_usage`). Fixes are checked on both sides (weight 60–130 % on the stepper grid, swaps only from the app's list, rest 30 s – 10 min). Build-as-you-go keeps the mic in the top bar. Demo screen `player-talk`.
- **AI usage report** (#877, epic #865): there was no way to see what each AI feature costs. Every paid model call (Coach chat, week review, gym / food / plate / snap scans) now adds to a daily row per feature and model: calls, failures, input / output / cache tokens (no member ids, table `ai_call_log`). Actions → **AI usage** (staging or production, 1–90 days) runs `admin.ts ai-usage`: per day calls, failures, estimated US$ cost and the `AI_DAILY_LIMIT` use, then per feature tokens, cache hit and cost. Backend `aiUsage.ts`, `tracked()` in `coach/models.ts` (tests `aiUsage.test.ts`).
- **Weekly Coach review** (#867, design A + C from `ai-epic/a1.png`, epic #865): Train only looked ahead, nobody told members how their week went and what to change. Now on **Sunday and Monday** Pro members get **Coach's week review** on top of Train: a purple gradient card with a facts line (*4 of 5 done · 2 PRs · sleep ↓*), a **week score** 0–100 and two or three sentences from the week's workouts, new bests, last night's sleep, HRV against the usual and Mind check-ins. Under it up to **3 changes for the coming week**, each with **Apply**: move a workout to a free day (*Legs to Tue (you skip Thu)*), skip one, make one **lighter** (−1 set on every lift) or **+1 set** on a lift; **Apply all**; **Ask Coach about it** opens the chat on the review to tweak it in words; ✕ closes it for the week. Coach only names workouts and lifts the app sent, and the app checks every change again. Asked once a week (3 a day max on the server), kept on the device. Backend `POST /v1/me/coach-review` (`coach/review.ts`, `routes/coachReview.ts`, top model, cached prompt; tests `coachReview.test.ts`); engine `WeekReview` (tests `WeekReviewTests`); app `WeekReviewCard`, `AppModel+WeekReview`; demo screen `week-review`.
- **App Store listing as code** (#882): the store text (name *MonkeyWorkout*, subtitle *Gym plans, Coach and Watch*, Health & Fitness + Lifestyle, description, keywords, promo text, privacy / support / marketing URLs), the age rating answers and the App Review notes live in `ios/AppStore/listing.json`. New manual workflow **App Store listing** (`asc-listing.yml`, `.github/scripts/asc_listing.py`, dry run by default) pushes them to the editable iOS version in App Store Connect, with the demo account and contact phone from secrets `REVIEW_DEMO_EMAIL`, `REVIEW_DEMO_PASSWORD`, `REVIEW_CONTACT_PHONE`. Privacy labels and screenshots stay manual (no API); see `docs/app-store-review.md`.
- **What can I eat? Meal ideas from your pantry** (#869, epic #865, designs A + B from `ai-epic/d1.png`): Fuel showed what was left of the day but not what to cook with what's at home. Today's Fuel day now has a lime **What can I eat?** tile under Log what I ate (Pro, same gate as the food scan): Monkey reads What I have, the next meal and the protein and carbs still to eat, and answers **3 ideas** (e.g. *Eggs, feta & spinach wrap · 15′ · 41 g protein · 520 kcal*), each with **LOG** (logs the foods by weight on that meal), *Uses: …* and **Other ideas ↻**. Tapping an idea opens its recipe card: grams per pantry food, protein / carbs / fat / kcal, numbered steps and **Log as eaten · 640 kcal**. Ideas only use foods you have (by id); numbers are added up on the device from the app's food list, never from the model; diet and foods to avoid are respected. With an empty pantry the tile opens What I have. API `POST /v1/me/meal-ideas` (`{pantry, meal, left{protein, carbs}, diet?, avoid[]}` → `{ideas[≤3]{name, minutes, foods[{id, grams}], steps}, remainingToday}`, 20/day per member plus the global AI budget, table `meal_ideas_usage`; tests `mealIdeas.test.ts`). Engine `MealIdea` (tests `MealIdeasTests`); app `MealIdeasCard`, `MealIdeaSheet`; demo screens `fuel-ideas`, `fuel-idea`.
- **Ask your history** (#868, epic #865 C1, design A + C from `ai-epic/c1.png`): Progress opens with a lime **✦ Ask your history…** bar and chips (*Best bench press this year*, *Bench press vs June*, *Workouts this month*, *Weekly volume*). Ask by text or voice: "Am I squatting more than in June?" → a big-number card (**+9 kg**) with a mini chart of the months between and one sentence; tap it to open the exercise. The same questions in Ask Coach get the chart card under the answer. Apple's on-device model calls the history tools and words the answer on iPhones with Apple Intelligence; elsewhere the app reads the question itself. Nothing leaves the iPhone, free for everyone. Engine `HistoryAsk` (`best`, `compare`, `count`, `volume`, `periods`, `exercise`, `starters`; tests `HistoryAskTests`); app `ProgressAskBar`, `HistoryAnswerCard`, `HistoryAskService` with FoundationModels tools; demo screen `progress-ask`.
- **Read a nutrition label with the camera** (#872, epic #865, design C + A + B from `ai-epic/d2.png`): a food that isn't on the list had to be typed in value by value. Snap it now has a fourth chip, **🏷️ Label**, free for everyone: point the camera at the nutrition table and kcal, protein, carbs and fat fill live in big tiles while a lime frame guides you. Read on the iPhone (English, German, French, Italian labels; per 100 g column, kJ-only labels converted, saturates/sugars rows ignored), offline, nothing sent. **Use · per 100 g** opens a new food form with the read values in lime (fibre and salt too), a name and a usual serving (30/45/60 g, or 200/250/330 ml for drinks); **Save to What I have** adds it to your foods and pantry. Engine `NutritionLabel` (tests `NutritionLabelTests`), `Food.fibre/salt`; app `LabelReadSheet`, `LabelTextScanner`, `LabelFoodForm`; demo screen `fuel-label`.
- **Train around pain and injury** (#870, epic #865, design A2 from `ai-epic/a2.png`): a sore knee or shoulder meant skipping workouts or swapping moves one by one. Now **A** *Pain or injury?* (My plan, and under *Sore today* in auto-tune) opens **Where does it hurt?**: tap the spot on the body figure (neck, shoulders, elbows, wrists, lower back, hips, knees, ankles, left or right), pick **Mild**, **Sore when I load it** or **Sharp**, and **Coach changes · until better** lists every swap across this week's plan (*Back squat → Leg press*, *Box jump removed*) before **Protect my right knee · 6 swaps**. **B** say it in **Ask Coach** (*My knee hurts when I squat*): Coach asks one question (*Sharp, or more of an ache?*) then offers a **Knee-friendly plan · 6 swaps · 3 workouts · until better** card with **Apply**; red flags (swelling, numbness, gave way, pain at rest) get *see a doctor or physio first*. **C** while on, Train shows **Protecting right knee** (day, swaps, level; tap to change) with **Feels better** (ends it, Undo), swapped moves carry 🛡 and *pain-free range only*, and after 2 weeks *Still hurts? Get advice*. Swaps come only from the catalog through the engine (same role and muscles, your equipment), never from the model; every level swaps at least what the one below does; uids are kept so logs still match. Not medical advice. Engine `PainProtect`, `PainArea`, `PainLevel`, `PainProtect.guarded` (tests `PainGuardTests`); app `PainSheet`, `PainPlanCard`, `PainProtectBar`, `PainEntryRow`, `AppModel+Pain` (`SyncedProfile.pain`). API: coach chat answers may carry `protect {area, side, level, seeDoctor}`.
- **AI moderation of community shares** (#871, epic #865): only a word list stood between a member and the community, so threats, harassment, spam or a nude profile photo went live until three members reported them. Now a small model (Claude Haiku 5.5, `AI_MODEL_MODERATE`) checks what others will read or see before it goes live: post captions and titles, exercise names, shared plan and workout names, nicknames, abouts and profile photos. A flagged post, plan or shared workout is **held**: hidden from others, still visible to its owner, mailed to the moderation mailbox (photo attached); a flagged photo is kept aside and the old one stays; a flagged nickname or about is refused like the word filter. Only new text is checked (an auto re-share costs nothing); calls count in `AI_DAILY_LIMIT`, free to members. Model off, over budget, slow (8 s) or failing → the word filter alone, as before. In the app the owner sees an amber **IN REVIEW** tag (design A from `ai-moderation/proposals.png`) on the held post, plan or workout and on a held photo (*New photo in review · old one shows until then*), instead of the old *hidden while reports are reviewed*. Owners review with the new **Moderation holds** workflow (`admin.ts holds`, `hold-release`, `hold-reject`; log shows ids only). Backend `community/aiModeration.ts`, table `moderation_holds`; API: avatar uploads answer `held`, owners get `inReview` on posts, plans and workouts and `avatarInReview` on profiles; app `InReviewPill`, demo screen `community-review`; test `aiModeration.test.ts`.
- **Plateau explainer** (#875, epic #865 C2, design A + C from `ai-epic/c2.png`): a lift whose weekly best est. 1RM stayed within 1 % for 3+ weeks just looked flat on its chart. Now, on iPhones with Apple Intelligence (hidden otherwise, free, nothing sent), the **exercise page** shows *Stuck 3 weeks at 85 kg · est. 1RM flat since 7 Oct* with **WHY?**: the on-device Coach says the likely cause in two sentences and one fix, a set style to **Apply** (top set + back-offs when volume dropped, else cluster sets, % of 1RM or AMRAP; never the one in use). The **finish screen** gets a **Coach noticed** line (*Bench press stuck at 85 kg, 3rd week · likely: less volume*) with **FIX** opening the same. Causes are rules over the flat weeks vs the same span before: sets of the lift down 15 %+, under 75 % of planned workouts done, 2+ more workouts without lifting, last night under 6 h of sleep, else same sets every week. Lift done in the last 2 weeks, deloads ignored. Engine `Plateaus.stalled`, `StalledLift`, `PlateauCause` (tests `PlateauExplainTests`); app `OnDeviceCoach.plateau`, `PlateauCard`, `PlateauNudge`, `PlateauExplainer`, `AppModel.stalledLift`.
- **Personal Coach nudges** (#876, AI epic #865, designs A + C from `ai-epic/e2.png`): reminders said *Today: Push · 55 min. Tap to see your session.* for everyone. **A** the session reminder keeps its time but Coach writes it from your last time: *Last Monday you did 82.5 kg on barbell bench press. Go get 85 kg. 50 min, you usually start around 18:05.* (sets to finish when last time fell short), reworded by Apple's on-device model on iOS 26 when available, nothing sent. **C** **Comeback nudge**: only when you drift (2+ planned sessions missed since your last workout, or 10+ days away), once, at the next 12:00 or 17:00: *Busy week? A 20-min upper body session fits your lunch break. Want it?* (after a long break it starts the **Comeback quest**, +100 XP) with **Start 20′ now** (a 20′ session of the missed focus added to today, straight into the player) or **Tonight 19:00** (added, reminded at 19:00). Settings → Reminders & alerts: new **Comeback nudge** toggle. Engine `CoachNudges` (tests `CoachNudgesTests`), `NotificationPrefs.comebackNudge`, `PlannedSession.focus`; app `OnDeviceCoach.nudge`, `ComebackPick`, `AppModel.acceptComeback`; backend prefs `comebackNudge`.
- **Set up the plan by chatting with Coach** (#873, design B + C from `ai-epic/a3.png`, epic #865): a new member had to work through every plan form step alone, with words like *hypertrophy* or *undulating* and nobody to ask. Now the onboarding plan step opens with **Tell Coach your goal**: say it once, typed or spoken (*"lose weight and build muscle, 4 days, 1 hour max, bad knee"*), and Coach fills in four tiles, **Goal**, **Days**, **Length** and **Protect**. Tap a tile to fix it, then **Build my plan** builds it for the gym and machines picked in the steps before; **Protect** (where it hurts, side, how bad) turns on **Train around pain** (#870) for that spot, so the first plan already swaps the moves that load it. The form stays, and each of its main steps (goal, gym days, week to week, training method) gets a **🐵 Not sure? Ask Coach** line: Coach explains the options from the answers so far and offers **Use Coach's pick** in one tap. Free for every member (part of onboarding), 15 calls a day. Backend `POST /v1/me/coach-plan` and `/v1/me/coach-plan/help` (top model, low effort, cached prompts, tracked in the AI usage report as `plan-setup` / `plan-help`), table `coach_plan_usage`. Demo screens `coach-plan`, `coach-help`.

Progress keeps its name and the default board now opens on **Today**: a **Body** square (the auto-tune ready score, opens auto-tune) next to a **Mind** square (Mind score, opens the Mind page) with Coach's one line (*Both high: a good day to push*, *Mind's strained: 3 min of breathing before you train helps*, …). A new **Mind** widget sits with the part tiles (small: score and level; wide: the score card with 7-day bars) and opens the Mind page, which gains a **4-week mood calendar** (a square a day in the check-in's colour). Customised boards add both from the widget gallery. How it works → Mind score explains the Body + Mind call. Now it asks **What will you do?**: a *Climbing stays on Wed* line on sport days (the sport is kept), the **Coach pick** (generated for the muscles least trained this week, at your plan's session length; long press for length and focus), this week's workouts to **move here** (next to what the day has, Undo), then squares **My workouts**, **Library**, **Generate**, **Build my own**, **HIIT** (format and 10–30 min) and **Log a climb**. **Generate** takes any length from **20 min to 2 h**, a focus (Upper, Lower, Full-body power, Push, Pull, Legs, Conditioning) or your own muscles, and shows the exercises (sets × reps) and XP before **Add to Wed 29 · 60′**. - **Climbing live: Track live tracks your climb** (#829, designs A + B from `climb-track-live/proposals.png`): Track live on a climbing day only opened the log form. Now it opens **Climbing live** on the iPhone, with or without a Watch: time on the wall, rest since the last go, Boulder / Lead / Top rope / Outdoor, **Font or V** grade chips, **Flash · Sent · Fell** one tap per problem, Undo, and today's grade pyramid. **Finish** logs the climb with its minutes, top grade, effort and every go (the day's climb row reads *6 sends · 2 flashes · 3 falls*); closing keeps it running (*Climbing live* on the card); the place (#832) starts on your last climbing gym. With a paired Watch, the Watch tracks a **Climbing** workout (heart rate, kcal) on a goes page: the grade on the Crown, **Sent · Fell**, **Flash · Undo**, rest timer; every go shows on both, finishing on either ends both, and the Health workout is saved once. Climbing is also in the Watch's Track a sport. Engine `ClimbTally` (tests `ClimbLiveTests`); app `ClimbLive`, `ClimbLiveView`; Watch `ClimbWatchPage`, `TrackedSport.climbing`; `WClimbLive` both ways. - **Where you climbed or did your sport** (#832, mix of A + B from `sport-place/proposals.png`): Log climb and Log sport had no place, so a session at the climbing gym, a crag or a ski resort looked the same. The log card now has **Where** chips under the times: places used for that sport (newest first), your saved gyms (not for outdoor-only sports like snowboard, ski, MTB, hike), then places from other sports; the picked one is white, tap again for no place. A new log starts where the sport was done last time. **+** opens a **Where** sheet: Apple Maps search (gyms, crags, resorts, trails), *Use "…"* for a name Maps doesn't know, and tiles for **You're here** (only when location is already allowed), places used before (*Used 4×*) and your gyms. The Save button reads *Save Boulder · Block Factory · 1h30*. The place shows in History rows and as a map tile on a sport session page (a rated Health session can get a place there). Synced: climbs and sport sessions take `place` `{id, name, address?, latitude?, longitude?, gymId?}`. Engine `SessionPlace`, `SessionPlaces.chips/last`, `Sport.outdoorOnly` (tests `SessionPlaceTests`); app `PlaceChips`, `SessionPlaceSheet`, `SessionPlaceTile`, `AppModel.placeUses/lastPlace`.
- **Form check with the camera** (#833, designs A + B + C from `form-check/proposals.png`): members could not get feedback on how they move. Now the phone, propped 2–3 m away, watches a set: Apple's body pose runs **on the iPhone** and keeps only 14 joints (no picture is recorded or sent). **Live mirror** (A): skeleton over the camera with green / amber / red joints, **FORM** score and **REPS** tiles, one big cue (*Go deeper*, *Chest up*, *Push knees out*, *Hips up*, *Finish tall*, *Slow down*) said by the Coach voice, a coloured square per rep. **Ghost** (B): the exercise's 3D position guide drawn as a lime ghost fitted over the member (scaled to their body, on their feet, facing their way), joint angle chips vs the guide and **MATCH %** per rep. **Set report** (C) on Done: score, best rep, *faded from rep N*, rep tiles, a replay of the worst rep's joints with its numbers, two **Fix next set** tiles (*Knees caved reps 6–7 −18 %*, *Torso lean grew +9°*), **Ask Coach** (sends what the camera saw), **Film again**; kept in the exercise's history (last 60, on this device, wiped on sign-out) and shown on the exercise page. Reps are counted on the joint that moves most in the guide; faults are only those the guide's side can show (side: range, finish, trunk lean, hip sag; front: knees caving, one side ahead), each taking points once off a rep's 100. Ways in: **Check my form** on the exercise page (Explore), **Watch my form** in Coach (tile, or say *check my form*; picks from today's workout or any exercise, or the exercise Coach was opened on) and a camera chip on the player's exercise card, whose report can **Use N reps** for the current set. Front or back camera, cue voice on/off. Exercises with reps, a 3D guide and a joint moving ≥ 25°. **How it works → Form check** lists every rule and threshold. Engine `FormCheck` (`FormModel`, `FormAnalyzer`, `FormReport`, `FormDemo`; tests `FormCheckTests`); app `FormCheckViews` (`FormCamera`, `FormCheckView`, `FormReportView`, `FormReportStore`, `FormCheckLink`, `FormHistoryCard`, `FormCheckCoachTile`, `FormCheckPicker`), `ExerciseHeroCard.onFilm`; camera permission text updated; demo screens `form-live`, `form-ghost`, `form-report`, `form-coach`.
- **Mind: mood check-in, Calm sessions and a Mind score** (#834, designs A + B + C from `mental-health/proposals.png`): the app trained the body only. Now **A** Train gets a **Mind** card under Daily: *How do you feel?* with five mood squares, what it is about (Sleep, Work, Family, Friends, Training, Health, Money, Weather) and **Save check-in**, saved on the phone and to Apple Health as a daily **State of Mind**; once checked in it shows the **Mind score** tile and **Coach's breathing pick** (4-7-8 in the evening, physio sigh on a rough day, box when HRV is low, wake-up on a so-so morning). ✕ hides it for today, the third ✕ stops it. **B** Explore → Ready-made → **Calm**: mindful minutes this week, *Breathe* (Box 4-4-4-4, 4-7-8, Physio sigh, Wake-up) and *Relax* (Body scan, Pre-lift focus) tiles opening a full-screen player: a square that fills on the breath in and empties on the breath out, phase countdown, spoken cues (system Coach voice, mute button), a tap per phase, lengths chips; sessions end on a breath out and save **mindful minutes** to Health (✕ after a minute saves what was done with Undo). **C** **Mind score** 0–100 from mood (35), HRV against the 30-day baseline (25), sleep (25) and workout days in the last 3 (15), +1 a calm minute (max +5), with 7-day bars, HRV / sleep / 7-day mood tiles, a Coach pattern after 5 check-ins (mood the day after training, what low days are about, short nights), the parts behind today's score and a new **How it works** topic. Mood and sessions stay on the device (`mind.json`, wiped on sign-out). Demo screens `mind-checkin`, `mind-train`, `explore-calm`, `breathing`, `mind`.
- **Mind on Apple Watch** (#834, designs A + B + C from `mental-health/watch/proposals.png`): **A** Home row **Check in** / **Mind** opens the Calm list (every session on its gradient); the breathing player taps the wrist on each phase (stronger on the breath in, so eyes can stay closed), shows the filling square, live heart rate and time left; tap or double tap pauses. A minute or more is saved as mindful minutes through the iPhone. **B** mood check-in on the wrist (five squares + Later) syncs to the iPhone and Health, then the **Mind card** (score, level, 7 days) with Coach's pick. **C** two new Smart Stack cards: **Morning check-in** (7–10, until done) and **Wind down** (21–23, 4-7-8), opening the Mind screen; the done screen shows the heart-rate drop (e.g. 72 → 61) and the Mind bonus. - **Add another supplement, obvious** (#742, mix of A + C from `add-supplement/proposals.png`): in the My stack sheet the only way to add a supplement was a sliders icon. Now under the stack: a lime **Add a supplement** tile (opens the full list) and four quick squares, **From a brand**, **Scan label**, **Creatine**, **My own**, each opening the editor in one tap (Scan label starts on the camera); the sliders icon became a pencil (edit). App `SupplementAddPanel`, `SupplementEditor(scanFirst:)`; demo screen `fuel-stack`.
- **Invite a friend, story posters, socials on your profile** (#745, invite design A + C, socials S2 from `referral-invites/proposals.png`): Social's **Invite** tile opened Gym buddies. Now it opens its own **Invite** page (and shows every day, not only when nobody trained): your **code** (tap to copy the invite link), **Messages · WhatsApp · Insta story · More** share buttons, **Joined · Pro · XP earned** tiles, **Got a friend's code?** (first 14 days, +200 XP welcome) and the **reward ladder**: 1 friend joins → Recruiter +200 XP; 2 → Iron app icon at any level; a friend's first paid month → 1 month Pro each; 5 → +1,000 XP and Titan icon; 10 → Pro for a year. Pro rewards go out as App Store offer codes once Pro launches (never server-side Pro); free trials don't count. Gym buddies sits under the ladder. **Story posters**: 9:16 images (1080 × 1920) with your invite code for Instagram Stories and the share sheet, from the **workout summary**, a **PR result** and the **week chest**; the invite poster carries a QR code. **Your socials**: Instagram, TikTok, Strava and YouTube handles in Your profile, shown as square tiles on your member page to everyone, members who follow you (default) or nobody. Backend `GET /v1/referrals/me`, `POST /v1/referrals/redeem`, public invite page `GET /i/:code` (`INVITE_APP_URL`, default the TestFlight public link), `PUT /v1/community/me/socials`; tables `referral_codes`, `referrals`, columns `community_profiles.socials`, `socials_visibility`; referrals in the data export. App `InviteView`, `ReferralStore`, `InviteReward`, `StoryPoster`, `StoryShareButton`, `SocialTiles`, `SocialsEditor`; demo screen `social-invite`.
- **Week challenge: 60-card deck, levels, member weeks** (#748, design C from `week-challenges/proposals.png`): the week challenge was 8 AMRAP 12′ WODs built into the app, back every 8 weeks; changing one needed an app release and members could not suggest any. Now the **server deals the WOD** from a **60-card deck** in four formats (AMRAP, EMOM, For time, Tabata; all kit-free), shuffled per year so **none repeats inside a year**. Pick a level, **Easy** (½ reps), **Rx** or **Beast** (+1 round or a vest), which ranks first on the board. The room shows a **next week teaser** (format, time, how many moves) and your **past 4 weeks** with your rank. Scores fit the format: rounds + reps, Tabata total reps, For time your time (or reps at the cap). **Member weeks**: the last week of each month runs the members' pick. **Suggest a challenge** (name, format, time, 2–5 moves from the kit-free list with reps); the **Coach check** refuses moves off the list and unsafe reps or times, then the owner approves (Actions → **Week challenge review**: list, approve, reject), and members **vote** on up to 3 finalists until the week starts (the winner shows *by* its author). Your suggestions show their status and can be withdrawn until they run. API `GET /v1/community/challenge` adds `wod`, `next`, `history`, `vote`; score takes `seconds`, `tier`; new `GET`/`POST /v1/community/challenge/suggestions`, `DELETE …/:id`, `PUT /v1/community/challenge/vote`; tables `community_challenge_suggestions`, `community_challenge_votes`, `community_challenge_weeks`, in the data export and removed when leaving Community. Backend `community/challenges.ts`, `admin.ts challenges | challenge-approve | challenge-reject`; app `ChallengeViews` (`ChallengeRoomView`, `SuggestChallengeSheet`), `WeeklyChallenge.timer` (the app's 8 stay as the offline fallback); demo screens `challenge`, `challenge-suggest`.
- **Social, simpler** (#738, design B from `social-simplify/b.png`): Social stacked a gym card, three stat tiles (often 0), three share tiles, Feed · Plans · Fuel · Mine and Members · Following before the first post (one post per screen, four ways to post). Now: a **story row** of square tiles (**Live** buddies or **Invites**, the **week challenge**, each member who trained today with a lime ring; tap a member for a full-screen story, swipe through today's), chips **For you · Following · People**, then **one feed**: wins, tips, recipes and member plans mixed (a plan after every third post). The **+** opens one **Share** sheet: Win, Plan, Meal, Tip. Posts are lighter: one **💪** cheer tile with the total (hold for all six), no rank chip, and **one action**: **Beat it** on a member's personal best (opens the PR attempt), **Save to Fuel** on a member's recipe. Your own posts live in Me → Your profile. Removed: gym today card, stat tiles, share row, Plans / Fuel / Mine tabs. App `SocialStories` (`SocialStoriesRow`, `StoryViewer`, `SocialChips`, `ShareHubSheet`, `PostAction`); demo screens `community-post`, `community-story` (replace `community-plans`, `community-fuel`, `community-mine`).
- **Fuel redesign: Fuel Tank + Magic Snap** (#733, mix of A + C from `fuel-redesign/a.png`, `c.png`): Fuel had 5 ways to log, 3 cameras in 2 places (Snap my plate hid inside Just ate), three looks with different cards, a row of 6 small tool tiles, and the day itself below the fold. Now Fuel has one home: the **tank** (what you ate today in protein, carbs, water and caffeine, % fuelled; tap water for a glass), one big **LOG WHAT I ATE** button, the next meal, and **Kitchen** squares with live numbers (What I have, Meal plan, Groceries with basket cost, My stack) plus Recipes, Fuel week, Learn and Settings chips. The Log sheet (was Just ate) opens with **Magic Snap**: one photo, Monkey sorts it, so a plate or drink is logged with grams, and a fridge, shopping or a receipt goes to What I have. Plate / Fridge / Barcode chips go straight to one kind. Backend `POST /v1/me/food-scan` takes `mode: "auto"` and answers with `kind` meal | stock (same Pro gate and daily count). The settings tile left the header; the Day / Timeline / Portions picker is gone ("Hide numbers" keeps the hand portions view of the numbers). Demo screens `fuel-log`, `fuel-snap` replace `fuel-timeline`, `fuel-portions`.
- **Me sheet, clearer** (#736, design A from `me-sheet/proposals.png`): the Me sheet had a hero and a *Your profile* row opening the same page, the level twice (hero and a Levels row) and half the sheet empty. Now one hero: photo, name and **Edit ›** open Your profile, the level bar opens Levels; under it six square **Settings** tiles (You, Training, Watches, Look & apps, Account, Help, each with what it holds) open Settings at that chapter, **All ›** opens it at the top. App `MeSheet`, `SettingsChapter.summary`, `AccountView(start:)`; UI test `testMeSheetChapterOpensSettings`.
- **More daily routines, your own routines, and an add card on Train** (#732, mix A + C from `daily-routines/v2/train.png`, builder from `builder.png`): Daily had five routines, members could not make their own, and the slim *Add a daily routine* prompt sat under Next up and went away for good once closed. Now **6 more built-ins** from existing moves: **Hip opener**, **Shoulder care**, **Roll out** (foam roller, ball), **Runner's legs**, **Wrists & fingers** and **Splits & straddle** (11 in all, same length chips and scaling); Explore → Ready-made → Daily gets **Recovery** and **Mine** filters. **Build my own**: name, colour, icon and moves (stretches and mobility drills from the catalog, reps or seconds and rounds per move); saved as a custom workout with a routine look (synced, plays and logs as one mobility block, no added cool-down), listed under **Mine** with Edit / Duplicate / Delete (Undo), pinnable to Train like any routine. **Train**: until a routine is picked, an **Add daily routines** card under the day card offers three picks for the member (Stretch, Hangs or Mobility, Hips) with one-tap +, **See all 11** and **Build my own**; ✕ closes it (Undo). After that, on days with nothing on Daily, **Coach pick** suggests one routine for the day (pull or push day → Shoulder care, leg day → Hip opener, cardio → Runner's legs, rest → Roll out, climbing day → Wrists & fingers) with lengths, **Make it daily**, **Just today**, Other routines and Build my own; ✕ hides it for the day, the third ✕ stops it. Engine `Routine` (new cases, `starters`, `coachPick`, `Kind.recovery`), `CustomWorkout.routine`, `RoutineLook` (tests `RoutineTests`, `CustomWorkoutTests`); app `DailyRoutine`, `DailyStarterCard`, `CoachRoutineCard`, `MyRoutineEditor`, `RoutinePrefs` (keys `dailyRoutineOffer`, `dailyRoutineCoach`); API stores the look as part of the custom workout record (no server change); demo screens `daily-offer`, `routine-builder`.
- **Snap my plate** (#723, mix of C + A + B from `scan-plate/proposals.png`): a photo of a cooked meal could not be logged; Snap the fridge only listed foods per 100 g for What I have, and Just ate's plate was built by hand. Just ate now opens with **Snap my plate** (Pro, same daily scans as the fridge): Monkey names the dish (**Looks like** *Chicken teriyaki bowl*), lists each food with its grams (unsure ones dashed and unticked), **S / M / L / XL** per food against its guess, **How much did you eat?** ¼ ½ ¾ All, live protein, carbs, fat, kcal and caffeine, then **Log <meal>**; **Save as my dish** keeps it as a usual for one tap next time. Foods not on the list are kept on the device; small amounts log by weight (≈ 15 g). API `/v1/me/food-scan` `mode: "plate"` adds `dish` and per food `grams`, `caffeine`. Engine `PlateScan`, `FoodServing(foodId:grams:of:)` (tests `PlateScanTests`); app `PlateScanSheet`; demo screen `fuel-snap-plate`.
- **Workout on the TV** (#724, design mix B + A from `tv-cast/option-a.png`, `option-b.png`): a workout could only reach a TV as the mirrored portrait phone with black bars. Now **Screen Mirroring** to an Apple TV or AirPlay TV, or an HDMI adapter, shows a **full-screen TV layout**: the exercise movement large on the block's gradient, name and position top left, a **big rest / work timer**, **SET · KG · REPS** gradient tiles, heart rate, workout time and %, and one **Up next** bar with a progress line; everything in fixed places. The iPhone stays the remote (log sets, controls as usual). With no workout open the TV shows a *Start a workout on your iPhone* card. App `TVPlayer` (`TVCast`, `TVLive`, `TVSceneDelegate`, `TVPlayerView`), `AppDelegate` external display scene; demo screen `tv-player`. **Cast button** (Netflix-style, top bar of the player): lists **Chromecasts / Google TVs** on the Wi-Fi and the **AirPlay** picker; the TV's own player plays a live video of the same layout (no page or app on the TV; a few seconds behind), the button turns lime while a TV shows it. The phone draws the layout in a window behind the app, encodes H.264 and serves live HLS (fMP4, 1 s segments) on the Wi-Fi; Chromecast through Google's Default Media Receiver over Cast v2 (no Google SDK). App `TVStream` (`TVStreamer`, `TVStreamServer`), `Chromecast` (`CastDevices`, `CastSession`, `CastMessage`, `AirPlayStream`), `CastSheet`; Info.plist local network + `_googlecast._tcp`; demo screens `tv-player`, `cast-sheet`.
- **Log plain foods like coffee and cereals** (#710, mix of A + B + C from `log-foods/proposals.png`): a member logging breakfast coffee and cereals got nothing counted, because Just ate only searched recipes and shakes, "cereals" matched no food, foods had no caffeine and the caffeine meter only counted supplements. Now Just ate's search finds **foods** too (plurals match; cornflakes, muesli and granola answer to *cereal*), **quick food chips** per meal (recent foods first) put foods on a **plate** with ½-step amounts, live protein, carbs, kcal and caffeine, a *With milk?* nudge for cereal, and **Log breakfast** in one tap. Small foods add up in grams across the day into hand portions; the Portions page says what foods added. **Save as a usual** keeps the plate as a tile (*My breakfast*: one tap logs it; hold to delete). Coffee, tea, iced tea, cola and energy drinks carry caffeine: logged mugs fill the **Caffeine today** meter, now shown even without a supplement stack. Engine `FoodServing`, `FoodUsual`, `FuelDayLog.log/setFoods/foodMacros/foodCaffeineMg`, `Food.caffeine`, `FoodBook.quick/wantsMilk` (tests `FoodLogTests`); app `FoodPlateViews`; demo screen `fuel-plate`.
- **Today's place on Train, and where routines happen** (#700, design A + C's "Where" from `day-places/proposals.png`): a day's gym could only be set as a weekly pattern in Training profile, and daily routines had no place. With more than one saved gym, the workout day card now has a **📍 Puls 5 ▾** chip: **Just this Friday** (one-off, rebuilt for that gym's kit, same session type) or **Every Friday** (the weekly place). Daily routines get **Where** (Home, Gym, Anywhere) from the tile's long press or the Explore → Routines card; the tile says *At home · …* or *At Puls 5 · …* (today's gym). Daily hangs default to home. Engine `Profile.placing` (tests in `WeekPlacesTests`); app `PlaceMenuChip`, `AppModel+Places` (`setPlaceToday`, `setPlaceEveryWeek`), `SyncedProfile.placeOnDay`, `RoutinePlace`.
- **Daily routines are your choice** (#696, design B from `daily-routines-optin/proposals.png`): Train always showed two Daily routine tiles. Now the Daily row is empty until the member picks: a slim **Add a daily routine** prompt opens a picker (square routine tiles, up to two, with lengths); ✕ hides the prompt (Explore → Routines can still pin one). Members who already did a routine keep those tiles. Long press on a tile adds **Choose routines…**. App `DailyRoutineSuggestion`, `DailyRoutinePicker`, `RoutinePrefs.suggests/choosing`, `AppModel.usedRoutines`; demo screen `daily-pick`.
- **HIIT sessions** (#693, designs A + B + C from `hiit/proposals.png`): the app had a HIIT circuit template (supersets, no timer) and stand-alone WOD timers with text moves, nothing in the plan or History. **A · HIIT days in your plan**: Training profile → **HIIT** (also Explore → HIIT): 0–3 HIIT days a week, **Finisher on lifting days** (a 4′ Tabata in place of the cardio block), **Low impact** (no jumps). Coach puts them on free days away from leg days and from each other, rotates the formats every week (Engine burner: Tabata + EMOM + AMRAP; Thirty-thirty; Pyramid power; Race the clock), keeps the deload week easy and eases them on low readiness. **B · Explore → HIIT**: Tabata, EMOM, AMRAP, For time, Pyramid and 30/30 tiles open a builder (10–30′, No kit / Dumbbells / Kettlebell / My gym, impact, moves from the library with Reshuffle) → **Do today**, **Add to day** or **Save**. **C · Interval player**: the screen takes the phase colour (format gradient while working, cyan resting), huge countdown, move animation, round segments, heart rate, one Up next bar with Skip, Pause / +1 round / Done, 3-2-1 beeps and haptics; the Watch shows the same step with heart rate and double tap pauses. Logged to History (one set per interval) and Apple Health as HIIT; X with work done saves with Undo. Engine `Hiit`, `HiitWorkout`, `HiitPlan`, `HiitSpec`, `Session.hiit`, `Profile.hiit` (tests `HiitTests`); app `HiitViews`, `HiitPlayerView`; Watch `HiitMirror`, `HiitRemoteView`; demo screens `explore-hiit`, `hiit-builder`, `hiit-player`, `train-hiit`.
- **Same day actions on every day, and Rest day on sport days** (#671, design A from `day-card-consistency/proposals.png`): next week's workout card had Plan sport only (no Move, no long press) while this week's had Plan sport + Move, and a sport day could not become a rest day. Every coming day card, any week, now ends with three tiles in fixed spots, and the strip day's long press offers the same moves: **workout day** Move · Plan sport · Skip; **sport day** Workout · Change · **Rest day**; **rest day** Workout · Plan sport · Recover. Later weeks can move a workout to another day of that week (or move one onto a free day: *move one here*) and skip it ahead. **Rest day** asks just this day, every <weekday> or the whole trip, then the day is a normal rest day (Recover, Add workout, Plan sport) with Undo; also in the sport block's *Change ›* menu. Engine `Profile.sportOff`, `PlanCalendar.weeks(laterFixed:)`; tests `SportOffTests`.
- **Add your own gym** (#680, designs A + B from `add-own-gym/proposals.png`): onboarding, Your gym and trips only offered gyms Apple Maps knows; "Home or hotel" had no address or pin, and a search ended on "No gyms found". Now **Search by name** and **Find a gym** end with **Add "<name>"** (orange row, also on empty results) opening **Add your gym**: name (required, prefilled from the search), address and a pin (optional; tap the map, type an address, or Use my location; a pin fills the address). **Long-press the Find a gym map** to drop a pin for a gym that isn't there. Then the usual machine setup. Own gyms keep a private `custom:` id (not shared to gym memory). `SavedGym.customRef(name:address:latitude:longitude:)`; app `OwnGymSheet`, `AddOwnGymRow`; demo screen `own-gym`.
- **Where I train, day by day** (#684, design A from `week-places/proposals.png`): the whole plan used one gym, gym days only said *when*. With more than one saved gym, Training profile → Week now shows **Where I train**: a row per weekday with a chip for each saved gym (home, hotel, your gym…) and **Climb**, so Monday can be home, Tuesday climbing and the gym. Each day's workout is built for that gym's equipment and zones; a session moved to another day takes that day's gym. The Train day card shows **Lift · Home**. Engine `DayKit`, `Profile.dayPlaces/dayKits`, `setPlace`, `Generator.fitPlaces`, `Session.place`; app `WeekPlacesEditor`, `SyncedProfile.placedProfile`. Members without places get the same plans as before.
- **Hybrid: lifting and cardio work together** (#685, design A + B's Coach note): runs were placed around lifting, but the workouts ignored them. Now on a run day you **lift first, then run**, and the run **replaces the workout's cardio block**; a legs day before (or on) a long run or intervals keeps **one set less on leg work**; Hybrid sessions **grow through the block** (Zone 2 +5′, intervals +2 reps a week, easier in a deload). The day card shows the day's parts numbered in order (**1 Lift · 2 Zone 2 ride 40′ · 3 Climb**) and a ⚡ note with what changed. Engine `RunPlanner.blend`, `RunPlanner.step`, `Session.synergy`; app `DayComboRow`; tests `WeekPlacesTests`; demo screen `train-hybrid` (hotel gym on Monday and Thursday).
- **Daily routines: stretching by length and daily hangboard** (#689, designs A + B from `daily-routines/proposals.png`): a tester missed daily routines (a stretching routine in different lengths, daily hangboarding). Train now shows a **Daily** row under Next up on every day: two square tiles (Stretch + Hangboard for climbers, else Stretch + Mobility) with length chips, a 🔥 streak and a check once done today; long press swaps the routine or hides the row. **Explore → Ready-made → Routines**: Full-body stretch (5, 10, 15, 20, 30′), Daily hangs (5–20′), Morning mobility, Desk reset and Before bed, each with every length and **Show on Train**. A routine fits the length picked (more moves, then longer holds or a second round). **Daily hangs** are low load: 10 s on / 50 s off on a 20 mm edge, half crimp and open hand in turn, 40–60 % bodyweight (feet on a chair or pulley), effort 3–4/10, never to failure; on a climbing day the tile says *Climb day · keep it easy*. Template items can now carry an intensity line. Engine `Routine` (tests `RoutineTests`); app `RoutineViews`, `AppModel+Routines`; demo screen `explore-routines`.
- **Community tab: your gym today, small tiles, shared plans, fuel and tips** (#666, designs C + B from `community-redesign/proposals.png`): four full-width cards (Your gym, Find members, Gym buddies, Week challenge) filled the first screen. Now a **gym today** card on top (who trained at your gym, who's live, the week challenge with a bar for the week and the top 3; opens the challenge room), three small square tiles (gym items, following, buddies live or invites), and quick buttons **Share plan · Share meal · Post a tip**. Under them, tabs **Feed** (Members / Following chips, Trained today), **Plans**, **Fuel** and **Mine**. **Plans**: programmes members shared, filtered by level, each with its phases and signature workouts; **Copy to my programmes** makes it yours and the app builds every week for your gym, days and time; share one of My programmes with a level and a line about it, stop sharing any time; report or block from the plan. **Fuel**: members' recipes and fuel tips. **Tips**: train or fuel advice up to 280 characters, as a note card (`tip:train|fuel`). API `/v1/community/programs` (list, get, put, delete, copy, report; same blocks, filter and auto-hide as posts), tables `community_programs`, `community_program_copies`, in the data export, removed when leaving Community. Demo screens `community-plans`, `community-fuel`, `community-mine`, `community-plan`, `community-share-plan`, `community-tip`.
- **New member onboarding** (#664, mix of designs A + B + C from `new-member-onboarding/mix.png`): a new member only got one long plan form, with the gym silently set to Puls 5, machines and zones hidden behind the gym card, no programme choice and no tour. Now a **guided flow**: **Gym** (near me, by name, home or hotel) → **Machines** (tick what the gym has) → **Map** (zones and floor plan) → **Plan** (build my plan, now without the gym section, or start a programme) → **Tour**, with a progress bar and **Skip** on every step. The **app tour**: 🐵 bubbles over Train, Explore, Progress, Fuel, Community and Ask Coach, each tab opening behind its bubble; the Pro offer waits for its end. Steps skipped wait on a **Finish set-up** card on Train (one tile each, ✕ hides it). **Settings → You → Onboarding**: run it all again (with Keep my plan) or one step, the tour, the card on or off. Engine `OnboardingProgress`, `OnboardingStep` (tests `OnboardingTests`); app `OnboardingFlowView`, `SetupCard`, `OnboardingHubView`, `AppTourOverlay`, `AppModel+Onboarding`; `SyncedProfile.onboarding`. Members from before keep no card.
- **Current gym in Explore** (#662, design A, tile 1 from `explore-current-gym/pinned.png`): **My gym** is now pinned on the left of Explore's machine chips (muscle pages too) as an orange sunset tile with the gym's name and machine count; the other chips slide under it. Tap filters to what your gym has (✓, white outline, "Only mine"); **▾** opens the gym sheet: **Gym map** (floor plan, draw zones and where machines sit), **Here** (machines in your gym) and **Add missing** (machines with exercises your gym doesn't list yet), both opening the equipment page with zones; edits re-plan the week. `GymChipTile`, `ExploreGymSheet`, `AppModel.setGymEquipment`; demo screens `explore-gym-chip`, `explore-gym`.
- **Around sport days, for every sport** (#658, page designs B + A and Train design B from `around-sport/proposals.png`, `train-proposals.png`): the app planned gym days around climbing with big choices (Keep it light, Strong push or legs, Rest, No adjustment) but other sports only had small chips. Settings → **Around sport days** sets one choice for **all sports** (day before, day after, on sport days); **Your sports** opens a sport's own page with a sport strip and words from its body load (run: *No heavy legs or jumps. Upper body preferred.*, *Strong upper body*; climbing keeps its grip and pulling words and the Wall session toggle) to plan it differently, *Use the default* to go back. Diving keeps rest the day after. Also on the Wall session and Sport prep pages; free for every sport (Pro keeps the Wall session). Engine `SportAround` (`Sport.activity`, `aroundLabel`, `aroundBlurb`, `Profile.sportDefaults`, `Profile.sport(onWeekday:key:)`), profile `sportBefore/sportAfter/sportSameDay`; app `AroundSportView`; tests `SportAroundTests`; demo screens `around-sport`, `train-sport-day`.
- **Sport block on Train** (#658): a sport day showed Log climb, Sport day edit and Log sport side by side. The day card now shows the day's sport as one gradient block in its colours: **Change ›** (change sport, edit the sport day, Gym around it), **Track live · Log · Prep** today (Log on earlier days, the climb log on climbing days, Prep = Wall session or the sport's prep), with Add workout and Freestyle below. The separate prep and Wall session bars hide while the block shows.
- **Heart-rate zones** (#609, designs A + B + C from `apple-tech-radar/t7.png`): sport, run and Hyrox screens on the Watch show five square **zone tiles**, the current zone wide and lit, with a buzz on zone change. Pick a **target zone** before a sport (Off, Z2, Z3, Z4) or get a planned run's: the **target lock** washes the screen orange or blue when out of it with "Slow down ↓ 10 bpm" / "In Zone 2 · 18:40", and a stronger tap when leaving or coming back. Zones are Apple Health's five zones when set (iOS 27 `preferredWorkoutZoneConfiguration`), else from age and resting heart rate (heart-rate reserve 60/70/80/90 %). watchOS 27: our zones go on the workout when Health has none and Apple's live zone changes drive the tiles (`didUpdateWorkoutZone`). Progress → Training: **Zone 2 this week** card, minutes per zone over every workout from every app, 150′ goal ring (Apple's zone times on iOS 27, else heart-rate samples). Engine `HeartZones`, `ZoneTime`; Watch `ZoneTracker`, `ZoneTiles`, `AppleZones`; demo screen `zone-week`.
- **Run & cardio programmes** (#618, designs A + B + C from `x1.png`): Training profile → **Run & cardio**: a **race** (5K, 10K, half; race day, goal time, name, 2–4 runs a week) or **Hybrid** (fitter + stronger: two Zone 2 sessions, bike/row/run, plus 8 × 1′ intervals). Runs slot around lifting: long run Saturday, never on or the day after legs, hard runs not after legs, gym-free days first; Base → Build → Peak → Taper → race week, paces from the goal time (easy, long, tempo, intervals 400/800/1000 m). Train shows the **goal race card** under Next up: countdown, M–S strip (lift / run / rest, ✓ when done), today's run. **Run player** on the Watch: steps as big blocks (REP 3 OF 6 · 400 M), pace now vs target, metres or time left, steps move on by themselves with a buzz and a spoken cue, each step a Health segment. A run counts as done when Health has a run, ride or row of half its time that day. Engine `RunGoal`, `RunPlanner` (profile `runGoal`); app `RunGoalCard`, `RunSessionSheet`, `RunGoalEditor`; tests `RunPlanTests`; demo screens `train-run`, `train-hybrid`, `run-session`, `run-start`, `run-goal`.
- **Send to Apple's Workout app** (#610, designs A + C from `t8.png`, WorkoutKit): any planned run or cardio session has **Send to Workout app** (Apple's preview sheet: send to Watch, alerts, Workout Buddy) built as a custom workout: warm-up, interval blocks with repeats, cool-down, pace alerts (±3 %) or heart-rate zone alerts. **Start** asks MonkeyFit player or Apple Workout, with *Remember for running* per sport. No automatic scheduling. Results come back through Apple Health and mark the run done.

### Changed
- **What's new 2026-10-30.1** (bundle 87, TestFlight 87): Silky moves (60 fps exercise animations, #945), Focus tile next to the Insights ask bar (#943); 2026-10-29.3 moved to history as build 86.
- **Pick a focus on the ask bar's line** (#939 follow-up, TestFlight 86 feedback): with *A bit of everything*, the *Pick a focus* chip sat on its own line above *Ask Coach about your training…*. It is now a square **Focus** tile on the right of the ask bar, same height, one line. Before the first workout (no ask bar yet) the chip stays. App `InsightsFocusTile`, `ProgressAskBar.onPickFocus`.
- **Clearer Insights ask bar** (#938, B + C mix from `ask-history-label/proposals.png`): *✦ Ask your history…* didn't say what it was for. The bar now shows the Coach monkey and reads **Ask Coach about your training…**, and the starter chips (*Best incline barbell bench press this year* ran off screen) are one compact row of gradient question tiles: **BEST** *Bench press this year*, **COMPARE** *Bench press vs June*, **COUNT** *Workouts this month*, **VOLUME** *Weekly volume*. Same answers as before, on device. Engine `HistoryStarter`, `HistoryAsk.starterTiles` (tests `HistoryAskTests`); app `StarterTile`.
- **Production scaling step 1** (#923, audit 4): production ran on one 256 MB machine that stopped when idle (5.6 s cold start measured), a 1 GB volume and one off-site copy a day. Now `fly.production.toml` runs **1 GB RAM, always on** (`min_machines_running = 1`, no auto-stop); **Fly setup** (production) grows the volume to **10 GB** (`flyctl volumes extend`, only grows); and the API uploads an **encrypted copy every hour** to the Tigris bucket (`hourly/`, same AES-256-GCM `BACKUP_KEY` format, kept 48 h, `BACKUP_OFFSITE_HOURS`), so a lost volume costs at most an hour of data instead of a day. **Fly backups** → offsite-fetch accepts `YYYY-MM-DDTHH`. Backend `startHourlyCopyJob`, `OffsiteStore.uploadHourly` / `listHourly` (tests `backup.test.ts`, `offsite.test.ts`). Cost ≈ US$10–15 a month more.

- **Dependency bumps** (dependabot #645-#651): backend fastify 5.12.7, @fastify/compress 9.2.2, nodemailer 10.1.7, vitest 5.0.5; CI actions/setup-go v7, actions/setup-java v6, docker/build-push-action v7. Backend tests pass (240).

### Fixed
- **3D position guide: body on the equipment, zoom** (#959): on the leg press the feet floated off the sled, the hands sat beside the handles, and the guide had no zoom. Now **feet** put their soles on the plate whatever way the toes point (the foot's roll follows the shin; rig.py `dorsum`, `BodyRig.dorsum`) and **foot plates** move onto the soles (leg press family, hack squat, cable row, calf machines; a sled moves with its carriage and plates). **Handles**: each gripping hand gets its own handle through the fist (mirrored when the scene drew one), a cable that ran into the hands gets a bar. **Rollers**: leg extension / curl rollers, pivots and pedals are padded rollers (not red balls), foam rollers are foam cylinders, the air bike / rower / ski erg fan is a wheel. **Hangs**: pull-up family bars sit high enough to hang without kneeling; rings and straps end in the hands (iron cross hangs instead of standing). The 3D data is regenerated from the current 2D scenes (some exercises still had the raw export: walls instead of mats). **Zoom**: pinch the stage or use **− 1.0× +** (bottom right, 1–3×, tap the level to reset). **Clearer position** (design B + C from `position-guide-clarity/proposals.png`): the numbered pins and angle tags on the body are gone; each technique step is a plain-language **callout** in a column beside the figure with a dashed leader and a ring on its joint (never overlapping), the angles sit in one line under the stage. **Tap a callout**, a step chip (**All · 1 Hands · 2 Back…**) or a cue row: the camera zooms onto that joint with one big gauge (*82°, Good: 72–92° here*) and its arc, the cue shows under the chips; **All** goes back. Tools `contact.py` (`foot_plates`, `handles`, `machine_parts`, `straps`, `lift_hang`), `rig.py`; app `PositionGuideView` zoom, callouts (`layoutCallouts`), steps and focus, `PositionCamera(zoom:focus:)`, roller / foam props.
- **Exercise animations: parts that didn't hold together** (#949): the *Bike* saddle floated above the frame (its seat tube stopped mid-air). A one-by-one pass over all 441 animations fixed 21 more: leg press + leg press calf raise (sled carriage floated off its rail), single-leg press / extension, leg extension (cable bent round a missing pulley), TRX hamstring curl (anchor bar hung from nothing), chest press machine (backrest strut short of the pad), machine-assisted dip (top beam through the head, lever hub off the column), machine calf raise (cable short of the carriage), seated dip machine (cable ended above the hub), belt squat (plate sank into the floor), sandbag lunge (bag covered the arms holding it), sissy squat (hand off the post), treadmill run / incline walk (handrail short of the console), cross-trainer (foot off the pedal), depth jump, ball slam, hang power clean, power clean (feet above the box / floor), world's greatest stretch (planted hand in the air), open book (body floating over the mat), lat stretch (elbows inside the bench). Frames, light frames and 60 fps clips regenerated for those exercises; the HD pack republishes on merge (ball slam now 33 frames: older builds show its light frames).
- **Exercise animations stood still in build 87** (#950): "Play movement" kept the resting picture while only the path dot and tempo chip moved. `MotionClipPlayer` kept the clip's track but not its asset (a track refers to its asset weakly), so the play's composition came out empty and no picture was ever decoded. The player now holds the asset; and if a clip still gives no picture (empty composition, reader failure, nothing within 0.4 s) the play falls back to the still frames, so the movement never stands still. Machine-mode Watch hint on leg press: "the sled handle" → **"the side of the sled"** (leg press handles sit on the fixed seat); leg press calf raise counts as a small movement. Hack squat unchanged.
- **Climb couldn't be picked on a workout + sport day** (#947): on a day with a workout and climbing (Train shows *1 Lift · 2 Climb*), tapping **2 Climb** opened the gym workout's page, and the **Sport** tile only offered a blank *Already done* sport log and *Track live* with a paired Watch. Now the **Climb** part (any sport part) opens a menu: today **Track live** (climbing opens Climbing live on the iPhone, Watch or not) and **Log climb**; an earlier day **Log climb**. Today's Sport tile names the day's sport: **Log climb** opens the climb log and **Track climbing live** opens Climbing live; other sports open Log sport with the sport already picked. App `DayComboRow.onSportTrack/onSportLog`, `DaySportTile.sport`.
- **Fuel showed a skipped workout as today's training day** (#936): after skipping today's workout on Train, Fuel still said *Today · Training day · Day 1 · Push*, planned recovery meals around it and showed training icons on the date strip. Fuel (tab, Train Fuel widget, Home Screen Fuel widget) now reads a day's workouts the way Train does, so skipped ones are gone; the Train Calendar widget and the Home Screen week dots no longer mark a skipped day as planned either. Engine `PlanCalendar.sessions(in:weekday:skipped:)` (tests `PlanCalendarTests`); app `AppModel.daySessions(in:weekday:)`.
- **Machine page picture cut off** (#896): Explore → Machines → a machine showed its picture in a fixed 180 pt strip filled edge to edge, so the 4:3 art lost heads, feet and machine tops (triceps machine, cross-trainer, …) and the small body map in its corner was cut. The picture now shows whole at its 4:3 shape. The **Trains** body map now lights each muscle by its share of the machine's work instead of the top three at full heat, so the triceps machine shows triceps hot and chest / front delts lighter. Engine `Exercise.muscleLoad(using:)` (test `ExploreMachineTests`).
- **A discarded custom gym session no longer offers Resume** (#903): after *Discard* on a custom gym session and the Undo bar running out, Train still showed **Resume custom session**. The player that discarded it put it back when it showed again as the workout map closed, cancelling its own discard. Only a reopened player (Undo, or starting it again) now keeps a discarded workout; once the Undo bar is gone the run is cleared and the custom session dropped, so Train offers a new one. App `PendingDiscard`, `AppModel.dropFreestyle`; UI test `WorkoutMapUITests.testDiscardedCustomSessionIsNotResumable`.
- **Share nudge stuck on Train** (#905): the *Level 5 · Your story is ready · Share* bar stayed above the tab bar until Share was tapped, covering the cards under it, and its ✕ did nothing on device (an overlay on the tab view took no taps). It now shows in its own window like the Undo bar, with a grabber: it **hides by itself after 8 s**, and ✕, a tap on the bar (outside Share) or a **swipe down** hide it at once. Same for every moment nudge (level, badge, PR, week goal, new programme, new meal). App `MomentToastCenter`.
- **A workout logged on a rest day shows as a workout day** (#902): an extra workout logged on Wed 28 (*1 logged* on its card) still showed the moon of a rest day in the Train date strip and an empty day in the Calendar. Now any day with a logged workout or sport and no plan session shows done (lime ✓) in the strip and the Calendar, and Coach on that Calendar day hears what was logged instead of *rest day*. Engine `PlanCalendar.loggedDays` (tests `LoggedDaysTests`).
- **Box, physio sigh and wake-up start at the 5 minutes studied** (#892): Box breathing defaulted to 3′ and the physio sigh to 1′, though the trial behind them (Balban 2023) used 5 min a day. These three now start on **5′**; 3′ (and 1′ of sighs for quick relief) stay as shorter options, box keeps 10′ for a longer practice. Length chips stay in ascending order. How it works → Mind score & calm explains the 4-4-4-4 count (equal counts at a comfortable pace, about 4 breaths a minute, slow-breathing range).
- **Freestyle: ✕ instead of an empty pause sheet** (#857, design A from `freestyle-pause-empty/proposals.png`): the freestyle start screen's pause button opened a *Workout map · Nothing added yet* sheet with blurred rows, *Keep for later* and *Move to Watch*, with nothing to keep. It is now the same **✕** as the player: with nothing added it closes at once and the *Workout discarded* bar offers Undo. The empty map sheet and its *Keep for later* button are gone. App `FreestyleStartView`, `WorkoutPlayerView.mapActions`.
- **Undo on the Undo bar could not be tapped** (#857): after discarding a workout (e.g. an empty custom gym session) the *Workout discarded* bar sat on the tab bar and Undo did nothing. The bar's window let every touch through, because SwiftUI answers each point with its hosting view; it now takes touches only inside the bar's measured frame. The tab bar height is measured again once the closed screen is gone, so the bar sits above the tab bar. App `UndoClose.swift` (`PassthroughWindow`, `UndoBarState.barFrame`).
- **Science review of the other areas** (#864): checked every How it works topic against the research. **Readiness pill and auto-tune** now compare your 7-day HRV average with your 30-day one instead of a single noisy reading (as the Mind score, #862); the readiness sheet shows the 7-day value. **Carb loading** before a long event is 10 g/kg (was 8; ACSM 10–12 g/kg for the 36–48 h before). The **cool-down** is described as optional (feels good, no proven drop in soreness or injuries; Van Hooren & Peake 2018). **Starting loads** and **water 35 ml/kg** are labelled as app design, water gets the EFSA reference. Engine `Fuel.carbLoadPerKg`.
- **Mind score follows the research on training and HRV** (#862): more workout days lowered the Mind score (3 days in a row left 20 % of the training part), though exercise reliably lifts mood (Singh 2023).
