Now one or two workout days in the last 3 score full, none 60 %, three in a row 70 %; the part is called **Training** (was Load). HRV now compares your **7-day average** with your 30-day one instead of a single noisy reading (Mind page tile *HRV 7d*). How it works → Mind score says the weights are app design and adds sources (Singh 2023, Watson 2015 for sleep). Engine `MindScore.loadPoints`; app `HealthSnapshot.hrvWeek`.
- **4-7-8 counted in breaths, not minutes** (#861): 4-7-8 offered 4 or 8 *minutes* (13 or 25 breaths), well past the method's own guidance (Weil: 4 breaths twice a day, up to 8 once it feels easy). It now offers **4 breaths** (about 1¼ min) or **8 breaths**, on the iPhone and the Watch (chips *4 breaths* / *8 breaths*, Watch *4×*). Other sessions checked against the studies they come from (box, physio sigh and wake-up: 5 min a day as in Balban 2023; body scan and pre-lift focus: about 6 breaths a minute) and kept. How it works → Mind score & calm lists the length rules with sources, and says the counts are a pace to follow. Engine `Breath.byBreaths`, `lengthLabel`; `WBreath.byBreaths` (older iPhone apps send nil = minutes).
- **Mind check-in: chips explained** (#859): the Sleep, Work, Family… chips under the mood squares had no label, so their purpose was unclear. They now sit under **What's behind it? · optional** with *Pick any. Coach uses them to spot what lifts or drags your mood.*
- **UI tests (demo mode) flaky on busy runners** (#741, #597): the suite failed on a different test each run (*Set logged*, *no keyboard focus*, first-test launch timeout) and blocked PRs. CI now builds first (`build-for-testing`), waits for the simulator to finish booting (`simctl bootstatus -b`), then runs `test-without-building` with `-retry-tests-on-failure -test-iterations 2` (a test failing twice still fails). `testStartLogSetAndFinish` taps *SET n DONE* again if the first tap landed mid-transition and waits 20 s; `testPushLowerOnlySetupOpensAgain` waits for the keyboard before typing.
- **Track live no longer looks live after a discarded climb** (#847): on a climbing day the Track live button stayed white after *Discard session*, as if a session still ran. It is now white only while a climbing session runs (*Climbing live*), dark otherwise like Log.
- **Skipped is gone, adding a workout no longer brings it back** (#849): on a day whose workout was skipped (e.g. Pull on a climbing day), **+ Workout** added the new one but the card flipped back to the skipped Pull (*Wednesday · skipped*, **Unskip Pull**, Move · Unskip · Sport). Now a skipped workout leaves its day for good once the Undo bar is over: no *skipped · Unskip* line, Unskip tile, button or menu on Train, the week strip or the Calendar day sheet; a day with an added workout shows just that workout (and its sport). App `AppModel.skippedOff`, `WeekView.daySession`, `SkippedLine` removed.
- **Unexplained Saturday "Cond." workout: it's your HIIT day, now removable every week** (#841, design B from `hiit-saturday/proposals.png`): with HIIT days on (My plan → HIIT), Coach puts the HIIT workout on a free day (often Saturday). The Calendar tile said *Cond.*, nothing linked it to HIIT, and **Remove…** offered no **Every Saturday** because Saturday isn't a gym day (it would also have dropped a lifting day). Now the tile reads **HIIT**, the Calendar day card says **⚡ HIIT day · 1× a week, Coach picks the day** with **HIIT ›** opening HIIT settings, and Remove… on a HIIT day offers **Every Saturday** (*no more HIIT days in your plan*, or *HIIT 1× a week instead of 2*): one HIIT day fewer, the others stay on their days, lifting untouched; Undo toast. *Every <weekday>* on a lifting day no longer counts HIIT days as workout days. Engine `Profile.droppingHiitDay` (tests `HiitTests`); app `AppModel.isPlannedHiit`, `removeEveryWeek(_:hiit:)`, `SessionPreview.hiitSource`.
- **Wall session: back from a part works** (#828): back from Wall warm-up (or a Sport prep warm-up) opened it again a few times, then jumped to Train past the Wall session page. Both pages now open the same way as the sessions in them (`WallSessionRoute`, `SportPrepRoute`), so back returns to the page.
- **Climbing day: Wall session easy to find again** (#826): on a climbing day today's sport card opened the Wall session from a button labelled **Prep** (flame), so members saw no Wall session at all. On climbing that button now reads **Wall session** (hand icon); other sports keep Prep.
- **Remove workout sheet fits its content** (#838): the Calendar *Remove <workout>* sheet had a guessed height and a navigation bar, so an empty blurred band sat above the title with the ✕ floating in it and the bottom showed the page behind. Now the sheet is measured to its options (as the player Change sheet, #807), the ✕ sits in the title row and no nav bar is drawn. App `RemoveWorkoutSheet`.
- **Calendar: tap a workout to open it** (#840): on the Calendar day sheet the workout card (title, stripe, exercise list) looked tappable but did nothing, and a later week's workout could not be opened at all. Now the whole card opens the full workout page (chevron next to the duration): this week's as from Train, a later week's as Train's *See workout* (#758: edit, swap or remove exercises ahead; nothing to start). App `SessionPreview.summary`.
- **Holiday days drop the weekly sport** (#830): on an away day (holiday, trip, can't train) the Train day card still showed the weekly climbing block and the Climbing day card. Now away days drop the recurring weekly sport and climbing everywhere (day card, week strip, Calendar, Fuel, sport-day nudges, planning around sport days); a sport added for that day (Sport → Plan) still shows. Engine `Profile.sport(onWeekday:key:date:away:)`, `Profile.sportDays(on:calendar:away:)` (tests `SportOffTests`); app `AppModel.daySport`, `AppModel.sportDays`.
- **Full app bug audit 3** (#817–#825; report `full-app-audit-3/report.md`): every defect found app-wide fixed (no leftover).
  - **Player + machine setup** (#817): after Swap, *Not here?*, Remove or Undo the set card reloads (sets were saved under the swapped-out exercise or not at all); a weight changed this workout carries over when coming back to the exercise (superset, Previous, resume); correcting a done set and tapping an effort rates that set instead of also completing the next; correcting an earlier set no longer overwrites the coming sets' weight; Discard removes the sets logged during the run; a second run of the same session today no longer edits the first run's sets; a run closed and resumed later keeps its first sets for Undo and the finish report; HIIT runs survive iOS closing the app; a superset round keeps its order after a restore; *Train now* on an old missed notification is off once the workout is done; a setup value typed in an unnamed field is kept as *Setting*; the WOD big screen no longer plays a burst of cues back from the background.
  - **Day state** (#818): skips made ahead, one-off places and removals keep working while a programme runs; reminders and the Agenda widget leave out skipped and removed workouts; *Move to…* never offers an earlier day; *Remove → Every X* is not offered for added or moved workouts (and *Just X* on an added one removes it); a removed past day is a rest day on the strip, past-day card and This week list; Unskip clears the removed mark; Calendar's earlier days this week offer *Log a workout* instead of adding to today; logging a plan workout's copy on a past day doesn't tick the planned one; the done ring, This week tile, widgets and *N planned* leave out skipped and removed workouts; a later-week day is shown as rest only when all its workouts are skipped; the daily Coach routine pick ignores a skipped or removed today and follows sport days off and trips.
  - **Fuel + Social** (#819): ticking a recipe keeps foods already logged on that meal; Just ate with every meal ticked adds an extra meal instead of replacing one; a meal shake no longer wipes the meal's log; pinned meals follow when the day's meal slots move, and meals ticked on a slot gone from the plan stay in Just ate's done list; *Not eaten after all* also removes the foods logged next to the recipe; buddy live-share toggle can't crash; a failed challenge vote or plan unshare is put back; blocking from a post or profile also hides the member's plans and is kept offline; feed paging no longer skips posts sharing a timestamp; the load-more row retries once the cursor moves; members hidden after reports no longer show in feeds or on public share pages (backend).
  - **Watch, Garmin, notifications** (#820): a Watch relaunch no longer rolls back what was done offline; Watch and Garmin sets arriving late keep the time they were done (the record gets its weights); one Apple Health workout per iPhone workout tracked by the Watch; a Watch-player workout no longer reuses a paused iPhone-tracking session; the water *Later* reminder survives opening the app; Garmin events are never applied twice.
  - Water removed in the app is also removed from Apple Health; a failed *load more* in Social shows Retry instead of a spinner.
  - **Pass 2, with a member's eyes** (#822, #823, #824, #825; every CI screen and 10 member journeys):
    - **Workout live on the Watch** (#822, design 1A from `full-app-audit-3/mockups/proposals-1.png`): Train's Next up becomes the live card (*LIVE ON WATCH · N′*, current exercise) with one **Open on iPhone** button; no second Start for that workout anywhere (it opened an empty player and saved two workouts).
    - **One Start on Train** (#823, design 2C): on a workout day the Coach card's Start folds into Next up as a *Coach: …* line, so header, Next up and Start fit above the tab bar on programme days too. Rest days keep the Coach card.
    - **Undo bar** (#822, design 4A from `proposals-3.png`) after Move to…, Swap with…, missed Mark done / Do today / Skip, DONE after a finish (*Workout saved · Undo*), removing a trip or Can't train, switching to Hyrox / Snow ready, and closing a new sport day with something entered.
    - **Watch summary** (#824, design 5A): names the workout, XP / time / kcal / avg HR as 2×2 tiles above DONE; rings home says *HOLD TO START* in readable type and labels readiness.
    - **Player Coach bubble is see-through** and still fades by itself, so the set values under it stay readable.
    - **Journeys** (#822): a missed check-in for a week already gone no longer offers dead buttons or claims *skipped*, and Move it moves the workout instead of marking the day Can't train; signing out keeps Fuel, WOD timers, swims and saved HIITs on this iPhone for that account (they were wiped, and Fuel is never on the server); the check-in sheet closes with ✕ top right; the finish summary's time stops counting; *Also save to Health* only shows for a new measurement; the tour moves on only with NEXT and names the tabs as they are; Verify email can resend again after 30 s; the water sheet says which day's pace it shows; Invite promises a Pro month only once Pro is on.
    - **Copy and numbers** (#823): invite and post share links never use a test address (`localhost`); one ✕ on Train's noticed cards; no debug *vertices* line under the 3D body; *MonkeyWorkout* in member text; *stay as I am*; gym distances in metres / km; date ranges like *Oct 29 – 30*; *Monkey noticed* without *1 thing*; Progress level card matches the header; programme *start → now* rows hide equal or empty values; a missed session has one name everywhere; no mid-number or mid-word wraps (*82.5*, *Compromised run*), minute marks no longer clipped, *save 17%*, Snowboard fits, *Open authenticator* fits; Home widgets title once.
    - **Watch** (#824): boxing, swings and swim screens render in demo; one XP number per workout; rep ranges shown in full (*4×6–8*); auto-log banner turns into *Logged*; records show kg; Pro text says the same in every look.
    - **CI** (#825): a screenshot captured blank or still loading is retaken (up to twice) and listed in the job summary.
  - **Round 3, no leftover** (user picks as decision cards):
    - **Paywall** (A): one lime buy button; products load on tap if not loaded yet.
    - **Notifications settings** (A): reminders turned off dim their cards and say *Off*; missed check-in is honest about a past week; ✕ top right.
    - **Edit exercises** (A): two-line rows, names never cut.
    - **Grey lists become gradient tiles** (A, `GradientTile`): cycle phases, Groceries, Taste, Train presets, climbing categories, signature workouts, How it works.
    - **Onboarding** (A): ‹ Back in the header on every step after the first.
    - **Watch workout finishing on iPhone** (A): the mirrored player shows the finish screen with its summary; **Finish workout** ends it on the Watch; DONE gives the *Workout saved · Undo* bar.
    - **iPad** (A + B): sign-in column capped, Calendar two-week grid, session flow as a grid, live-on-Watch card, Undo after Move / programme switch / Plan sport.
    - **Gym counts** (keep both, clearer labels): machine and item counts come from one source and say which they count.
    - **UI test**: Your gym tests match the new *N items · M machines* row.
    - **Close buttons**: water, cook mode, challenge score, suggest a challenge, measurement guide, workout style, muscle picker and report sheet use the lime ✕ top right.
    - **Demo data agrees with itself**: one level (13), one avatar letter, Train / Progress / week chest show the same done session, *today* is the same weekday on every screen, one growth goal, hotel gym lists match, *Deload* everywhere, Bento says *Work sets*.
    - **Truncations**: style, goal, records, set-style, muscle-tile, focus, gym-result, Coach-memory and customise rows wrap to two lines; measurement tips shortened; gym chip and widget record names shrink to fit; day sheet button reads *Remove*.
    - **Ask Coach**: the *no barbell on your back* reply no longer comes with back squats or good mornings.
    - **Player and data**: a resumed workout keeps its first sets in the report; discarding a workout removes its set logs; a rest running when the app closed resumes; HIIT resumes after a relaunch; Train now is off once the session is done; WOD timer skips cues missed while in background; unnamed machine fields kept as *Setting*.
- **Planning a sport on a rest-day-ed day did nothing** (#815): after a sport day was turned into a rest day (Rest day, #671), planning a sport on it again (e.g. today's wall session: Sport → Plan → Plan Climbing day) closed the sheet but the day stayed *Rest day* with the Sport tile, so it looped. Planning once, a trip or every week from that day now gives the day its sport back. Engine `Profile.clearSportOff` (test `SportOffTests`).
- **Separate push / lower weight only where it makes sense** (#813, design B from `push-lower-capable/proposals.png`): the machine setup sheet offered the push / lower split on every exercise, even the Cross-trainer. Now the full card shows only for strength moves on a **weight-stack machine** (leg curl and extension, leg press, chest and shoulder press, lat pulldown, rows, pec deck, abductor, ab crunch, rotation, calf, biceps, triceps, glute, squat, pullover, lateral raise machines). Other loaded strength moves (free weights, cable, plate-loaded) get a small **⇅ Push ≠ lower** chip in the settings chips, for smart machines the catalog doesn't know; cardio, stretches, holds and bodyweight never show it. Setups already switched on keep the card. Engine `Equipment.weightStack`, `Exercise.splitLoadMachine/splitLoadPossible` (tests `SplitLoadTests`).
- **Player Setup button stays in place** (#809, option B from `setup-strip/proposals.png`, without the bar): once a machine setup was saved, the dashed **Setup** button left the exercise card and a wide **My setup** bar took a line from the animation, which shrank; a setup with only *Separate push / lower weight* on showed the bar with no values, so it looked empty and not editable. Now **Setup** keeps its slot next to Form / Change, filled white with a tick (*Setup ✓*) once saved, and opens the setup to edit; the animation keeps its full height. The bar and the player's full-screen setup photo are gone (the photo shows in the setup sheet). **Use** from another gym copies the push / lower switch too. App `MachineNoteChip` (replaces `MachineNoteStrip`, `MachineNoteAddChip`, `MachineNotePhotoViewer`); UI test `PlayerSetupUITests`.
- **Player Change sheet fits its options** (#807): the Change sheet (Swap for similar, Taken? Free zone, Remove) used a fixed height, so it opened far above its buttons with an empty area below. The sheet now measures its content and sits right under the last option. App `ChangeExerciseSheet`.
- **Player clock no longer wraps** (#804): with a full player toolbar (progress, Coach, mic, TV, clock, close) the workout time split onto two lines (*0:2* / *8*). The time pill is now one line at its full width (also h:mm:ss and the finish-early countdown); the progress dots give up width first. Same on the freestyle start screen.
- **Exercise picker grid lines up** (#790, design A from `exercise-picker-grid/proposals.png`): in *Add exercises* the *+N variations* pill sat under the card, so columns drifted out of line, and the + / order badge (top right) covered the muscle map drawn into some pictures. Now every card has the same height with variations as a footer row inside it (empty when none), the badge is a rounded square at the bottom right of the picture, the New exercise card fills its row, and a fade sits behind the *Add N exercises* bar. App `PickerCard`, `VariationsChip`.
- **Removed day no longer says Skipped** (#787): after *Remove… → Just <day>* the Calendar week tile still labelled the day *Skipped*. Now it shows as a plain rest day, like the day sheet.
- **Skipped workouts leave the Calendar** (#784): a workout skipped on Train (this week or ahead) still showed in the Calendar week card, the day sheet (full preview with Move to…) and the *Next 4 weeks* count. Now the day tile shows *Skipped* as a rest day, the day sheet shows *Pyramid power skipped · Unskip* (same line as Train's rest day card, #698) and the count leaves it out. App `SkippedLine` (shared with `RestDayCard`), `AppModel.isSkipped(_:in:current:)`.
- **Workout page hero no longer clips, HIIT days show rounds** (#782): on the Poster hero a two-line title, the chips and four Target bars overflowed the fixed height, so the title touched the top edge and the last bar was cut. The hero now grows to fit (map size is its minimum). HIIT days showed **0 Work sets** (their blocks aren't lifting): the tile now shows **Rounds** (work intervals). **Up first** listed warm-up moves on HIIT days; it now shows the main HIIT moves. App `SessionBoard` (`Session.setsStat`, `upFirst`).
- **Vivid sport and option tiles** (#755): in Plan sport (and every sport strip: Log sport, Log climb, Add sport, Around sport) the unpicked sports were muddy brown, dark green and maroon, unlike the full sport list. Unpicked tiles now keep their full gradient and the picked one gets the white ring, as in the full list. Same fix in every picker that dimmed its gradients: `SelectableGradient` (challenge level and format, cardio minutes, muscle groups, training style, climb type, WOD timer, Wall session day, warm-up / cool-down, expert lift, Pro plans), Training profile goal and variety cards, post visibility, daily routine tiles (now with a ring) and away setup cards. Widget boards keep dimming added widgets (`selectableGradient(dim:)`).
- **Settings chapter jump lands under the bar** (#743): tapping a chapter chip (You, Training, Watches, Look & apps…) scrolled so the chapter title and first rows hid under the pinned search + chapter bar. Chips now have their own ids (`chip-…`; a bare chapter id clashed with the chapter's jump target) and the jump target sits one measured header height above the chapter, so the chapter title shows just under the bar at any text size. App `SettingsChapterBar`, `SettingsGroup`, `EnvironmentValues.settingsJumpInset`.
- **Smoke UI test after logging a set** (#730): `AppSmokeUITests.testStartLogSetAndFinish` failed on main ("Set logged"): after SET DONE the player shows the rest card, whose controls read **SKIP REST** / **CONTINUE** and whose timer is not a static text, so the check found none of its targets in 5 s. It now also accepts SKIP REST, CONTINUE, +15 s, any "Rest" element and FINISH WORKOUT, within 10 s.
- **Couldn't say where a workout happens** (#705): the day card's 📍 place chip (#700) and Training profile → Where I train only showed with two or more saved gyms, so most members had no way to pick. The chip now shows on every workout day this week, the long-press menu has **Where · <place>** (Just this day / Every <day>), and **Home** is always a choice (your saved home gym, else *Home · bodyweight*). On a **travel day** from Calendar the chip shows the trip (✈︎) and the menu points to Calendar, since the trip's kit plans that day. Picking a weekly place no longer narrows the week to that one day. `PlaceMenuItems`, `SavedGym.builtInHome`, `AppModel.tripThisWeek`.
- **Backend audit: hardening, privacy, correctness, speed** (#712, #713, #714, #715, audit 2026-10-24): **Hardening**: 50 MiB byte quota per member, lower row caps (10 000 workouts, climbs, sports, PR attempts, measurements), pulls page with `limit` + `nextCursor` (no `limit` = whole list, streamed), the export streams row by row; a password change, reset or sign out everywhere now ends access tokens too (`users.tokens_valid_after`) and re-auth grants are single use for password, email and delete; gym tallies count one vote per member and keep one row per member and place; achievement pushes 20 a day, buddy invite push once a day per pair; call-name recordings count against `AI_DAILY_LIMIT`, stay in memory only and go with the account; `SUBSCRIPTION_TESTS=on` refused on production. **Privacy**: problem reports redact text and every fact; export adds Garmin, community level/pronouns/photo, reports filed, library saves, plan copies, passkeys, 2-step and watch-key status; custom exercise photos stripped; word filter on gym map zones; members hidden by reports 404; leaving Community removes your photo and the reports you filed; account delete clears its throttle records. **Correctness**: sync ids lower-cased (stored rows migrated once), `gym{lat, lon}` accepted, export sorted by instant, re-sharing keeps caption and visibility, plans page by creation, challenge ranks and counts skip blocked and hidden members (ISO week checked), copy/save counts counted from who copied, deleting someone else's post or plan 404, cheers checked before removal, notification prefs merge, 409 `quota_exceeded` / 413 `too_large`, Garmin inbox `eventId`; API docs corrected. **Speed**: gym columns on community profiles and normalized gym `name_key`, batched member and plan stats, paged Following list, indexes on per-member cheers, live cheers and challenge scores. Backend tests `hardening`, `communityAudit`, `hotQueries`.
- **iOS and Watch audit fixes: logic, smoothness and animations** (#716, #719, #720, audit 2026-10-24). **Logic (#716)**: a session skipped ahead for next week stays skipped when the week rolls (the roll cleared it again); a workout running past midnight keeps its sets, load suggestions and re-logs (`ActiveWorkout.startedAt`), and the record's weights are that run's heaviest per exercise, not every run's last; a library workout in place of a planned day remembers which session it replaces (`PlanInsert.replacingId`, older inserts still go by index); Siri's "log 8 at 80" during the rest or the Next-in countdown corrects the set just done instead of moving on, and only answers *Logged* when it was; the Watch plays supersets like the iPhone (members alternate each round, rest after the round; `WItem.group`) and uses the iPhone's loggable rule; the week rolls at midnight with the app open; water and supplement reminders keep their clock time on DST days; the HIIT player no longer fires a burst of missed beeps back from the background; the duplicate `.inactive` case (never ran) merged; sign-out clears queued water-widget taps and saved HIITs, and taking the water queue keeps a glass tapped meanwhile; the Home widget hides yesterday's Next up and its Start; the Watch picks the next session by planned day and passes skipped ones. **Smoothness (#720)**: HIIT steps built once and the clock paused while paused; the Watch HIIT clock ticks once a minute wrist down, stops when paused, and its workout ends after 15′ without a step (the iPhone queues the end message out of reach); daily routine stats and the past day card's history cached; Fuel saves coalesced and written off the main thread; the Duo posture motion manager made on first use; the next exercise's frames decoded ahead (cache 150 MB); profile photos downsampled with ImageIO into a small cache; listening bars still with Reduce Motion; the live room poll stops with its room. **Animations (#719)**: gaits, carries and machines (treadmill run, incline walk, farmer / front-rack / trap bar carry, sled drag and sprint, mountain climber, bicycle crunch, stair climber, bike, air bike, cross-trainer) are drawn as one whole cycle and play forward and wrap instead of half a stride and back (`ExerciseMotion.cyclic`, `cycles.py`), with a *STRIDE* / *TURN* chip and no tempo; the player's replay respects Reduce Motion; the HIIT figure keeps moving for the interval at a quicker pace instead of 8 lifting reps; the arms of the ball, TRX and slider hamstring curls lie on the mat instead of the elbows going through it; the max hangs plate hangs behind the legs; skater squat and sled sprint toes stay out of the floor; weighted pull-up needs a dip belt; `check.py` adds an absolute floor check and a knee / elbow bend scan, `sample.py` shows start, middle and end.
- **Daily routines can be removed** (#703): in Explore → Routines the ✓ of a routine on Train did nothing, so a routine could never come off the Daily row. The ✓ now toggles it off (the last one off hides the row; pin one back any time), and a Daily tile's long press adds **Remove from Daily**. `RoutinePrefs.removing`.
- **Skipped day becomes a rest day** (#698, design A from `skipped-rest-day/proposals.png`): skipping today's (or a later day's) workout kept the workout card (*Sunday · skipped*, Unskip button, Move · Plan sport · Unskip) and a dash in the strip. A day whose workouts are all skipped, with nothing else on it, is now a **rest day**: moon in the strip (or the day's sport), the rest-day card with Workout · Plan sport · Recover (sport block on a sport day) and a quiet *~~Pull skipped~~ · Unskip* line under the title. Today skipped opens on that rest day, its chip jumps to the next session (next week's too). Later weeks' skipped days too; long press in the strip still offers Unskip. Also: with nothing left this week, the strip opened on next week's first session (Mon 26 on Sat 24); it now opens on today like every other day, its chip jumps to next week's session.
- **Style stopped changing Train after editing it** (#681): Settings → Style (Monkey coach / Quest map / Level bar) only changed the default Train layout; once Train widgets were edited, the saved layout kept the old look's card. Now a Style change, on the iPhone or the Watch, swaps the Coach card and the Level bar in place (same spot, same size) in an edited layout too (`TrainLayout.restyled`, tests `TrainLayoutRestyleTests`). Settings row **Widgets** renamed **Home Screen widgets**: it sets the iOS Home and Lock Screen widgets, not Train.
- **Sport strip tiles jumped on tap** (#691, design A from `sport-strip/proposals.png`): on Track live, Log sport, Log climb and Plan sport, a tapped sport moved to the first slot, so tiles slid over each other and the tapped spot held another sport. The strip now keeps the member's order and the picked tile lights up where it is; it scrolls to the picked sport when off screen.
- **Sport day showed the rest moon in the week strip** (#670): a sport day of a later week (climbing Tue 3 Nov) showed the rest moon while its day card said Climbing; the strip read the calendar's climbing days instead of the day's sport. Later weeks now use the same sport as the day card (dated days first). Also, today's **Wall session** / **Sport prep** bar no longer shows under another picked day (a Mountain bike or Pull day showed today's climbing Wall session).
- **Fuel set-up forced on first open** (#686, design A from `fuel-setup-skip/proposals.png`): the first time Fuel opened, its settings sheet popped up and testers found no way out without filling it in. Fuel now opens straight on today with default pointers and a lime **Set up Fuel** card on top: **Set up · 1 min** opens the settings (✕ top right leaves them for later, **Save my fuel** saves), **Later** or ✕ hides the card with an Undo bar. **Settings → You → Onboarding** gets a **Fuel** row (Later / Done ✓). Demo screen `fuel-setup-card`.
- **Tab bar folded when scrolling** (#677): on iOS 26 the bottom tab bar shrank while scrolling down (Liquid Glass design A, #616). It now always stays full size on every tab (`tabBarMinimizeBehavior(.never)`).
- **Gym map empty on first open** (#673): the Gym map from Explore's gym sheet or a workout's route card showed an empty grid with only the door the first time, the drawn zones only on the second open. The plan sheet now gets a real binding to its draft, so the zones it lays out show at once.
- **Profile edits dropped settings kept beside the profile** (#664): any training profile edit or new week rebuilt the synced profile without the sport add-on (#620), calisthenics skills (#621), cycling FTP (#623) and the life-stage check-in (#625), so they were lost (the life-stage check-in already on starting its programme). They are now kept, like Growing with you.
- **Recover card only on this week's rest days** (#659): the rest-day **Recover** routine (#619) showed on today's and this week's coming rest days only; a rest day picked in a later week showed none. It now shows on every coming rest day (not on climbing or sport days).
- **Climbing showed on a snowboard day** (#658): a dated sport day on a weekly climbing day kept Log climb, the Wall session bar and the climbing icon in the week strip. The day's sport now puts dated sport days and trips first everywhere on Train.
- **TestFlight build 63 upload rejected** (#656, #605): App Store Connect error 90626, an App Intent description can't name Apple products. **Log a set** now reads "Logs the coming set of the workout in progress." (was "…running on this iPhone"). All App Intent, App Shortcut and entity titles, descriptions and phrases checked: no other Apple product names.
- **TestFlight build 62 upload rejected** (#602, #603): App Store Connect refused `UIBackgroundModes: workout-processing` on the iPhone app ("Invalid Info.plist value"). Removed from the iOS target; the Watch keeps `WKBackgroundModes: workout-processing`, and HealthKit still wakes the iPhone for a mirrored workout through `workoutSessionMirroringStartHandler`.
- **Next up chip on its own row** (#652, design A from `next-up-chip/proposals.png`): with another day picked on Train, the day card's jump chip ("Next up · Thu ›" / "Today ›") sat on its own line above "Sunday · planned". It now sits on that label's line, right aligned, one row shorter (rest and past-day cards already had it beside the title).
- **Spinners and blanks at launch and offline** (#641, audit after #639): the account, Community, Pro and MonkeyGrade climbs only existed once the server answered, so a cold launch (sleeping server) or no network showed a spinner in Settings (forever offline), no initials in the Me pill, a spinner or Retry on Community, Pro locked for a Pro member and MonkeyGrade climbs gone. The last answers are now kept on the device per account (`DeviceCache`, `Application Support/cache/`): account, Community profile and feeds (first 30 posts each), Pro entitlement, MonkeyGrade sessions. They show at once and are refreshed in the background; wiped on sign-out and account switch.
- **Profile photo took ~10 s to show at launch** (#639): the Me pill only knew your photo after `/me`, the community and public profile calls and the image download, one after the other, the first paying the server's cold start. Your photo is now kept on the device per account (`MeAvatar`, `me-avatar.jpg`) and shown at once; it is downloaded again only when its URL changes, forgotten when you remove it, and wiped on sign-out or account switch.
- **Past days on Train looked different and hid what was logged** (#592, design A from `past-day-cards/proposals.png`): picking an earlier day in the week strip showed one of three cards (last week: Log a workout / History; a done session: Done · see workout; a rest or climbing day: Log a workout / Log climb), and the rest/climbing card never read the logged climbs, so a climb logged on Tuesday didn't show and Log climb made a second one. Every past day now uses one **day log** card: everything logged that day as gradient rows (icon, detail, big time; gym → what you did, climb or sport logged here → **Edit**, Health sessions → their page), a missed session as a white **Log it** row, and **Workout** (log one of the plan's workouts on that day as an extra, the planned one stays as is) + **Sport** tiles (**Log climb** on a climbing day with no climb yet). **Long press** a row for Edit (or See / Log it) and **Delete**. Log climb and Log sport open in **edit mode** for a logged session: saved over itself (its Apple Health workout is replaced, not doubled) and **Delete**. `PastDayCard`, `DayLogRow`, `DayActionTileLabel`, `MissedLogSheet(day:)`; demo screen `train-past-day`.
- **Extra workout: Session flow had no Edit, and "Upper body" worked legs** (#590): a quick extra added from Add workout (Upper body, Push, Legs…) showed its Session flow (Strength / Mobility / Cardio) without Edit, since flow editing was only for plan sessions. Generated extras now open the same flow sheet (drag time between blocks, add or remove blocks, Back to plan flow); the extra is picked again for the new flow at the same length, this workout only. The quick **Upper body** tile used the full-body upper-emphasis day (hinges and lunges: Glutes, Quads, Hamstrings as main muscles); Upper body and Lower body now use the upper and lower days. Test `quickUpperAndLowerStayOnTheirHalf`.
- **Supplement editor kept the old type's values** (#572): switching Type kept the previous preset's name, serving and amounts (a "Meal shake" of Type Creatine with 40 g protein) and its time. The name, serving, amounts and time now follow the new type unless you changed them (`Supplement.switching(to:)`). Only the amounts that fit the type are shown. The grey form is replaced by gradient cards (no grey list).
- **Build as I go stuck after the first exercise** (#581): a Coach pick or More only opened the new exercise once the current one was done, so a cardio exercise whose timer wasn't run (Cross-trainer 10 min) held the workout and picks queued silently; skip was greyed out on the last exercise. Picks and More now open the picked exercise at once (Add still queues it in Up next), and skip on the last exercise opens the picker. X on a freestyle workout with nothing logged discards it (Undo brings it back) instead of keeping it paused, and Discard takes the workout off Train at once, so Freestyle no longer says resume while the Undo bar shows (Undo or opening it again puts it back). Freestyle workouts are named Freestyle, not by focus ("Full body · upper emphasis"). Test `namedFreestyleNotByFocus`. **Pick → do → pick layout** (design C from `freestyle-loop/proposals.png`): a **trail** under the top bar replaces "1/1" (done ✓ in lime, this exercise lime, queued ones dashed, tap to go there, + to queue one); the **Coach picks always hold Up next's place** and light up with a lime outline ("Pick next · Coach") once the exercise is done; 🔍 search takes skip's place; the lime button reads **FINISH WORKOUT** when nothing is queued. `FreestyleTrail`. Train's tile and the Workouts card are named **Freestyle** in both states (the tile read "Build as I go" once nothing was in progress).
- **Food scan recognition** (#573): a beer bottle scanned live came out as cheese. The label text "Appenzeller" was an alias of Hard cheese, there were no beers or other alcoholic drinks to match, and Open Food Facts products without protein or fat values (most beers and soft drinks) were dropped, so the barcode found nothing. Now: **barcode first** in Scan live (words read within 4 s of a barcode product are dropped); Open Food Facts products count missing values as 0, keep the brand in the name and stay **drinks** (🍺/🍷/🥃/🥤, alcohol flagged) from their categories; a label with an alcohol strength ("4,8% vol", "alc. 5 %") only matches drinks; region and brand words need the food word ("Appenzeller Käse"); "wine vinegar" is vinegar. **29 new foods**: beer, alcohol-free beer, red and white wine, cider, spirits, cola, cola zero, soft drinks, mineral water, energy drink, iced tea, smoothie, protein shake and pudding, soft cheese, cream, ice cream, salami, sausages, pizza, oven fries, fish fingers, croissant, biscuits, milk chocolate, flour, sugar, ketchup (more cheese names). **Snap the fridge**: the vision model (Claude Opus 5.5, medium effort) first identifies each item from labels and packaging, then gives a list id only when it is that same food; anything else comes back as a **new food** with its name, group and values per 100 g (kept on the device like a scanned barcode) instead of the nearest of the list; items it was unsure of start unticked. **Check list** (design A with B's guesses, `food-scan/proposals.png`) on Snap the fridge and Scan live: one gradient card per item, teal **sure** (Photo, Barcode), pink **check** ("Not sure" or "Read on a label", with **FIX**), violet **NEW** (kcal per 100 g); tap a card to leave it out. FIX opens **Or it's…**: three guesses (name words, then same group by closest values, `FoodBook.guesses`), a search over every food, and "Yes, it's …". `ScanCheckList`, `ScanFixSheet`, `ScanPick`; test `scanGuesses`. Server answers `found` (id or new food, `sure`, `alcohol`, `per100`) next to `items` for older apps. Tests `beerLabelIsBeer`, `openFoodFactsBeer`, `foodScan.test.ts`.
- **Just ate never empty** (#574, design A + C rows from `just-ate/proposals.png`): with no planned meal left today and no recent or favourite recipes, the sheet showed only a search field and four bare hand tiles at 0. It now opens on the **open meal nearest now** (big gradient card with what it should hold), with **6 ideas** for it (the planned recipe first, then recents that suit it, then fresh ideas), chips to switch to another open meal, **Today**: meals already ticked with **Undo**, and hand tiles named Protein, Carbs, Veg, Fats. Opens full height.
- **Meal plan end to end** (#569): tapping the plan opened a blank **Plan** sheet and it couldn't be deleted: for saves from before #558 the plan built from `planDays`/`mainOnly` got new ids on every read. It is now written down once on load (fixed ids as a fallback), so its dates stop moving with today and it edits and deletes. **Delete plan** also from a long press on the plan card; Undo brings back the plan and its planned meals. The basket counts the **days with meals** from today (was always 31) and only coming meals; the budget follows those days, and the planner spreads the weekly budget over days with meals only (it spread 31 days of budget over 5 days, picking expensive meals). Timeline shows the current month; last rows clear the Ask Coach bubble; with no plan in the coming 31 days Re-plan is replaced by a hint. Test `budgetCountsOnlyDaysWithMeals`.

### Security
- **Security audit 4 fixes** (audit + pen test 2026-10-29, report in #919): **AI moderation budget** (#920, High): moderation spent the shared `AI_DAILY_LIMIT`, so a member editing captions in a loop could drain Coach for everyone and then post unchecked text (moderation failed open). Moderation now has its own pool (`MODERATION_DAILY_LIMIT`, default 1000), 40 checks per member a day (then 429 `daily_limit`) and remembers clean texts. **Member reports as GitHub issues** (#921): title links replaced by `[link]`, member text fenced, banner marks it untrusted (prompt-injection guard for agents). **Achievement pushes** (#922): text with a link or domain → 400 `links_not_allowed`. **HSTS** (#925): `strict-transport-security: max-age=31536000` over HTTPS, plus `x-content-type-options: nosniff`, `referrer-policy: no-referrer`. **Hash queue** (#927): >64 waiting password hashes → 503 `busy`. **Unverified accounts** (#928): email accounts never verified are purged after 7 days. **Staging hardened** (#924): Pro test mode (fake purchases) only for accounts in `SUBSCRIPTION_TEST_EMAILS` (Fly secret from the GitHub secret, unset = nobody); other testers' fake purchases stop counting; staging `AI_DAILY_LIMIT` 150. **JPEG sanitising** (#929): bytes after EOI and APPn/COM between progressive scans dropped. Tests `securityAudit.test.ts`, `aiModeration.test.ts`, `reports.test.ts`.
- **Passwords hashed with Argon2id, backend on Node 24** (#762): CodeQL flagged password hashing; the flagged SHA-1 is only the Have I Been Pwned lookup key, but the stored hash, scrypt N=2¹⁵ p=1, was below the OWASP minimum. New passwords are hashed with **Argon2id** (32 MiB, 3 passes, PHC string `$argon2id$v=19$…`), built into Node 24, so the backend runtime moves from Node 22 to **Node 24** (Docker image, CI, `engines` ≥ 24.7, `@types/node` 24). Existing scrypt hashes still verify and are replaced with Argon2id at the member's next successful sign-in. Dependabot alerts on: transitive `fast-uri` and `ip-address` (moderate) bumped via `npm audit fix`. Backend `crypto.ts` (`needsRehash`), `auth.ts`; tests `crypto.test.ts`, `hardening.test.ts`.
- **Push token out of URLs** (#762, CodeQL `swift/cleartext-transmission`): signing out sent the APNs device token in the URL path (`DELETE /v1/me/push-device/<token>`), so it landed in request logs. The app now sends it in the body (`DELETE /v1/me/push-device {token}`); the old path stays for installed apps and is redacted in logs (`logUrl`). App `APIClient.unregisterPushDevice`; backend `routes/push.ts`; tests `push.test.ts`, `coachVoices.test.ts`.
- **CodeQL first scan triaged** (#762): GitHub code scanning (CodeQL default setup), secret scanning and push protection are on. First scan of `main`: the Community member search escaped only `_` in its nickname `LIKE` pattern (`js/incomplete-sanitization`, not reachable since the text is already cut to `[a-z0-9_.]`); it now escapes `\`, `%` and `_` like the programme and library searches. `js/insufficient-password-hash` on `hibp.ts` is a false positive (SHA-1 is the Have I Been Pwned range API format; passwords are stored with scrypt). New page [docs/security-alerts.md](docs/security-alerts.md): what is enabled, how alerts are captured, triaged and dismissed, and a scan log.

### Added
- **Skip today's workout without moving it** (#643, design A from `skip-today/proposals.png`): when you couldn't train, the only options were Move to… or Swap; Skip only existed for missed past days. Long press the Train day card or a day in the week strip (today or later this week) → **Skip today** / **Skip <day>**: done at once with the Undo bar. Next up, Coach Monkey and the week count move on to the next session; the strip shows a dim dash, the card *<day> · skipped* with **Unskip**; once the day is over it stays a skipped missed session. The card's long press also has Move to… and Plan a sport (later days). `AppModel.skipSession/unskip/canSkip`, `skipWithUndo`; demo screen `train-skipped`.
- **Effort and mood to Apple Health** (#614, designs A + B + C from `apple-tech-radar/batch3.html`): gym sessions didn't count in Apple's Training Load. The finish screen has a **How hard?** card (Apple effort 1–10, prefilled from the session's reps in reserve, saved on the Health workout when the summary closes) and **five mood faces** (🤩 Amazing added) saved as an Apple Health **State of Mind**. On the Watch the Done screen asks both: Digital Crown for effort, tap a face. Progress has a **Mood vs training** widget (last 10 workouts: effort bars, the face picked after each, usual feeling on the right; on the default board, in Add widget for saved boards) Members connected to Health before get one Health sheet for the two new types. `WorkoutEffort` (engine, tests), `HealthEffort`, `EffortCard`; effort kept in `state.json` (`efforts`).
- **Ring through Silent: rest and interval alarms** (#613, designs A + B + C, iOS 26 AlarmKit): end of rest rings like an alarm on iPhone and Watch, through Silent and Focus, with **Start set** and **+30 s** on the alarm; Tabata, EMOM and rounds ring at the end of each timed block. Off by default: one ask the first time a timer runs (Not now / Allow), toggle in Settings → Reminders & alerts. While on it replaces the rest-over notification. `TimerAlarms`, `AddRest30Intent`, `NSAlarmKitUsageDescription`.
- **Smart Stack cards that rise on their own** (#612, designs A + B + C): a new Watch **Smart Stack** widget with three moments: **Gym time** (30′ before your usual training time on a training day, and at your home gym), **Rest day** morning (easy day + water pace) and **After workout** (recap + protein for an hour). Relevance from entry scores and relevant intents (date windows, gym region); the home gym's position now goes to the Watch (`WatchSnapshot.gym`).
- **Live group sessions** (#626, designs B + C from `apple-tech-radar/batch4.html`; no SharePlay): **B** **Gym buddies**: Gym buddy on a member's page invites (push notification to the invitee, and back to the inviter once paired; tap opens the member's page), the other side accepts (Community → **Gym buddies** card lists invites and pairs). While either of you trains, your set goes to your buddy ("ANA · LIVE · Squat 70 kg × 8 ✓") and you send 🔥 🍌 🦍; buddies' finished sets and cheers for you pop up over the iPhone player and the Watch, with a buzz and cheer-back buttons on the Watch. **C** **Week challenge** room in Community: one kit-free **AMRAP 12′** for everyone all week (rotates each ISO week), START opens it on the big-screen timer, then rounds + reps go on a **live leaderboard** (best score stays, members training it now marked LIVE, joined and training counts). Backend `/v1/community/buddies`, `/v1/community/live`, `/v1/community/live/cheers`, `/v1/community/challenge` (+ `/score`), tables `community_buddies`, `community_live`, `community_live_cheers`, `community_challenge_scores`; blocks and leaving unpair; data export `live`. App `LiveRoom`, `LiveViews` (`BuddiesCard`, `BuddyButton`, `BuddyToast`, `ChallengeRoomView`), APIClient `Live.swift`, LiftSensor `WeeklyChallenge`; Watch `BuddyPingView`, `WBuddyPing`, `WatchEvent.buddyCheer`. Tests `live.test.ts`, `LiveTests`, `WeeklyChallengeTests`.
- **Life-stage programmes** (#625, designs A + B + C from `apple-tech-radar/batch4.html`): safe, coach-led programmes, no medical claims. **A** Explore → Programmes → **Life stages**: **Pregnancy** (trimester-aware strength), **Postpartum** (core + pelvic floor return), **50+** (balance · bone · power), **Back to training** (after injury or break); each page starts with a short **check-in** (due date, baby's birthday or weeks off) and a **doctor's OK** that unlocks Start. **B** **Safety guard**: while one runs, risky moves are swapped automatically and each workout says why ("Bench press → Incline DB press: 2nd trimester: no lying flat on your back for long"; no jumping in pregnancy, no impact or crunching in the first 16 weeks after birth, controlled power at 50+, no fast lifts after 4+ weeks off). **C** **Pace by feel**: every working set reads "Easy–medium · you can still talk", PR attempts are hidden in the player. Engine `LifeStage` (rules, `guarded`), `LifeStageCheckIn` (`SyncedProfile.lifeStage`), `Programme.lifeStages`; app `LifeStageSetup`, `AppModel+LifeStage`. Tests `LifeStageTests`.
- **Cycling with sensors** (#623, designs A + B + C from `apple-tech-radar/batch4.html`): **A** the Watch pairs Bluetooth **power meters, cadence sensors and heart-rate straps** (Road bike / MTB → **Sensors**, battery shown; also a page during the ride) and connects them for every ride; Settings → Watches → **Bike sensors** on the iPhone shows FTP, W/kg and the power zones. **B** With a power meter the ride screen becomes the **power player**: zone tile ("ZONE 3 · TEMPO"), watts big, 3″ average, cadence and heart rate. **C** **FTP test · 45′** on the Watch: 15′ warm-up, 20′ all out (live average), 10′ easy; 95 % of the 20′ average becomes the FTP on Watch and iPhone, zones follow; retest after 6 weeks. `BikePower` (zones, FTP, GATT packet parsing, cadence) in the LiftSensor package with tests `BikePowerTests`; Watch `BikeSensors`, `BikeSensorsPage`; iPhone `BikeSensorsView`; `WatchEvent.bikeFTP`, `WatchSnapshot.bikeFTP`, `SyncedProfile.bikeFTP`; Watch `NSBluetoothAlwaysUsageDescription`.
- **On-device Coach** (#608, designs A + B + C from `apple-tech-radar/t6.png`): **A** on iPhones with Apple Intelligence (iOS 26+), short Ask Coach questions ("How long rest for 5×5 squat?") are answered by Apple's on-device model: ⚡ On device badge with the time it took, works without signal, nothing sent, no daily limit; asking for a workout still goes to the server Coach, and the on-device Coach answers when that one is off, out of messages or unreachable. Settings → Ask Coach → *Quick answers on device*. **B** the finish screen opens with a **recap**: two lines (biggest gain vs the last time, missed sets or every set done) and a **Next time** tip, written on the iPhone the second the workout ends, reworded by the on-device model when there is one. **C** **Wrist Coach** on the Watch rest screen: "Too heavy?" after two failed sets → drop ~5 % with **Apply**, "Too light?" after two easy ones, stop one short after a fail. Engine `CoachRoute`, `WorkoutRecap`; app `OnDeviceCoach`, `OnDeviceBadge`, `RecapCard`; Watch `WristCoach`; tests `OnDeviceCoachTests`. The Watch uses rules, not the language model (no Watch model API to build against yet).
- **Small wins** (#617, all three from `apple-tech-radar/t15.png`): **A** voice logging with **SpeechAnalyzer** (iOS 26, older recognizer as fallback) and one-sentence sets: "eight at eighty" fills and logs the set (`VoiceCommand.set`, number words like "sixty two point five"). **B** **compass + waypoints** for hike, mountaineering and trail run on the Watch: heading, Mark waypoint, arrow and distance to it and back to the start. **C** **Visual Intelligence**: point the camera at a machine, MonkeyFit lists its exercises and opens the page (`MachineVisualQuery`, `MachineMatch`, `OpenExerciseIntent`); **TipKit** hints for hold-to-talk, voice logging and fixing a set. Tests `repsAndWeightInOneGo`, `numberWords`, `MachineMatchTests`.
- **iPhone + Apple Watch as one** (#603, #604, #605, #606, #607; designs from `apple-tech-radar/`):
  - **Watch ↔ iPhone mirroring** (#603, A + C): a workout run on the Watch shows live on the iPhone in a **twin player** (set, kg × reps, −/+, the lime button, rest with +15 s / Skip, heart rate, calories); log on either, the other follows. Closing it leaves a **Live on Watch** banner on Train. **Move to Watch** in the player's workout map wakes the Watch to take an iPhone workout over; its mirrored session keeps the phone answering with the screen off. `MirroredWorkout`, `TwinPlayerView`, `WatchLiveBanner`, `WMirrorState`, `WCommand.kgDown/kgUp`; background mode `workout-processing`.
  - **Double tap everywhere on the Watch** (#604, B + C): the pinch presses the screen's lime button (log set, start, next, Skip rest, the iPhone workout's button, LAP, Hyrox DONE), marked with a small hand; the Crown sets the reps first.
  - **Siri and Shortcuts** (#605, A + B + C): Start today, Log set ("log 8 at 80"), Add water, Just ate, Best lift (answer card), Ask Coach as App Shortcuts; exercises in Spotlight; the exercise page tells Siri what's open.
  - **Workout in the Watch Smart Stack** (#606, A + B): an iPhone workout shows on the wrist with Log ✓ during a set and the rest ring while resting.
  - **Controls** (#607, A + B + C): Start today, Smart next, +1 glass, Just ate for Control Center, the Lock Screen and the iPhone Action button.
- **Calisthenics skill tree** (#621, designs A + B + C from `apple-tech-radar/batch4.html`): bodyweight skills with levels for home, park and travel. **A** Explore → Programmes → **Skill tree**: four branches (**Pull** dead hang → negative pull-up → pull-up → archer pull-up → muscle-up; **Push** knee push-up → push-up → pike push-up → wall HSPU → freestanding HSPU; **Legs** squat → split squat → box pistol → pistol; **Balance & core** plank → hollow hold → L-sit → tuck front lever → front lever) as square nodes. **B** Each node opens its **level test** (max reps with − / +, or a hold timer) and the target that unlocks the next ("2 more to unlock L3"); the best test is kept. **C** **Skill of the month**: pick one skill and Train shows a 10′ practice bar on Monday, Wednesday and Friday with the month's days ("12/13"), a chest when done. Rep counting by the Watch lift sensor is not part of this change (the test counts on the phone). Engine `Skills`, `SkillProgress` (`SyncedProfile.skills`); app `SkillTreeCard`, `SkillTreeView`, `SkillNodeSheet`, `SkillPracticeBar`. Tests `SkillTests`.
- **Sport season blocks** (#620, designs A + B + C from `apple-tech-radar/batch4.html`): gym plans that make members better at their sport. **A** Explore → Programmes opens with **Get ready for…**: Ski, Padel, Climb, Run, Surf, Football tiles open Snow ready, Climber's power or one of four new 8-week season programmes (**Padel ready**, **Run ready**, **Surf ready**, **Match ready**, each with two signature workouts); their page asks when the season starts and fits Base and Build to it (5–12 weeks). **B** When a sport was logged 3+ times in 14 days (sports and climbs), the Coach offers its add-on in the notice slot ("Coach · you played padel 3× · Add padel add-on?" **Add 2× week** / **No**, hidden 30 days). **C** **Sport add-on**: 15′ of sport work (padel: rotator cuff, wrist, lateral lunges; climb: antagonists, finger care; …) added to the week's first 1–3 strength sessions, plan otherwise unchanged; set or removed under the tiles. Engine `SeasonSport`, `Programme+Season`, `SportAddOn`, `SportAddOnPref` (`SyncedProfile.sportAddOn`); app `SeasonPickerCard`, `SportAddOnRow`, `SportAddOnSuggestCard`. Tests `SeasonBlockTests`.
- **Mobility & recovery days** (#619, designs A + B + C from `apple-tech-radar/x2.png`): rest days were empty. **A** Today's and coming rest days on Train show a **Recover** bar: a ~12′ mat routine (mobility drills, then holds) for the area trained hardest in the last 7 days (**Hips & back**, **Legs**, **Shoulders & chest**, or **Full body** when nothing stands out) with why ("Legs hit hard Mon + Wed"); it opens the routine's page with Start. **B** On a "Take it easier" readiness day (2+ markers low) with a strength session not started, a **Readiness swap** card ("Readiness low · 5H sleep · Swap Legs for mobility?") moves the session to the next free day this week (**Swap**) so today becomes a recover day, or stays away until tomorrow (**Keep**). **C** Example, own and shared workouts without a cool-down get a 5′ **Cool-down** finisher (4 mat stretches for what they loaded most); on Apple Watch every cool-down is **guided**: timers start on their own, per-side holds run left then right with a haptic at the switch, "Cool-down · 2 of 4". Engine `Recovery` (routines `recover-*`, `area`, `hardDays`, `withFinisher`); app `AppModel+Recovery`, `RecoverBar`, `RecoverSwapCard`; Watch `WBlock.guided`. Tests `RecoveryTests`.
- **Motion sensing, timers and swimming** (Apple tech radar #602; #611, #615, #622, #624; designs A + B + C of `apple-tech-radar/t9.png`, `t13.png`, `x5.png`, `x7.png`). **High-rate motion (#611)**: the Watch's batched sensors (`CMBatchedSensorManager`, 800 Hz accelerometer, 200 Hz device motion, inside a workout session) count **racket and club swings** (padel, tennis and new **Golf** open on FH / BH / SMASH tiles with the top speed, `SwingCounter`) and **punches** per round (`PunchCounter`); lift sets get a **rep speed graph** (bar height = rep speed, red past the stop limit, still buzzing at −20 %). **WOD and combat timers (#622)**: one engine `WodTimer` (Tabata, EMOM, AMRAP, For time, Rounds) in `LiftSensor`; iPhone **Timers** in the Workout library (kind tiles, builder with moves, saved timers with Undo delete), **Boxing rounds** (6 × 3′ / 1′, bell, clap at 10″, punches counted on the Watch), **big screen mode** (full screen, sideways, screen kept on, sound + haptics) that the Watch's **double tap** starts and pauses; Watch **Timers** row with square tiles and the iPhone's saved timers, run in a boxing / HIIT Health workout. **Swimming (#615, #624)**: Watch **Swim** (pool 25 / 50 m, open water, snorkel) with **Water Lock**, laps, stroke, pace per 100 m, SWOLF; **swim sets** with send-offs buzzed on the wrist (`SwimWorkout`); open water route, water temperature and an iPhone **buddy alert** if the swim runs past the set time; **depth log** (depth, max, time under water) on Apple Watch Ultra (Snorkel, up to 6 m; Shallow Depth and Pressure entitlement, #634). iPhone **Swim**: 8-week plans (Learn to swim, 1 km non-stop, 2 km) on days without lifting ending with a non-stop test (`SwimPlan`), sets sheet, Start on Watch, **Pool + gym** swim warm-up or finisher on a gym day (also on the session page). Stores `WodStore` (`wod-timers.json`), `SwimStore` (`swim.json`), on the device. Tests `MotionTimerSwimTests`; Watch demo screens `timers`, `boxing`, `swings`, `swim`, `swim-sets`, `open-water`.
- **iPhone Duo postures** (#598, designs A + B + C from `iphone-duo/postures/postures.png`): the workout player follows how the Duo is held. **Flex** (half open on a table, landscape): top half shows the exercise card and its picture, bottom half the input, controls and Up next, nothing on the hinge. **Book** (half open, portrait): the cockpit layout with one page each side of the hinge. **Tent** (half open, portrait, stood on the floor: still for 3 s): one huge number readable from 2 m (rest, timer or this set's reps × kg), what's next and the controls. Detection: iOS 27.1 `UIHingeInteraction` (partially open) + orientation, and device motion for Tent since iOS has no tent status (`DuoPosture`, `.duoPosture($posture)`); other iPhones always use the flat layouts. DEBUG `-duoPosture flex|book|tent` forces one for CI screenshots.
- **Meal replacements and supplements in meals** (#599, designs A + B + C from `meal-replacements/proposals.png`): a Huel or yfood could only be added in Supplements with a schedule, and Just ate and the meal plan only knew recipes. **A** Just ate has a **Mine** row under the meal card: your meal shakes and hot meals fill that meal in one tap, other supplements tick today's take (or count as an extra take), **+** adds one; search finds them too. **B** **From a brand**: Huel (Black Edition, Powder, Ready-to-drink, Black Ready-to-drink, Hot & Savoury, Complete Protein), yfood, Saturo, Jimmy Joy, Soylent, KaEx, Myprotein, ESN with their variants, typical label values (≈, scan the label to correct) and a serving size (½, 1, 1½, 2). **C** a planned meal's **Swap** has **Recipes / Mine**: plan a shake for this meal or every planned meal of that kind from that day. New option **Only when I pick it** (no schedule, `Supplement.onDemand`); products picked from Just ate or the plan start that way. A meal product is a recipe `product-<id>` (`Supplement.recipe`), so ticks, plan cards and Ate it work as for recipes; deleting it takes it out of the plan. Engine `ProductCatalog`, `Supplements.extra`; views `ProductCatalogSheet`, `MineStrip`; tests in `FuelTests`.
- **Over-the-air updates** (#583, scope "Standard", designs A + B + C from `ota-updates/proposals.png`): fixes and content can ship from the backend without a new build. App Review 2.5.2 allows data only, so code still needs a build. `GET /v1/app-config` (public, ETag/304, optional Ed25519 `X-Content-Signature` with `CONTENT_SIGNING_KEY`) serves **feature flags / kill switches** (build range, % rollout per install, paused text; `foodScan`, `gymScan`, `askCoach`), **remote values**, **minimum and recommended build**, and **content packs** listed by SHA-256 (`/v1/packs/<name>/<sha256>`, immutable): `recipes`, `foods`, `whats-new`, `coach-tips`. Everything lives in `backend/content/` (checked by `remote.test.ts`), so publishing is PR → staging → Promote to production, same image. App: fetched at launch and on foreground (every 5 min at most), kept on disk for offline; packs are hash-checked, used from the next launch, dropped when the app updates, bundled file whenever a pack is missing or doesn't decode. **A** update banner on Train (Later per build), **B** Update needed screen over the app (builds below `minimumBuild`, opens TestFlight or the config's link), **C** Paused card in Food photo scan, Gym scan and Ask Coach. "Content signing key" workflow makes the key (Fly secrets) and prints `CONTENT_PUBLIC_KEY` for the xcconfigs. Engine `RemoteConfig`, `ContentPacks`; app `Remote`, `UpdateBanner`, `RequiredUpdateView`, `PausedCard`; tests `RemoteConfigTests`; demo screens `remote-banner`, `remote-required`, `remote-paused`.
- **Complete documentation** (#586): `docs/` now covers the architecture (Mermaid diagrams), every iPhone tab and screen, the Apple Watch, widgets, Live Activity, complications, Garmin and other watches, on-device data, every API route (method, path, auth, body, response, errors, limits) with conventions and the sync protocol, the SQLite data model, security and data protection with a threat model (secret names only), infrastructure, CI/CD and release flow, legal pages, local development and contribution rules; the training methodology moved in. `docs/reference/` (`routes.json`, `schema.sql`, `openapi.json`) is generated by `npm run docs` (`backend/src/docsReference.ts`; `buildApp` takes an `onRoute` hook), and `backend/test/docs.test.ts` fails when a route is missing from `docs/api/endpoints.md` or the references are stale. Endpoint and security sections of `backend/README.md` moved to `docs/`.
- **Supplement schedule** (#572, designs A + C from `supplement-schedule/`): a supplement had one moment of the day and "Also on rest days". The editor now has **quick plans** (Daily, Twice a day, Training days, Load then daily, Cycle 8 on / 4 off, My own) with the plan in one sentence, and a **rhythm builder**: how often (every day, training days, picked weekdays, every N days), **times a day** (each at a moment or a set clock time, with servings per take), **course** (ongoing, N weeks, until a date, start date, week squares), **cycle on/off**, **loading phase** (2–4× a day for N days) and **reminders at each time**. Each take shows on the Day plate and Timeline with its own tick (first take keeps the old tick key), caffeine, protein and creatine scale with servings, My stack shows the schedule and course, Ask Coach knows the schedules. Older saves keep working (`timing` + `restDays`). Engine `SupplementRhythm`; tests in `SupplementTests`.
- **More fun Community** (#566, designs A + C from `community-fun/proposals.png`): every shared workout was the same blue card (its colour came from the category with most sets, nearly always strength) with faded cheers. Workout cards now take the colour of their focus, read from the title (**Legs** orange, **Chest/Push** pink, **Pull/Back** teal, Arms, Shoulders, Core; sports keep theirs, `WorkoutFocus`). **Coach Monkey** adds a silly line under workouts and climbs ("9.1 t = 1.5 elephants lifted. Banana earned.", `CoachQuip`). Cheers are bold square tiles with an emoji burst when you cheer, plus two new ones, **🦍 Beast** and **🍌 Banana** (server: reaction kinds `beast`, `banana`; the `community_reactions` CHECK is rebuilt once, cheers kept; test in `community.test.ts`). **Trained today**: square tiles of members who shared a workout, sport or climb today, tap opens the member (`TrainedTodayRow`). **Moment cards**: personal bests (gold, NEW PR 🏆), streak milestones (fire, STREAK 🔥) and level ups (purple, LEVEL UP), style `moment:pr|streak|level`; auto-share now also posts the day's biggest new best (not first lifts), a streak reaching 2, 3, 4, 6, 8… weeks and a new level (`WinCard.moments`). Header unchanged.
- **Fuel: next planned meal and Just ate** (#565, designs A + B from `meal-plan-pattern/proposals4.png`): with a meal plan, the Day look's "Your plate" still showed random ideas, and logging a meal meant the Timeline tick or a recipe's "I ate something like this". The card now shows the **next planned recipe** big (**Ate it** · **Swap** · **Other**, or **Eaten · Undo**) and the planned meal after it (**Then**); without a plan it stays as it was. A lime **+** on Fuel (on the side the Ask Coach bubble isn't on) opens **Just ate**: today's planned meals not ticked yet, recent and favourite recipes, a search, or portions by hand (✋ 🤲 ✊ 👍, tap to add, hold to take one off). A recipe goes into the open meal nearest now that suits it. `PlannedPlateCard`, `ThenMealCard`, `JustAteButton`, `JustAteSheet`.
- **Meal plan: named plans, meals per day, dates, repeat** (#558, designs A + B + C from `meal-plan-pattern/proposals3.png`, blocks C1 from `proposals2.png`): the plan was 3, 5 or 7 days of one choice (Lunch & dinner or Every meal). Now the member keeps **named plans** (My plans). Each plan is **blocks one after the other** (up to 31 days), each a big preset card (**Every meal**, **Lunch & dinner**, **Dinners only**, **Weekday dinners, weekend L+D**) or a **Custom** week (Breakfast / Lunch / Dinner per weekday, snacks on or off), with its length; the cycle starts again back to back or **every 1–8 weeks**. **When**: one or more date ranges per plan (from → until, or ongoing); where plans overlap, the range that starts last wins; days no plan covers get nothing planned. The Meal plan screen shows **timeline lanes** (a lane per plan over the coming 3 months, today marked), the plan **active today**, the other plans with their date tags and **New plan**. **Delete plan** in the plan editor is instant with Undo. The planner fills the next 31 days and tops itself up a week ahead when Fuel opens. Basket, budget and batch dinners follow the plan: a batch dinner only becomes next day's lunch when that day has lunch. Older saves become "My plan" with their length and choice. Engine `MealPattern`, `MealBlock`, `MealSchedule`, `NamedMealPlan`, `MealPeriod`, `MealPlanBook`, `MealPlanner.plan(daySlots:)`; views `MyPlansCard`, `PlanLanes`, `MealPlanEditor`, `MealBlockSheet`; tests in `FuelTests`.
- **MonkeyWorkout for Garmin watches** (#550, design B from `other-watches/garmin-proposals.png`): a Connect IQ watch app (`garmin/`) for Forerunner 255/265/955/965, fēnix 7, epix 2, Venu 3 and vívoactive 5, in Garmin's own black data-field look. **Home**: next session (exercises, time) with START, today's water; **set screen**: kg x reps (Up/Down, menu → Change kg), heart rate, LOG SET; **rest**: green ring draining around the bezel, +15 s, skip, buzz at the end; **glance**: next session and water; menu: Water +1, Refresh, Unpair. It records a Garmin strength activity (heart rate, calories, a lap per set) that Garmin Connect adds to Apple Health. No phone link of its own: it talks to the server through the Garmin Connect app. **Pairing**: the watch shows a 6-letter code, typed in Settings › Watches › **Garmin watch** (`GarminView`; the chapter is now "Watches"). The iPhone publishes what the watch shows and picks up its sets, finished workouts and water when it opens (`GarminSync`, recorded like Apple Watch events, no second Health workout). Server: `/v1/garmin/pair`, `/v1/garmin/snapshot`, `/v1/garmin/events` (`X-Garmin-Token`), `/v1/me/garmin/claim|devices|snapshot|inbox` (tables `garmin_devices`, `garmin_snapshots`, `garmin_inbox`, removed with the account); tests `garmin.test.ts`. CI workflow `garmin.yml` builds the app once the Garmin secrets are set.
- **Any watch: workouts from Garmin, Polar, Coros, Suunto… mark today's session done** (#549, design A from `other-watches/health-proposals.png`): a gym workout recorded on another watch reached Apple Health but not the app, so the session stayed undone. Train's session card now shows **Found in Health · \<watch\>** with length, average and max heart rate and calories when another app wrote a strength, functional, HIIT or core workout on that session's day, with **Mark done** (every planned set, the watch's times, heart rate and calories) and **Log sets** (the Log workout sheet prefilled with the watch's times). Our own app and Watch workouts are left out, and a workout already in History within 30 minutes is not offered again. `HealthSnapshot.recentGym`, `GymEntry`, `AppModel.healthMatch/markDone(_:from:)/logFromHealth`, `HealthMatchBox`.
- **Sport prep on sport days** (#523, design A from `climb-row/sport-proposals.png`): climbing had its Wall session, other sports nothing. On a planned sport day (weekly day, one-off day or trip) Train shows a **\<Sport\> prep** bar under Next up, in the sport's colours. It opens a page like the Wall session: **Before** (no-kit warm-up, 6-7 moves), the sport (Track live or Log it), **After** (stretch). One pair per family: snow, ride, run & hike, water, court, combat, general. Engine `SportPrep`; tests `SportPrepTests`.
- **Water reminders, one-tap water widgets and a pace that follows your training** (#520, designs A + B + C from `nutrition-pointers/water-reminders.png`). **Pace**: the day's glasses spread from waking to sleep, more in the 2 h before a session and the hour after, less in the last 2 h before bed (`WaterPace`). **Smart nudges**: a reminder only when you're behind that pace (or every 2 h / every hour), never during workouts, sleep or notification quiet hours, with **+1 glass / +2 / Later** buttons on the banner and the Apple Watch; rebuilt each time a glass is counted. **Widgets**: a Water widget (Home Screen small and medium, Lock Screen circle and line) with a +1 button that counts without opening the app (`AddWaterIntent`, glasses wait in the App Group `WaterQueue` until the app is active), and a Water complication on the Watch face. **Fuel**: the water card shows "On pace" or "Behind" and opens the Water sheet with today's pace chart, Remind me and Quiet settings. Tests `WaterPaceTests`; demo screen `fuel-water`.

### Changed
- **Less climbing-only wording** (#658): sign in, sign up and welcome say *sports* instead of *climbs*/*climbing*, rest-day lines say *Rest or play your sport*.
- **Liquid Glass on iOS 26** (#616, designs A + C from `apple-tech-radar/batch3.html`): the app kept the iOS 18 look. On iOS 26 the tab bar is the floating glass bar and tucks away while scrolling down (`glassTabBar`); sheets show their glass edges (Just ate, Change exercise, Superset fix, Not here, Zone, Missed, Programme, Sport season, workout map; `glassSheetBackground`). Gradient cards, the player and its solid lime Log are unchanged; iOS 18 keeps the solid tab bar and sheets.
- **What's new 2026-10-22.3** (build 62, Apple tech radar #602): 15 cards incl. skip today (#644) and Next up chip (#653): iPhone and Watch as one (#603–#607), on-device Coach (#608), voice/compass/camera (#617), zones and race plans (#609, #618, #610), Health/alarms/Smart Stack (#612–#614), swim/box/timers (#611, #615, #622, #624), cycling sensors (#623), recovery days (#619), sport seasons (#620), skill tree (#621), life-stage programmes (#625), gym buddies (#626), Liquid Glass + iOS 18 minimum (#616); 2026-10-22.2 moved to history as build 61.
- **Minimum iPhone and Watch versions raised to iOS 18 and watchOS 11** (#602): needed for the Apple tech roadmap (Controls, Live Activity in the Watch Smart Stack, Siri and Shortcuts entities, double tap). iOS 17 / watchOS 10 version checks removed (`sidebarAdaptableTabs`, StoreKit renewal info, Hyrox double tap). Packages `GymCore` and `LiftSensor` follow (`LiftSensor` moves to swift-tools-version 6.0, Swift 5 language mode, for the `.v18` / `.v11` platform values).
- **What's new 2026-10-22.2** (build 61): past days one look (#593), your shakes one tap (#600), iPhone Duo outer screen (#596) and postures (#601), opens instantly offline (#640); 2026-10-22.1 moved to history as build 60.
- **iPhone Duo outer screen** (#594, design A from `iphone-duo/proposals.png`): on the folded Duo (466×678 pt) iOS keeps a column on the right for the status bar and the tab rail. The floating Coach 🐵 sat over the Train week strip; it now docks in that column on top of the tab rail (`AskCoachBubble`). The player was too short for its picture: under 720 pt high the exercise name and target share one line, the note line goes, and the timer ring is smaller with no hint, so the movement shows about twice as tall (`ExerciseHeroCard.short`, `TimedSetCard.short`).
- **What's new 2026-10-22.1** (build 60): fixes without waiting (#583), extra workouts flow edit and upper/lower (#590), full screen on iPhone Duo (#588); 2026-10-21.4 moved to history as build 59.
- **iOS builds use Xcode 27.1 for iPhone Duo** (#158): CI, TestFlight, App Store screenshots and symbolication ran on `macos-26` with Xcode 26.6, so the unfolded iPhone Duo showed the app in compatibility mode. Those jobs now run on GitHub's `xcode-27` runner with Xcode 27.1 pinned (iOS 27.1 SDK; `Xcode_27.2.app` there is a beta). **App Store screenshots** downloads the iOS 27.1 simulator runtime, captures every Duo display and files them by size (`iphone-duo-outer` 1398×2034, `iphone-duo-inner` 2007×2853); 6.9"/6.3" captures use iPhone 18 Pro Max / 18 Pro. iOS CI also builds the TestFlight scheme for device (unsigned), so device-only code and SDK breaks show before an archive run. The workout player's view body is split in three (`screen`, `lifecycle`, `remoteControls`): Xcode 27.1 timed out type-checking it as one chain.
- **Merged PRs close all their issues**: GitHub only auto-closed the first number in "Closes #531, #532, …", so 14 shipped issues stayed open. New workflow `close-linked-issues.yml` closes every issue listed after Closes/Fixes/Resolves when a PR merges to main, with a comment linking the PR. PR bodies also write the keyword before each number.
- **What's new 2026-10-21.4** (build 59): one profile (#578), food scan (#573), supplement schedule (#577), Freestyle build as you go (#581); 2026-10-21.3 moved to history as build 58.
- **One profile** (#578, design A from `one-profile/proposals.png`): the Me sheet had **Edit profile** and **Community profile**, and the community page only linked back to the edit page. Now one **Your profile** row opens one page: photo, name and @nickname, then **Shared · Cheers · Followers** tiles (members), About, Coach & pronouns, and Community (visibility, auto-share, your gym, **Blocked members**, **Community guidelines**, **Leave the community**). `CommunityProfileView` removed; demo screen `community-profile` shows `ProfileView`.
- **What's new 2026-10-21.3** (build 58): Just ate, smarter (#574); 2026-10-21.2 moved to history as build 57.
- **What's new 2026-10-21.2** (build 57): meal plans fixed (#569); 2026-10-21.1 moved to history as build 56.
- **What's new 2026-10-21.1** (build 56): meal plans your way (#558), Just ate (#565), Train tidier (#562, #564), one button for you (#560), Community got fun (#566); 2026-10-20.4 moved to history as build 55.
- **One header on every tab: the Me pill** (#560, design B from `header-menu/proposals.png`): the top right differed per tab (LV chip only on Train, buttons 36 to 44 pt, circles and squares, and Community showed two avatars, photo for the profile and the "J" gear for Settings). Every tab now ends its header with one gradient **Me pill**: level and your square profile photo (initials when there is none). It opens the **Me sheet**: photo, name, @nickname and level bar (opens your profile), Levels, Edit profile, Community profile (members) and Settings. Tab actions sit before it as 44 pt rounded squares (`HeaderTile`): Train plan menu and widgets, Progress widgets, Fuel settings, Community lime Share a win. `MePill`, `MePhoto`, `MeSheet`, `AppModel.meOpen`; `SettingsAvatar` and the header `LevelChip` removed.
- **Coach Monkey and week card in one** (#564, design D1 from `train-day-card/coach-proposals.png`): when both are on Train (wide), the week + readiness card becomes three equal tiles inside the Coach Monkey card: **This week** (n/N done → Calendar), **phase** (W2/4 with the cycle bars → phase sheet) and **Ready** (verdict + HRV/RHR → readiness sheet or today's tune; Connect Health when not linked). The "n/N this week" chip goes, the tile shows it. Separate cards again while editing Train. `WeekTiles`, `MonkeyCoachCard.weekFooter`.
- **Train day card, one coherent layout** (#562, design B from `train-day-card/proposals.png`): the rest day card lost its "Nothing planned. Train or climb?" line and dashed box. The day's title now has a lime jump chip on its right (**Thu · Push ›**, **Next up · Thu ›** or **Today ›**) instead of the bottom "Back to…" bar, and the actions are equal Explore-style tiles (icon tile left): **Add workout | Plan sport** on one row (Log climb joins on a climbing day), then Build as I go / Log sport / Track live. Planned later days use the same tiles (**Plan sport | Move**), past days **Log a workout | History**. `DayActionTile`, `DayJumpChip`; `LogClimbButton` and `BackToNextUpButton` removed.
- **What's new 2026-10-20.4**: third card "Buttons say what they do" for #553/#556 (build 55).
- **Garmin signing key workflow** (#550): `garmin-key.yml` (run by hand) makes the Connect IQ developer key and saves it straight into the `GARMIN_DEVELOPER_KEY` secret (public repository: never in an artifact or log; needs a one-time `GARMIN_SETUP_TOKEN`; keeps an existing key).
- **What's new 2026-10-20.4** (build 55): any watch counts (#549), Garmin is coming (#550); 2026-10-20.3 moved to history as build 54.
- **What's new 2026-10-20.3** (build 54): Train opens on today (#547), away days in their week (#543), clear log times (#545); 2026-10-20.2 moved to history as build 53.
- **Calendar: away periods inside their week** (#543, design C from `calendar-away-cards/proposals.png`): an AWAY section above the weeks repeated every trip and can't-train day the week cards already show. The section is gone; each week card's away note is now a tappable gradient strip ("Gran Canaria · Bodyweight only · 1–8 Nov ›") that opens the away editor (`AwayStrip`). Periods starting after the last week shown keep a strip under **Later**.
- **What's new 2026-10-20.2** (build 53): smoother everywhere (#535, #536), Train opens on today (#538), only today starts (#537), step out with Undo (#538), rest in its place (#538), Undo instead of confirm (#539), easier to read (#540); 2026-10-20.1 moved to history as build 52.
- **Only today's session starts** (#537, design A from `full-app-audit/mockups/today-only-start.png`): Start workout showed on any day picked in the week strip, the session page, the Coach card and the Home Screen widgets, so a Thursday session could be played on Monday and logged on the wrong day. Start now shows only for today's session (or one without a weekday, or one already in progress, which always resumes).
