A later day shows a dashed lime **Planned for Thu** badge in Start's place (`PlannedDayBadge`); an earlier day not done shows **Log it** (logged on its own day, `MissedLogSheet`); a past day in the strip offers **Log a workout for this day** (18:00) beside See in History. The session page numbers its exercises in rounded squares in the block's colour: lime ✓ done, red – skipped. The Coach card opens another day's session page instead of starting it; the Home Screen widget's **Start** opens today's session straight in the player (`monkeyworkout://start/<id>`, `PlayerLaunch`) and says **Open** for another day. `AppModel.startableToday`.
- **Train first screen: one slot for passing cards; player closes with X and Undo** (#538, design A from `full-app-audit/mockups/train-first-screen.png` and `full-app-audit/mockups/player-close-rest.png`): the variation Undo, taste recap, block review, what Monkey noticed and kind streak cards stacked above Next up and pushed Start workout off the first screen. They now share **one rotating slot under Next up** (bars to page, swipe for the next, ✕ puts one away; `TrainNoticeSlot`), and the default layout puts Next up right under the coach card. In the player the pause button opened a 5-choice dialog; now an **X closes at once** (the workout stays paused, Undo bar reopens it), the pause button and the progress dots open the **workout map** with **Finish**, **Discard** (Undo for a few seconds, `PendingDiscard`) and **Finish early** at the bottom, and an empty Build as I go workout can be kept or discarded there. **Rest takes the set card's slot** upright and in landscape (`RestSlotCard`: big timer, square bar, +15 s, optional effort; SKIP REST in the controls), so Up next and the controls never move; VoiceOver hears 10 s and 3 s left. The summary's **DONE** is pinned under the scroll. **Watch**: rest is one fixed screen (rest tile in the block's colour with +15 and Skip, a draining bar, effort or what's next), the controls page fits without scrolling, and Discard counts down 5 s with **UNDO** instead of a dialog. Removing an added session from this week is instant with Undo too.
- **Sheet chrome, gradient rows, auto-save Retry and instant deletes** (#539, all designs from `full-app-audit/mockups/sheet-chrome.png`): sheets mixed Done, Cancel, Close and Save in both corners, titles sat under the toolbar's glass buttons and settings lists were grey rows. Every sheet now closes with the **X top right**; a sheet that creates something has a lime **Save** beside it (`SaveToolbarItem`), one that edits something saved saves on its own. Big titles are drawn in the content (`ContentTitle`, `titleInContent`). Customise Train and Progress, Edit exercises, Sources, Blocked members, Report, recipe editor and more use Explore-style **gradient rows** with square toggles (`gradientRow`, `darkCardRow`, `SquareToggle`). A save that fails turns the Saved pill into a red **Couldn't save · Retry** (`.autoSave` now takes an `async throws` save); devices, Coach status, resend code and blocked members show an error card with **Retry** (`InlineErrorCard`). **Deletes are instant with Undo** (`UndoCloseCenter.deleted`): recipes, custom workouts and exercises, programmes, measurements, Train and Progress widgets, and **Undo workout** in History and on the session page (the record, its sets and the done marks come back; the server delete waits for the bar, `AppModel.restoreWorkout`). A Training profile section edit says **REBUILD PLAN**.
- **Readable everywhere: Dynamic Type, dark ink on light gradients, labels, 44 pt** (#540, design A from `full-app-audit/mockups/accessibility.png`): the heavy rounded type was a fixed size, and white text sat on the light warm-up and stretch gradients. `Theme.display`/`label` now grow with the text size (capped so tiles hold; the app stops at accessibility size 3), and type on light gradients is dark (`Category.ink`/`isLight`, `ink:` on `gradientCard`, `GradientSection`, `GradientStat`, `IconTile`, `CategoryTile`, `AccountHeader`/`AccountCard`; the player's warm-up and stretch cards; the Watch rest tile). Icon-only buttons got labels, and small controls (player top bar, Train header, steppers, Coach send and mic, Live Activity ± kg, notice ✕) a 44 pt target.
- **Polish: rounded squares, Watch counts, haptics, iPad width** (#541, all designs from `full-app-audit/mockups/polish.png`): icon buttons, numbered tiles, badges, the week done ring and the rest and countdown rings were circles in an app of square cards; they are rounded squares, and progress rings have square ends (`ProgressRing`, segmented `ProgressBar` for the streak week). The Watch home rows show real counts (PRs, sports, styles) instead of "›", and no plan synced yet is a gradient card that says where to build it. The same moments feel the same everywhere (`Haptics`: start a workout, saved, Undo, delete or failed save). Sheets and pages that stretched across the iPad keep a readable column (`readableWidth`).
- **Fuel tools within reach** (#525, designs A + C from `fuel-tools/proposals.png`): What I have, Recipes, Meal plan, Groceries, Fuel week and Learn were six big cards at the very bottom, under the tab bar. They are now one slim row of square tiles under the date bar, with live counts (`FuelToolRow`). The next meal's card (Day and Timeline) offers **From what I have**, **Other recipes** and, for a planned meal with food you don't have, **N missing · Groceries** (`FuelSlotActions`); on the Timeline it gets a lime outline.
- **What's new 2026-10-20.1** (build 52): Explore two-level menu (#516), exercise jump bar (#518), Fuel date bar slides (#510), What I have one box (#511), supplements and shakes (#514), Wall session on climbing days (#521), Sport prep (#523), Fuel tools at hand (#525), water reminders (#520); 2026-10-19.3 moved to history as build 50.
- **Train climbing row slimmed: Wall session on climbing days only** (#521, design B from `climb-row/proposals.png`): Log climb and Around climbing days under Next up repeated Log sport → Climbing and Settings. The row is gone; on a climbing day one full-width **Wall session** bar (warm-up · hangboard · cool-down) shows instead, and the hard-climb note stays. No climbing day and no hard climb: the widget takes no space. MonkeyGrade climbs now refresh from Train itself.
- **Explore: two-level menu and Muscles split hero** (#516, layout C from `explore-muscles-page/opt-c.png`, menu 3 from `explore-muscles-page/menu-m3.png`): the menu showed only the picked section's name, so members guessed what the four icons meant, and Muscles opened on a 540 pt grey map panel with capsule pills, unlike the gradient cards of the other tabs. The menu is now an **Exercises | Ready-made** switch with labelled square tiles under it (**By muscle / By machine / By type**, or **Workouts / Plans**); each side remembers its last tile. Muscles opens on a gradient hero: the figure on the left (2D or 3D, square Front / Side / Back and 2D / 3D switches), this week's **Most worked** (top 3 by sets) and **Needs work** on the right; tapping a muscle on the figure swaps the right column for its card with **See exercises**. Square gradient group tiles under the machine chips jump to that group's cards; machine chips are square. `ExploreMenu`, `MuscleHero`, `MuscleGroupJump`, `BodyMapFacing` (replaces `BodyMap3DPanel`).
- **Exercise page: sticky jump bar** (#518, design A from `exercise-page-nav/proposals.png`): the page was about 14 cards in one long scroll, so stats, setup or form cues meant scrolling past everything else. Under the title a bar of square chips (**Muscles · My stats · Setup · Form · Similar**) pins to the top once you scroll; the part on screen lights up in its colour and a tap jumps there. Parts with nothing to show (no stats yet, no similar moves) get no chip. `ExerciseJumpBar`, `ExercisePart`.
- **Fuel What I have: one box finds any food** (#511, design B from `nutrition-pointers/pick-search.png`): the kitchen box is now "Search or type a list"; every match from all 240 foods shows right under it as you type, ticked in place. Pick groups wrap with their counts instead of scrolling off screen.
- **What's new 2026-10-19.3** (build 50): hold a day in the week strip (#488), build a workout as you go (#490), start fresh (#492), Fuel (#496), Monkey grows with you (#495), built for heavy lifters (#499), rewards for every day (#501); 2026-10-19.2 moved to history as build 49.
- **What's new 2026-10-19.2** (build 49): smooth week strip (#486); 2026-10-19.1 moved to history as build 48.
- **What's new 2026-10-19.1** (build 48): week strip days open in the card (#481), cleaner Explore menu (#483); 2026-10-18.4 moved to history as build 47.
- **Explore menu as a card bar** (#483, design C from `explore-menu/proposals-c.png`): the round icon dock and the live card above it did not match the app's square cards. Now one rounded bar like the 2D/3D switch: the picked section widens, fills with its colour and shows its name; the others are compact icon tiles in their colour. The top card is gone, so the body map, machines or lists start right under the bar. `ExploreMenu`.
- **Plan other sports like climbing, plus trips and one-off sport days** (#474, design C from `sport-trips/proposals-2.png` and `sport-trips/add-sport-proposals.png`): other sports hid behind "+ Add" in Train → Edit plan → Your week while climbing had its own switch and day row, the Add sport sheet opened on a full grid of 27 dim tiles with the days far below, and a snowboard week could only be entered as "Can't train". **Your week**: climbing and every other sport are bold sport cards with an on/off switch and the week's days as dots to tap; a switched-off sport keeps its days (`SportPlan.paused`), the card opens season, event and the gym days around it. **Add sport**: one swipeable sport strip (your sports first, All ›) on top, usual days, Season row (month sheet), Race or event, Gym around it, Done pinned at the bottom. **Plan sport** from a coming day in Calendar or the Train rest-day card: **Once** (that day), **Every week** (adds the weekday to the sport's usual days) or **Trip** (until a later day, with a name, e.g. Laax week). No gym on a sport day or trip day: sessions move to free days, a full trip week has none, and the days around it spare what the sport loads; trip days show in the sport's colours in Calendar and the week strip, never as missed, and open the trip to edit or remove. Engine `SportTrip`, `Profile.sportTrips`, `Profile.sportDays(on:calendar:)` with dated days, `PlanCalendar.Week.trips`; tests `SportTripTests`. Demo screens `your-week-sports`, `add-sport`, `plan-sport`, `calendar-trip`, `day-trip`.
- **Sport picker in the Explore look** (#471, design C from `other-sports/picker-proposals.png`): the Log sport and Track on Watch grids were 18 dimmed tiles (gradient at 42% opacity) with no order. Now **Your sports** (profile sports, climbing for climbers, then logged ones; up to 3) sit on top as big gradient cards with an icon tile, the last session ("Last: Sat · 2 h · 32 km") and a Log / Start pill; every other sport is a full-colour tile grouped into Snow & mountain, Wheels & feet, Water and Gym, court & more. Track on Watch still lists only what the Watch records. `SportPicker`, `Sport.pickerGroup`, `AppModel.mySports`.

### Fixed
- **Buttons do what they say, app-wide** (#556, label/action audit after #553, report `label-action-audit/report.md`): Coach tip **Start Push** on another day now reads **See Push** (it opens the page); **Swap today** tip reads **Review & swap**; the **N missed** chips list every missed day, not only the first; sport prep **See warm-up** and Wall session **See …** (they open the page, Start is there); the player's ⏭ skip is off when nothing is left (it silently finished and saved the workout); the missed-session notification's **Train today** opens the check-in with Train now (it opened a page with no Start); the Watch starts only today's session (Next up, week deck, Rings hold, workout page and Action button started any day's) and shows **Planned · Thu** instead; the muscle page opened from an exercise and What's new get the X top right; muscle exercise counts match the list (variations folded, machine chip); Fuel reopens on today after midnight or a day away. `trainBackToToday`, `WSession.plannedOn`/`startsToday`, `PlannedTag`, `Exercise.rowCount`, `MissedCatchUpSheet(weekday: nil)`.
- **Follow-ups from the audit's second list** (#556, picks "all recommended", `label-action-audit/proposals.md`): the missed-session notification's **Pick another day** opens the day picker (Reschedule) instead of the check-in; the check-in's **Move it · Next free day this week** moves it in one tap with an Undo bar; Fuel's next-meal chip reads **🛒 N to buy for this meal** (Groceries lists the whole basket); a climb card in Recent sessions opens History filtered to climbing; Train's Calendar chip counts planned sessions from today (missed days no longer counted, like the Calendar widget); an earlier day this week can't take a new workout: its rest-day card offers **Log a workout**, and the Add workout / Add to plan pickers grey out past days. `NotificationRoute.move`.
- **Player: the top-left button is a ⋯ Workout menu** (#556, design A from `player-topbar/proposals.png`): it showed a pause icon but opened Finish / Finish early / Discard, while the ✕ is what pauses. Same sheet, now an ellipsis icon labelled Workout menu; ✕ still closes and keeps the workout paused.
- **Train: back button says where it goes** (#553): with Train opening on today, a picked day's button read **Back to next up (Thu)** but went back to today (Tuesday). It now reads **Back to today**, and the next session's own card (Thursday) has it too, so there is always a way back. `trainBackToToday`, `backButtonTitle`.
- **Train opens on today, and today's card has the sport tiles** (#547): a day picked in the week strip stayed picked overnight, so Train reopened on yesterday. A pick now lasts only the day it was made (dropped when the app comes back or the date changes), and picking today counts as no pick. Build as I go, Log sport and Track live showed under past days but not on today's climbing day; they now show on today's card only, climbing day included.
- **Log climb / Log sport: time reads Started → Ended, one How hard chip** (#545, design A from `log-climb-time/proposals.png`): the card pre-filled the start as now minus the duration (18:16 − 1h30 = 16:46) in an unlabelled pill, so it looked like a wrong time; effort 7 lit both Solid and Hard. The card now shows the day, **Started** and **Ended** times; − / + moves the start and keeps the end (now by default), and picking an end time moves the start. Effort lights only its nearest chip (7 → Solid, like its label), also on the sport detail rating. `CardTimes`, `FeelChips.chip(for:)`.
- **Train opens on today**: the week strip and Next up card opened on the next session's day (e.g. Thursday) on a rest day, which read as if Thursday were today. They now open on today (today's workout, or today's rest-day card with a **Next up · Thursday · Push** button); tapping the picked day again comes back to today.
- **Sync never overwrites newer data** (#533, full app audit): each upload now carries the version it was edited from (`baseUpdatedAt`); when another device saved a newer copy, the server keeps it and the app takes it instead of overwriting (`ServerVersioned`, `UploadResult`). A log pull older than the server's deletion window re-fetches everything. Sign in with Apple continues to the 2-step code step when the account has one; the coach call name travels in a header instead of the URL; export and scan limits explain themselves.
- **Smoother app everywhere** (#535, #536, full app audit): the training plan, calendar weeks, level, badges and history were rebuilt from scratch dozens of times per screen draw; they are now worked out once per change (`AppModel+Cache`). Saving is batched and written off the main thread (`persist()` coalesces 0.5 s, `persistNow()`/`flushPersist()` on background). Watch and widget snapshots are pushed once things settle (`WatchSync.schedulePush`). Exercise animations draw two frames instead of all 17-49 and decode them ahead (`IllustrationImages`); custom exercise photos decode once; the player builds its steps once per session and skips identical Live Activity updates; Health refreshes with the same values no longer redraw the app; Fuel's date bar works out each day's load once per drag, not per cell; muscle and category lists are lazy; the Watch player no longer redraws on every heart-rate sample.
- **Data kept safe** (#533, #534, full app audit): "How did you feel" survived only until the next launch; edits to climbs, sports, PR attempts, measurements and machine notes made while a sync ran were lost; one unreadable record emptied a whole list; a note deleted during a photo upload could crash the app; signing out mid-sync lost sets and could pull the old account's history back in; a Watch workout finished after the week rolled over was dropped. All fixed (`LossyArray`, `accountEpoch`, `WorkoutRecord.watchOnly`). Also: a new account signing in no longer inherits the previous one's Fuel, Move, lift sets, Pro state or notification settings; log pulls use the server's clock; reminders are rebuilt one at a time; the Watch water day uses the ISO calendar; Home widgets relabel "Today" after midnight; double taps on passkey or confirm-it's-you no longer hang; the Live Activity countdown range can't invert; the Watch reports a tracked workout live so Health doesn't get it twice; App Lock covers the app switcher and stops re-prompting after a cancel.
- **Backend hardening from the full app audit**: **sign-in**: a used refresh token stands in for a lost refresh response (#469) only within 5 min of its rotation and while unexpired (else it's a replay and the family is revoked); optional 2-step on Sign in with Apple (`APPLE_TWO_STEP=on`, off until the app handles it there); at most 2 password hashes at once. **Abuse and cost limits**: data export 3 an hour, 10 push devices per member (oldest dropped), 50 new community posts a day, 60 "Taken?" taps an hour, a library save counted once per member, a global AI budget for coach chat + gym and food scan (`AI_DAILY_LIMIT`, 500 a day) and scans need a verified email, Apple or a day-old account. **Sync**: optional `baseUpdatedAt` per uploaded item returns newer server copies as `conflicts` instead of overwriting; pulls older than the 180-day tombstone window say `fullResync: true`. **Speed**: SQLite `busy_timeout` 5 s and `synchronous=NORMAL`, indexes for busy taps, member reports and library look-ups, busy-tap pruning moved to the purge job, the export streamed section by section, follower pushes for achievements sent in the background 5 at a time, off-site backup scrypt and gzip off the event loop (same MWB1 format), gzip/br for JSON over 1 KB (`@fastify/compress`). **Other**: a problem report is no longer stored and then answered 503 (refused up front when reports go nowhere); the coach call name can come in `X-Call-Name` and is redacted from request logs; restore leftovers (`pre-restore-*.db`) go after 14 days and App Store notification ids after 90.
- **Train: Build as I go / Log sport / Track live are three equal tiles** (#529): in build 52 the parts under Start workout had different widths and Log sport had square corners with no gaps. Each is now its own tile, same width and height, same rounded corners, 8 pt apart; label length no longer sizes a tile.
- **Fuel visual fixes** (#524): on a big day the Portions carbs tile (15+ cupped hands) ran off screen and covered the protein tile; the squares now wrap inside the tile. A dinner that fell inside a training showed twice on the timeline (Dinner · Recovery and Recovery at the same time); it is now the one recovery meal after the session. Train, Explore, Progress and Fuel no longer show content through the status bar and Dynamic Island when scrolled (`statusBarScrim()`). Test `FuelTimelineTests`.
- **Train: Build as I go / Log sport / Track live fit the card** (#526): the strip under Start workout ran past the Next up card's right edge, clipping "Track live · on your Watch". Each part now shows its icon over the title in smaller type that scales down, with inner padding, in a rounded rectangle.
- **Fuel date bar slides day by day like Train** (#510, design B from `fuel-day-strip/proposals.png`): Fuel's bar was 7 fixed days around today. It now uses Train's centre-lens strip (one shared `LensDayStrip`, also used by `WeekDayStrip`): slides 1 week back to 2 weeks ahead, the day under the lens is shown and stays picked, a tap glides it to the middle. Days are tinted by load (big day orange, training day violet, rest dark) with 🔥 💪 🌙 kept; today has a lime outline.
- Fuel: opening a recipe from Recipes, Meal plan, What I have or Groceries no longer bounces straight back to the list (#508).
- **Build as I go: leaving the empty workout asks first** (#506): the X on the "What first?" screen dropped the workout at once, while a normal workout asks "Leave workout?". It is now the same pause button with the same choice: **Pause · resume later** (Train shows Freestyle · resume) or **Discard workout**; See what's left, Finish early and Finish & save show once an exercise is added.
- **Train week strip slides day by day with a centre lens** (#486, design B from `week-strip-swipe/smooth/proposals.png`): after swiping to next week and tapping a day, the card showed that day but the strip jumped back to today, because each card built its own strip starting on today's page. The strip now slides one day at a time (a flick moves several), 1 week back to 2 weeks ahead. The day in the middle is magnified and its neighbours shrink and fade; the day that settles under it shows in the card (slide = pick), a tap glides that day to the middle, and a new card opens with its day under the lens. Page dots removed.
- **Train week strip: every day opens in the card** (#481, design A from `week-strip-swipe/consistent/proposals.png`): tapping a day showed it in the card for this week but as a pop-up for other weeks, and picks were by weekday, so tapping last Sunday showed this coming Sunday while the strip stayed on today. Days are now picked by date and always show in the card, white in the strip. Later days with a workout get **Plan a sport** (or their sport day / trip) and, this week, **Move**; later rest days offer Plan a sport; last week's days show what you trained with **See in History**.
- **Week chest opening was never shown for Watch or logged workouts** (#476): the chest (+200 XP when the week's session goal is met) only opened on the iPhone player's finish screen, and big only in the Quest map look. A goal-meeting workout finished on the Watch or logged afterwards opened it silently: Train said "Chest opened · +200 XP" with nothing seen. Train now plays the chest opening (closed chest shakes, bursts open, +200 XP) the next time it opens when this week's chest hasn't been shown on the device; tapping the open chest on the Quest map replays it; the Coach and Level bar looks' finish screen shows a "Chest opened · +200 XP" card instead of a small chip. `ChestReveal`, `AppModel.chestRevealPending` / `markChestSeen`. Demo screen `game-chest`.
- **Share a win: today's workout was missing and auto share skipped it; sports can now be shared** (#479): Share a win and auto share only took workouts with ticked sets, so a workout tracked without ticking sets (e.g. 58 min Pull) was left out. Auto share also ran once, only when the Community profile was already loaded (a workout finished on the Watch can reach the iPhone before that), and never retried a failed post. Now a finished workout counts when it has sets or lasted 10+ min; auto share loads the profile first and catches up whenever the app opens or Health brings new sessions: everything of the last 24 h not posted yet (a post deleted by hand stays deleted; a win shared by hand is never re-posted over its caption). Share a win gets a **Sports** section (logged sports, Watch-tracked sports, Health sessions, climbs; newest 6) with the sport's own gradient; auto share posts logged sports, Watch-tracked sports and logged climbs too. Sports post as kind `workout` with ref `sport-<id>` (no server change). `Community.autoShare`, `WinCard.shareable/sport/climb`, `AppModel.autoShareWins`, `SportEntry.tracked`.
- **Train: Next up rolls to next week** (#473): with this week all done, the week card said "Week complete" even when the calendar had a session the next day. It now shows next week's first session as Next up ("Next up · Mon 19 Oct"; See workout opens that day, nothing to start ahead of time). "Week complete" only shows when nothing is planned in the next two weeks.
- **Track on Watch shows up when the Watch is ready** (#462): the lime Watch button on Train and the **Track live** switch in Log sport (#439) checked for a paired Watch with MonkeyWorkout only when the screen first drew, before the iPhone had linked to the Watch, and never looked again, so they stayed hidden. They now show (and hide) as soon as the Watch link comes up or the Watch is paired, unpaired or gets MonkeyWorkout, without restarting the app (`WatchLink`). The iPhone cannot tell whether the Watch is on the wrist: when **Start on Watch** cannot wake it (off the wrist, locked), the sheet stays open and says to put it on and unlock it, or to start from the Watch (Track a sport). Next up layout (design C from `other-sports/row-proposals.png`): the three buttons were squeezed in one row ("Start worko…"); **Start workout** now takes the full width and the sports sit under it in one pill split in two, **Log sport** (past session, pink) and **Track live** (on your Watch, lime; only with a Watch). Rest days: Add workout, then the same pill.

### Added
- **Fuel supplements and meal shakes** (#514, all three designs from `nutrition-pointers/supplements.png`): add your stack (pre-workout, creatine, protein, coffee, vitamins…, or a meal shake / instant hot meal like Huel) with amounts typed or read off the label. Each one sits on the day around your training and is ticked like a meal; a My stack card shows a caffeine meter (400 mg guide, last caffeine 6 h before bedtime) and warns when a late pre-workout would hurt sleep. Protein from shakes counts toward your palms, creatine days add a glass of water, and a meal shake fills the meal it replaces with its macros.
- **Fuel: a nutrition tab linked to the day's training** (#496, designs A + B + C from `nutrition-pointers/section-home.png` and `section-screens.png`): a new tab between Progress and Community. Settings moved from the tab bar to an avatar with a gear on every tab's header (iOS shows 5 tabs). Each day's protein, carbs, fat and water come from the workouts, sports, climbs, steps and tomorrow's plan (rest / normal / big day, carb loading 1–2 days before a race or event), the goal and weight (lean mass when measured); general pointers, not prescriptions. Three looks the member picks: **Day** (dashboard tiles, plate bar, water glasses, ideas, coach card), **Timeline** (meals placed around training: pre-workout, recovery, bedtime) and **Portions** (palms, cups, fists, thumbs; "No numbers" mode). **Recipes**: 211 detailed recipes (ingredients that scale with servings, 4–9 steps, tip, prep/cook time, macros, allergens, cuisine), filters for meal, diet, allergens and tags, favourites, own recipes, cook mode with step timers, "I ate this", drawn plate pictures. **What I have**: type, pick, live-scan text and barcodes (Open Food Facts, only the code is sent) or snap the fridge (Pro, `POST /v1/me/food-scan`, 20 a day); the app builds the closest meal to the next target from what's there, with what to add, and lists recipes you can make. **Meal plan + groceries**: 3/5/7 days, lunch & dinner or every meal, people count; follows each day's training, diet, favourites and pantry, keeps picks, batch dinners become next-day lunches; grocery list by aisle minus what's at home, share, "Put bought in What I have". **Budget and less waste** (design C + B from `nutrition-pointers/section-budget.png`): weekly budget (Saver / Normal / Treat or an amount, CHF), Week basket card with cost against budget and a packs-used ring, Cheaper and Less waste buttons, cost per meal, a cheaper swap for the priciest meal; groceries buy whole packs with estimated Swiss prices (editable per food) and flag what would be left over. Budget and prices follow the iPhone's region (currency + local price level, `FuelMarket`), changeable in Fuel settings and the Budget sheet; in Switzerland a price opens Find at Migros / Coop (plain search links; real supermarket data tracked in #504). `Food.price`/`pack`, `MealPlanner.Shopping`, `MealPlanner.cost`/`basket`. **Fuel week** (load vs fuel bars, streak, XP: 10 for protein, 5 for water per day, added to the level), **Learn** cards, **race fuelling** card, **eating out** tips, refuel reminder after a workout (once a day, quiet hours respected), recovery card on the workout summary, Train **Fuel widget**, Home Screen and Lock Screen **Fuel** widget topic, **Watch water +1** row, water saved to Apple Health as dietary water, share a recipe to Community (note card), Ask Coach knows today's targets. Data stays on the iPhone (`fuel.json`). Engine `Fuel`, `RecipeBook`, `FoodBook`, `Pantry`, `MealPlanner`, `FuelWeek`; tests `FuelTests`, `RecipeCatalogTests`, `PantryPlannerTests`; backend `foodScan` route + `food_scan_usage`; privacy policy updated. Demo screens `fuel`, `fuel-timeline`, `fuel-portions`, `fuel-recipes`, `recipe`, `cook-mode`, `fuel-pantry`, `meal-plan`, `groceries`, `fuel-week`, `fuel-learn`, `fuel-setup`, `share-recipe`.
- **Expert lifters: Lift Focus, Pro Sets and Lift Lab** (#499, designs A + B + C from `expert-lifters/proposals.png`): the app had no set types beyond straight sets, a warm-up ramp only inside PR attempts, no plate maths, no way to peak one lift for a target by a date, and no rep-record table. **A · Lift Focus** (Training profile card, or **Focus this lift** in Lift Lab): pick a lift, a target and a template (**Monkey peak** 72→92% waves with deloads, taper and test, twice a week; **5/3/1 wave** at 90% training max with AMRAP and 5×5 back-offs; **GZCL T1** 5×3, 6×2, 10×1 plus a 3×8 day; **Smolov Jr** 3 weeks, 4 sessions a week) and a length; the test day gets three attempts (opener 91%, second 96%, third the target when within 3%). The lift goes into the week's best-matching sessions first in the strength block (added when the plan lacks it), the rest of the plan can drop to maintenance (−30% sets), and a weak point (off the chest / out of the hole / off the floor, or lockout) adds two accessories. The block page shows week bars, the predicted e1RM on test day, rest of plan and weak point. **B · Pro Sets** (exercise page → Set style): top set + back-offs (back-offs re-set from the top set's weight and effort), AMRAP last set, drop sets (2 × −20%, no rest), myo-reps, rest-pause, cluster sets and % of 1RM, for every workout with that exercise. The player's set card shows the set's role and target, a warm-up ramp before the first set of heavy barbell work and the plates per side; drops and minis use their own short rest. Still one screen, no scrolling. **C · Lift Lab** (exercise page): e1RM and its trend, rep records 1-12RM (this month's coloured), bar speed at the last Watch-measured load against the first time, heavy sets ≥85% in 4 weeks and test-day attempts. Engine `ProSets`, `Plates`, `SetStyle`, `PlannedSet`, `LiftFocus`, `FocusTemplate`, `WeakPoint`, `LiftLab`, `WeekPlan.applyingLiftFocus/applyingSetStyles`; app `AppModel+ExpertLifts` (`SyncedProfile.liftFocus`, `setStyles`), `ExpertLiftViews`, `SetLogState.planned`. Tests `ExpertLiftTests`. Demo screens `lift-lab`, `lift-focus`, `lift-focus-setup`, `set-style`.
- **Kind streaks + Wins you missed** (#501, designs A + B from `rewarding/proposals.png`): rewards went to doing more (volume, PRs, session counts), one missed week reset the streak to 0, coming back after a break earned nothing, and a weak day ended with no win. **Streak shields**: every 4 goal weeks in a row earn a shield (2 held at most); a missed past week uses one and the streak carries on (Progress streak row shows 🛡, Train shows "Streak shield used" the week after). **Comeback quest**: after 10+ days without training, Train shows **Welcome back**; the first session back and the next two within 21 days earn +100 XP each (loads already ease after a break, #156). **Short sessions count**: every finished workout earns at least 100 XP. **Finish sheet**: always 3 personal wins (back after a break, heavier or more reps than last time, week goal, shield earned, every set done, more sets than last time, a new move, feeling good lately; session number, minutes and muscles fill a weak day) and **How do you feel now?** (4 faces, kept on this iPhone, feeds later wins). Engine `StreakRun`, `Comeback`, `Wins`, `Feel` (`KindRewards.swift`), `GameProgress.minWorkoutXP` / `comebackXP`, `ProgressStats.streakShields` / `shieldSavedLastWeek`; badges use the shielded streak. App `KindStreakCards`, `WinsCard`, `FeelRow`, `AppModel.comeback` / `wins(for:)` / `setFeel`. Tests `KindRewardsTests`. Demo screen `train-comeback`; `game-done-…` show wins.
- **Start fresh: a brand new plan from scratch** (#492, design A + B from `fresh-start/proposals.png`): there was no way to reset the plan; swaps, edited plan days, own split days, added or moved days, per-workout changes and a programme each had their own reset, or none. Training profile ends with a **Start fresh** card. Its sheet lists what goes (only what this plan has) and what stays (history, PRs and XP, profile and gyms, what Monkey learned, your own workouts). **A · Build my new plan** keeps your answers and makes a new plan (new seed, own day picks dropped); **B · Answer again** opens every plan question prefilled (goal, week, time, style, flow, method, gym), then builds it. A running programme is left (recorded as left, your own plan back). Workouts done this week and the one in progress stay as they were. A gradient **Undo** bar puts the old plan back. `AppModel.startFresh` / `undoFreshStart` / `freshStartClears` / `freshStartProfile`, `FreshStartCard`, `FreshStartSheet`. Demo screen `fresh-start`.
- **The app grows with the member** (#495, options A + B + C from `smarter-app/option-{a,b,c}.png`). **Block review** (A): when a new 4-week cycle starts, Train shows what the last block brought: sessions done, strength change of the main lifts (est. 1RM vs the block before), level up (3 of 4 sessions and +2 % stronger; growth level 3 = intermediate, 6 = advanced), plateaus (3 weekly bests within 1 %) with a variation to swap in on their plan days, the deload week ahead and a lagging muscle group (under 70 % of its counterpart's sets) as a new focus. **Start block** applies all, **Tweak** picks. **Daily auto-tune** (B): a ready score (Apple Health HRV, resting HR and sleep, the last workout's reps in reserve, days in a row, sore muscles tapped) picks Light, Medium or Push for today's workout: Light = 1 RPE lower on the main lift and one set less on accessories, Push = one more main-lift set (not in a deload week), sore muscles swap their moves for ones that spare them. Tap the readiness line on Train for the score, your own pick, sore muscles and every change with Undo; on by default, switch off there (then #156 easing applies as before). **Growth path** (C): new Progress widget (default board, after Streak) and page with a long-term goal (suggested from your most-logged main lift, editable), its predicted date from the last 8 weeks' trend, a milestone road, **What Monkey noticed** this week (goal hit → next goal, a skipped gym day → move it, rests longer than planned, a muscle group untrained for 2 weeks → focus, new best lifts) and **Coach remembers** (goal, level, days, focus, likes, avoided moves, your own notes), sent with Ask Coach questions (`memory` in the chat context). A slim "Monkey noticed" bar on Train once a week. Engine `Growth`, `BlockReview`, `AutoTune`, `TuneChange`, `GrowthGoal`, `Noticed`, `CoachMemory`; app `AppModel+Growth`, `GrowthViews`, `SyncedProfile.growth`, `LocalState.dayTune`; backend coach chat `memory`. Tests `GrowthTests`, `AutoTuneTests`, coach chat. Demo screens `train-smart`, `block-tweak`, `auto-tune`, `growth-path`, `coach-memory`.
- **Build a workout as I go** (#490, designs A + B + C from `build-as-you-go/proposals.png`): an unplanned session needed a workout built before starting. **Build as I go** (first part of the pill under Start workout on Train, rest days too, and a card on top of the Workout library) opens the player empty: **Coach picks** (3 strength exercises your gym and level allow, a big lift first, then muscles not trained yet, lifts you did before ranked higher), **By muscle** tiles and Search (the catalog opens on that muscle), and **Lately** (your last logged exercises). One tap adds and plays it. On the workout's last exercise Up next becomes the Coach's next 3 picks plus **More**; a pick plays at once when the exercise is done, otherwise it queues. A white **Add** chip on the exercise card queues more at any time. FINISH saves it to History like any workout; closing with nothing done keeps nothing. Paused freestyle shows as **Freestyle · resume**. Engine `Freestyle` (session, adding, suggest), app `AppModel+Freestyle` (kept on this device, `LocalState.freestyle`), views `FreestyleViews`; swap and remove in the player work on it through the session edit path. Tests `FreestyleTests`.
- **Long press a workout day in the Train week strip** (#488, design A from `week-strip-swipe/long-press/proposals.png`): only the This week stages had a long-press menu. A workout day of this week in the strip now lifts its workout (title, minutes, XP) with **Move to…**, **Swap with…** (the week's other workouts still to come), **Edit workout** (session page), **Plan a sport** (today on) and **Add workout this day**; done workouts keep Edit and Add. Later weeks follow the plan, so their workout days offer **Show in card** and **Plan a sport**.
- **Release notes for build 47** (What's new `2026-10-18.4`, 4 cards): plan your sports and trips (#478), Next up looks ahead (#475), never miss a chest (#477), share any sport (#480). `2026-10-18.3` moved to the history as build 46.
- **Week chest card on Progress** (#476, design A from `week-chest/proposals.png`): Progress never said a chest had opened or what the week earned. New **Week chest** widget (wide; second on the default board, in Add widget for custom boards): this week's chest (open, or "2/4 sessions · 2 to go"), **XP this week** in big type with chips for sessions (+workout XP), the chest (+200) and walking (Daily Move), and the last 8 weeks as opened 🎁 / closed 📦 chests. Tap an open chest to replay its opening; a closed one opens Training. Engine `GameWeek.recent`, `ProgressWidgetKind.chest`; app `WeekChestCard`, `MoveStore.weekXP`. Tests `GameTests`, `ProgressBoardTests`.
- **Release notes for the 2026-10-18 evening bundle** (What's new `2026-10-18.3`, 7 cards): livelier Explore (#459), swipeable week strip (#464), Log climb like Log sport (#460), Track on your Watch from Train (#462), faster sport picker (#471), Report a problem (#467), stay signed in (#469). `2026-10-18.2` moved to the history as build 45.
- **Train week strip: today in the middle, swipe across days** (#464, design C from `week-strip-swipe/proposals.png`): the week card showed Monday to Sunday only. It now opens with today in the middle (3 days before, 3 after) and swipes a page of 7 days at a time: one page back, two ahead, limited to 1 week back and 2 weeks ahead (days past that are dimmed). Each day shows its date. Days of this week work as before (tap = Next up follows the day, missed days open catch-up); later days show the calendar's plan and open the calendar day sheet; last week's days show a tick when you trained and open History. Page dots under the strip.
- **Report a problem** (#467, design A + B + C from `report-problem/proposals.png`): members tell us about bugs, things not working right and ideas from inside the app. **A · Settings → Help → Report a problem**: type chips (Bug, Not working right, Idea), area chips (Train, Workout player, Explore, …), text, up to 3 screenshots (draw on them to highlight); app version, build, iOS, device and screen attached. **B · Shake to report** anywhere, over sheets too: the screen is captured, the report opens with it attached (Settings → Help → Shake to report turns it off). **C · Report** next to the sync error on Train, error text prefilled. Backend `POST /v1/me/reports` (10 per hour): stored privately (deleted with the account, in Export my data), emailed to LEGAL_EMAIL with screenshots attached (EXIF stripped); when Fly secret `GITHUB_REPORTS_TOKEN` (fine-grained, Issues: write) is set via Fly setup, also a GitHub issue labelled `member-report` with the member's words and app facts only: no name, email, account id or screenshots, typed emails and phone numbers removed. Mail gained attachments (SES multipart MIME). Tests `reports.test.ts`. Demo screens `report`, `report-shake`, `report-error`, `report-sent`.
- **Explore by machine** (#456, design A + C from `explore-by-machine/proposals.png`): Explore only listed exercises by muscle and category. **A · Machines** (new Explore card next to Muscles): every machine and piece of kit with at least one exercise, grouped by area of the gym (Barbells, Dumbbells, Cables, Machines, Bars & rings, Functional, Cardio, Climbing), each a gradient card with its picture, the muscles it trains most, an "In your gym" tag and its exercise count. **Only what's in my gym** switch (shown once the gym is set up). Tap → machine page: picture, area, how to adjust it, what it trains on the body map (muscle chips open the muscle), New exercise prefilled with the machine, and every exercise using it by category with variations folded. **C · machine chips** on Explore → Muscles and every muscle page: All · My gym · Barbells · Dumbbells · Cables · Machines · Bars & rings · Functional · Body weight. A chip narrows the muscle cards (counts follow, muscles with nothing hide) and the muscle page's exercise list; tap it again to clear. The chip is remembered on the device. Custom exercises included. Engine `MachineFilter`, `Exercise.using(_:)`, `Exercise.muscles(using:)`, `Exercise.working(_:filter:gym:)`, `GymArea.browsable()`; tests `ExploreMachineTests`. Demo screens `explore-machines`, `explore-machine-chips`, `machine`.
- **Daily Move: steps and stairs count toward your level, with one smart nudge** (#454, design B + C timing from `daily-move/proposals.png`): walking and stairs from Apple Health (iPhone or Watch) now earn XP for the same level as workouts: 10 XP per 1,000 steps and 3 per floor, capped at 80 a day so walking never outweighs a workout, plus +50 XP for a week with 5 days at your usual. Your **usual** is the middle of your last two weeks (at least 3,000), not a flat 10k, so there's nothing to set up. XP counts from the first day the app reads your steps, so updating doesn't jump levels. **B · Daily Move tile** on Train (below Next up, so Start stays on the first screen; small or wide, can be moved or hidden): steps vs your usual with a lime bar, floors, XP today and active days this week; tap → Daily Move sheet with how it works. Members connected to Health before this get a "Count your steps" tile that asks for steps and floors. **C · Nudge timing**: at most one nudge a day, from the hour you usually walk (busiest afternoon / evening hour of the last two weeks, else 17:00) until 21:00, only when you're under 60 % of your usual and at least 1,000 steps short. It's skipped after a workout or sport (this app's, or any app's in Health), on a Rest day tap, in quiet hours, or when the switch is off. The nudge says the steps left, the minutes of walking and the XP ("1,400 steps to your usual 👣 · A 15 min walk or the stairs gets you there. +20 XP."), with **Later** (one more reminder in an hour) and **Rest day** (no more today). New steps wake the app about once an hour through HealthKit background delivery (new entitlement `com.apple.developer.healthkit.background-delivery`). Settings → Notifications → **Daily Move nudge** switch (on by default). Steps and floors stay on the iPhone and are never sent to the server; privacy policy and the Health permission text updated. Engine `DailyMove` + `DayMove`, `GameProgress.moveXP`, `NotificationPrefs.moveNudges`, Train widget `.move`; app `MoveStore`, `HealthManager.refreshMove` / `checkMoveNudge`, `MoveWidget`, `MoveSheet`. Tests `DailyMoveTests`.
- **Hyrox ready: race prep for a season of races** (#431, A + B + C from `programmes/hyrox-ready/proposals.png`): a new programme in Explore → Programmes (next to Snow ready) to prepare for Hyrox. 4× a week: signature **Station strength** (sled push and pull, sandbag lunges, thrusters for wall balls, farmers carry), **Compromised run** (1 km runs straight into burpees and thrusters) and **Engine** (SkiErg and row intervals); the fourth day is generated for your gym. **A** · on its page pick the division (Open / Pro / Doubles / Relay, saved to the Hyrox settings) and add **every race day**: the programme becomes one season. The first block fits the first race (Base, Build, Peak, a half-sim week, race week; closer races get a short Build or a sharpening week); each gap to the next race gets its own block (6+ weeks: recovery, Build, Peak; 3–5 weeks: recovery and a short Build; less: recovery then a sharpening week), and it ends after the last race with your own plan back. No race set = a standard 9-week block. Running, the page shows the next race and its countdown. **B** · Hyrox hub (Progress → Hyrox) gets a **Train for this race** card that opens it (race date from My sports prefilled); once running the card shows the week and phase. **C** · your goal time sets race pace on the runs (/km) and SkiErg / row (/500 m), and the division sets sled, sandbag, wall ball and carry loads, shown as each exercise's target; the sim week asks for a half Hyrox on the Watch, whose report compares every split to the plan. Engine `Programme+Hyrox.swift` (`hyroxSeason`, `targeting`), `ProgrammeSignature.Item.target`, `ActiveProgramme.races`; time and distance doses ("1000 m", "4 min") get realistic session estimates. Tests `HyroxReadyTests`. Demo screen `hyrox-ready`.
- **Find a gym → Near a place: suggestions, autocomplete, spelling help** (#447, A + B + C from `place-search/proposals.png`): the place field showed nothing until the member typed a full name and pressed Search, and a typo found nothing. **B · before typing**: bold cards for upcoming trips (calendar travel periods, with dates), places searched before (last 8, kept on the device only), your saved gyms' towns and the town you're in (only when location is already allowed; never asks), each with its gym count. **A · while typing**: Apple Maps autocomplete with the matched letters lit in lime; known places match despite typos ("zurih" → Zürich, "hotl adlon" → Hotel Adlon); **Did you mean …?** when the spelling looks off (known places first, else Apple Maps' own reading). **C · live map**: suggestions float over the map and the top match drops its gym pins as you type (debounced). Search on the keyboard picks the top suggestion. Engine `PlaceSuggest` (fold, edit distance with swaps, score, correction, highlight, initial, remember); tests `PlaceSuggestTests`.
- **Trainer workout: Chest & Back** (#451): the member's trainer plan "Trainingspläne 3 – Chest & Back" is a built-in workout in Explore → Workouts (next to Lower body and Arms + Shoulder): bench press, incline bench press, dumbbell fly, low-to-high cable fly, pull-ups 3 × 12, plate-loaded lat pulldown, single-arm cable row, deadlift, standing dumbbell reverse fly, dumbbell shrug; 3 × 10 otherwise, 102 kcal, 88 activity points as given. New catalog exercise **Plate-loaded lat pulldown** (lever machine, own illustration in 17 frames, 3D position, technique). Test `TemplateTests`.
- **Sport maps: ski day, route profile, Watch live map** (#445, A + B + C from `sport-maps/proposals.png`): the session page showed one speed-coloured route and a run list. **A · Ski day**: each run gets its own colour on the map and lifts show as dashed grey lines. A **Your day** bar shows riding, lifts and breaks in order (stopped 3 min or more is a break), with totals. Tiles show vertical, runs, top speed and distance. Tap a run to zoom the map to it and dim the others. Each run shows metres dropped, length, and top and average speed (🔥 on the fastest). A run now starts when the descent starts, not while waiting at the top. **B · Route profile** (hike, ride, run, trail run…): colour the map by **Speed / Climb / HR** (heart rate per point from Health). The elevation chart is by km with steep parts hot (blue going down, green flat, amber, red over 10 %), and dragging it shows km, altitude and grade. A 🚩 marks the top. Tiles show distance, ascent, **moving time**, and HR or top speed. **Splits** per km (5 km on rides over 30 km) give pace (speed for bikes) and metres climbed, plus stopped time and max grade. **C · Watch**: a new map page while tracking shows the breadcrumb, drawn as a line with no map tiles (cheap on battery, works without signal), km, climb and an arrow back to the start. Ski: the header says **ON LIFT · N RUNS** or **RUN N**, there is a tick when a run ends, "Last run 520 m · 61 km/h", and riding vs lift time. The summary adds a mini map and riding / lifts. Fixed: Pause → Resume on the Watch started a new route, so the part before the pause never reached Health. Routes stay on the device (Apple Health + iPhone) and are never shared. Engine `SportTrack` `distances`, `grades`, `maxGrade`, `movingSeconds`, `splits`, `dayParts`, `dayTotals`, `Run.meters/seconds/avgKmh`; tests in `SportTests`.
- **Customisable Progress page** (#444, design A + C from `history-page/custom-proposals.png`): Progress was a fixed page map plus long sections. It is now a **widget board** on Train's system (#260): Level, Streak, Training, Body, Records, Badges, Muscles, Programmes, Recent sessions, Consistency and Sessions per week, each small / wide / large where it makes sense. **Edit** (grid button by the title) wiggles the widgets: − removes, the corner button resizes, drag onto another to move; presets **Classic** (default, today's page), **Lifter**, **My sports**, **Minimal**; **Add widget** gallery and a **List** view (reorder, remove, reset). A tile opens its part as its own page (Training keeps live tiles, consistency, trends, climbing, Hyrox and other sports). **C · sport widgets**: Add widget → Your sports / More sports adds a card per sport (sessions and time in 30 days, top grade for climbing or farthest / longest session, last session; large adds 8 weeks of minutes); tap opens History filtered to that sport. The pinned section bar is gone. Board synced with the profile (`SyncedProfile.progressBoard`, nil = Classic). Engine `ProgressBoard.swift`; tests `ProgressBoardTests`. Demo screens `progress-edit`, `progress-sports`.
- **History page** (#442, design C from `history-page/proposal-c.png`): History was the last section of Progress, hidden at the bottom. Progress gets a **Recent sessions** widget (swipe strip of the last 8 sessions of any kind, See all) and the History tile opens a new **History** page: totals (sessions, time, sports), a search bar (title, exercises, sport, grade, app) and sport chips (All, Gym, then each sport present, most used first), and a day-by-day timeline of gradient rows for gym workouts, climbs, other sports and Hyrox. Gym rows open the session page (Undo workout unchanged), sport rows the sport session sheet. Train's **Recent climbs** strip is gone: past climbs live on History, where a long press on a climb logged here deletes it. Demo screen `history-page`.
- **Release notes for the 2026-10-17 evening bundle** (What's new `2026-10-17.3`, 5 cards): every sport counts (#421), Hyrox on your wrist (#427), Snow ready (#429), how we talk to you (#420), smoother gym map (#423) with lift sensor rep floor (#425) in one card. `2026-10-17.2` moved to the history as build 41.
- **Snow ready: pre-season ski / snowboard programme** (#428, A + B + C from `programmes/snow-ready/proposals.png`): a new 8-week programme in Explore → Programmes (listed first) to get legs ready for the season: Base (squats, single leg, Nordic, Copenhagen), Build (heavier legs, lateral bounds, landings), Peak (long-run legs: longer sets, 90 s wall sits), Test (squat, split squat), then an easy week before day 1. Signature workouts **Snow legs** and **Edge + balance**; the third day is generated for your gym. **A** · ride picker **Ski / Snowboard / Both** on its page (Ski: lateral lunge + side plank; Snowboard: Edge + rotation with Cossack squat and rotational throws, squat jumps for landings). **B** · **First day on snow** with a date picker: the programme length is fitted so it ends the week before (5–16 weeks; Base and Build stretch, Peak / Test / Ease stay); the start button says when it ends; while it runs the page counts down the days. Default day = start of the ski / snowboard season set in My sports, else the Saturday 8 weeks out. **C** · from September to December, members with Ski or Snowboard in My sports get a first Coach Monkey tip "Season's coming!" that opens the programme (until they open it once that season, or start any programme); Training profile → My sports shows a **Snow ready programme** link. Engine `Programme+Snow.swift` (`SnowRide`, `snowWeeks`, `defaultSnowDay`, `snowNudge`), `ActiveProgramme.firstDay`; programme headline counts weeks to the test week (not to the end). Tests `SnowReadyTests`. Demo screen `snow-ready`.
- **Other sports: snowboard, MTB, Hyrox, hiking, diving…** (#416, A + B + C from `other-sports/proposals.png`): climbing was the only sport next to the gym. Now any sport plugs into the same model; climbing and its MonkeyGrade link stay as they were. Engine: a catalogue of 28 sports (`Sport`), each with its body load (legs / pull + grip / push / cardio) and MET; `Profile.sports` (`SportPlan`: days, season months, event date, day before / after / same day). "Around climbing days" scoring now also places gym days around these sports by load (a legs sport keeps leg days away, the day after is lighter), with session notes ("MTB tomorrow · legs kept light") and focus-change warnings; off-season sports are ignored. **Scuba diving** defaults to no gym on dive days and rest the day after (exercise after diving raises decompression risk). **A · Log sport**: the Next up card's Log climb is now **Log sport** (rest days too; climbing days keep Log climb): a bold sport grid (your sports first, Climbing opens the climb log), when, duration, effort, distance; saved to Apple Health as the matching workout type. **B · My sports** in Training profile → Schedule: sport cards with days, season, race date and the day-before / after rules. **C · Spotted in Health**: workouts from the Watch, Strava, Garmin, Slopes… of the last 4 weeks are read with distance, ascent, HR and kcal; an unrated one from yesterday or today becomes the first Coach Monkey tip ("Spotted: MTB 2h40 yesterday") that opens a sheet to rate it (Easy / Solid / Hard / Wrecked) and, after a hard one, **Swap today** to a session that spares what it loaded (this week only). **Sport session page** (A + C from `other-sports/session-proposals.png`), from the Spotted tip and Progress: the Health route on a map coloured by speed, tiles for distance, vertical / ascent, top speed (3-point median) and average HR, an elevation chart you drag to replay (the map dot follows, with altitude and speed at that point), then the sport's own section: downhill runs for ski / snowboard (split at lifts), laps or Hyrox splits when the recording app saved them, summit height for hikes; routes are read on the device only (`SportTrack`, new Health read type workout routes). Week strip and calendar show sport days; Progress gets an **Other sports** card. Server: `GET/POST/DELETE /v1/me/sports`, table `sports`, data export + restore. Tests: GymCore `SportTests`, `sports.test.ts`.
- **Hyrox add-on: race mode on the Watch, hub and race report** (#426, A + B + C from `other-sports/hyrox-proposals.png`): Hyrox athletes race without their phone. **A · Watch race mode** (Track a sport → Hyrox: full race, half sims, 4 × 1 km + a station): 3-2-1 start ring, then one glowing screen per step (run pace and metres from the pedometer, station with work, division load, counted strokes / jumps / wall balls and target, Roxzone timer), time ahead or behind the plan, HR. **Steps record themselves**: the race order is fixed, so the Watch only tells running (cadence) from the next station's wrist-motion signature (`HyroxDetector`, 25 Hz motion + pedometer); a run ends at its last running second, a station starts at its first matching second, the gaps are Roxzone; unsure for 75 s → buzz and ask. Double tap (watchOS 11), the Ultra Action button or the big button move on by hand. Finish: confetti, total, PB vs last race, runs / stations / Roxzone. The race is saved to Apple Health (cross training, one workout), queued to the iPhone and, when the Watch has Wi-Fi or cellular, uploaded straight to the server with an upload-only key (`POST /v1/me/watch-key`, `POST /v1/watch/sports`, table `watch_keys`). **B · Hyrox hub** (Progress → Hyrox): division (Open / Pro / Doubles / Relay, men / women / mixed) with its sled, carry, lunge and wall-ball loads, goal time → pacing plan for all 16 splits and Roxzone, race name and countdown (race date from My sports), this week's sims (compromised runs on your weakest station, both halves) and **Start race on Watch**, past races. **C · Race report** (also on the session page): replay of the race as a 16-step track, every split vs plan and vs the last full race (tap to fix a split by 5 s), run pace fade chart, Roxzone, and what to work on next. Motion thresholds are first guesses: tune after the first real race. Privacy policy: Hyrox races and the Watch upload. Tests: `HyroxTests` (LiftSensor package), `sports.test.ts`.
- **Track sports on the Apple Watch** (#418, B + C from `other-sports/watch-proposals.png`): Watch home → **Track a sport** runs an Apple workout session in the background (HR, kcal, route and laps saved to Apple Health, then shown on the iPhone session page). **B · one dashboard per sport**: ski / snowboard speed + top + vertical down + run count (runs split at lifts), hike / mountaineering / trail run ascent + km + summit, bike / run km + speed + ascent, others HR + kcal; Hyrox has its own race mode (#426); other lap sports get LAP. Pause / End & save on the second page. **C · battery**: pick **Full** (GPS every 8–15 m) or **Saver** (coarse GPS once a minute) before the start; always-on screen redraws once a minute; route points written to Health in batches; the summary shows battery used. **Sport-day nudge**: 08:00 notification on planned sport days ("Snowboard today?"), mirrored to the Watch, off with session reminders. Dives are left to dive apps (no Watch tracking for scuba). Watch Info.plist: location when in use.
- **How we talk to you: pronouns and what the coach calls you** (#415, design C from `pronouns/proposals.png`): gender in Body & Health was only stored, never used to address the member. Settings → Body & Health → About you is now one **How we talk to you** card: **1 Gender** (same inclusive list), **2 Pronouns** (she/her, he/him, they/them, Custom…; prefilled from gender: Woman → she, Man → he, any other identity → they, not set / prefer not to say → nothing assumed; the member can override), **3 Coach calls you** (your name, Champ, Legend, Queen with she, King with he, Nothing; default your name), and **Show pronouns on my Community profile** (off by default). Live previews show the coach voice line, an Ask Coach reply and the Community row. **Coach voice**: the call goes before the big moments only (start, halfway, record, finish): "Alex! Halfway!" (Hype) or "Alex. Half done." (Chill, Drill), in the subtitle and the voice. Studio voices play a short recording of the call made once per voice, style and name (`GET /v1/me/coach-voices/:voice/call/:style?name=`, letters only, ≤ 24 characters, cached on the volume); without it the line is said without the call. **Ask Coach** gets the pronouns and the call name (neutral wording when unset). **Community**: pronouns pill next to the nickname on posts, profiles and Find members, only when shown (`PUT /v1/community/me/pronouns`, column `community_profiles.pronouns`). Body figure (#110) stays separate. App copy already spoke to the member as "you" everywhere (audited, no he/she strings). Engine `Pronouns`, `CoachCall`, `CoachLines.called`; tests `MemberAddressTests`, `coachChat` / `coachVoices` / `community` tests. Demo screen `body-talk`.
- **Ask Coach everywhere, by voice** (#412, A + C from `ask-coach-everywhere/proposals.png`): Ask Coach was only on the Train Coach card and only built workouts. **A**: a lime 🐵 bubble floats on every tab (drag it to either edge, kept); tap opens Ask Coach as a sheet that knows the screen ("Looking at: Barbell bench press", with that screen's starter questions), hold to talk. **C**: **✨ question** sparks on the exercise page ("Is this right for me?"), the Progress sessions-per-week chart ("How's my consistency?") and the calendar day sheet; a **mic** in the player's top bar takes hands-free commands on the device (done / "two left" / easy, "80 kg", "drop 5", "8 reps", lighter / heavier, skip rest, more rest, next, go back), anything else is asked to Coach about the current exercise and set. Voice: on-device speech-to-text (mic button in the chat when the field is empty; stops after a short silence), answers read aloud with the coach voice when you talked (Settings: When I talk / Always / Never). Coach now answers questions (not only workouts), may add one **Open …** button (exercise, Progress, Calendar, Explore, Settings) and up to 3 follow-up chips. Settings → Coach & Ask Coach: bubble on/off, player mic on/off, read aloud. Server: context `screen` + `focus`, answer `action` + `suggestions`. Privacy policy: voice is turned into text by Apple (on device when supported), only words are sent. Demo screens `ask-coach`, `ask-coach-bubble`; tests `coachChat.test.ts`, GymCore `VoiceCommandTests`.
- **Release notes for the 2026-10-17 afternoon bundle** (What's new `2026-10-17.2`, 5 cards): Ask Coach everywhere (#413), anatomy position guide (#409), Gym Map (#407), lift sensor control (#411), coach voice fix + setup (#403). Train sync error fix (#405) in the ChangeLog only. `2026-10-17.1` moved to the history as build 40.
- **Lift sensor: more control, clearer logging** (#410, all of A + B + C from `watch-gpath/control-proposals.png`): after a first try the Watch counted too eagerly (unracking and fidgeting became reps), and it wasn't clear when it logged a set. **Settings → Apple Watch → Lift sensor** is now a card with an on/off switch and three rows. **Counting** (Relaxed / Normal / Strict): a rep needs about 7 / 8 / 10 cm and a clear push. Once a set has 3 reps, moves much shorter than the set's typical rep are dropped (none / 35 % / 45 % of the median range), so unracking and fidgeting don't count. **Logging the set**: *Auto* logs it once the bar is racked, after a labelled 6 s countdown ("Logs in 5 s", was 4 s) with Undo. *Tap ✓* buzzes once and asks **✓ LOG 8 REPS**: nothing logs until the member taps. *I log* only counts and fills in the reps. **Stop buzz**: Off / −10 / −20 / −30 %. Each choice has a line saying what it does. **Learns from corrections** (B): on the Watch each rep shows as a bar sized by its range, and a short yellow bar is probably not a rep. When the member fixes the count with the Crown (or edits the reps on the iPhone while the Watch counts), that exercise counts half a step stricter (it counted too many) or looser (too few), within ±1.5 steps. The rest screen says "Counted 5, you said 4 · stricter next time". The exercise page's Bar speed card shows "Counting: Strict · learned from 2 corrections" with Relaxed / Normal / Strict / Reset. **Try it** (C): practice mode from the settings card. The iPhone shows the movement the Watch sees (last 6 s), a dot per counted rep, each rep's range and speed, and the count. Switching Counting recounts the same reps at once, and **Use Strict** saves it. The Watch shows a big count while practising. Maths in `LiftSensor` (`Strictness`, `SetAnalyzer.consistent`, `SetTracker.setStrictness` / `trace`) with tests. Demo screens: iPhone `watch-lift`, `lift-practice`; Watch `lift-confirm`, `practice`. Thresholds still need real-Watch calibration.
- **Coach voice setup** (#402, C + A from `workout-coach/setup.png`): the coach sounded like a robot because no studio voice was downloaded. With the coach voice on and no voice ever picked, the app now downloads the best studio voice for the member's accent (from the phone's region) in the background. The first workout then shows a toast, "Coach voice: Joanna · Change". If no studio voice is on the phone at workout start, a sheet asks once: three suggested voices with ▶ to hear one before downloading, **Use**, or **Not now**. Members who picked the iPhone voice on purpose are not asked.
- **Gym Map: scan, shared map, live crowd, real floor plan, your route, guided walk** (#406): zones (#274) only worked in the background and the floor plan didn't read as a plan. Design A + B extended to the six-part Gym Map plan (`gym-zones/v3/plan.png`). **Scan your gym** (Pro): on the Equipment page's zone card, **Scan** → pick a zone, take a photo of that area; Claude Opus 5.5 vision on the server (`POST /v1/me/gym-scan`, 30 photos a day, photo never stored) lists the equipment it sees, one tap ticks it and puts it in the zone. **Scan the room** on LiDAR iPhones (RoomPlan): the map gets the real room size (route metres use it) and the entrance. **Shared by your gym**: drawn plans are shared per gym (`PUT/GET /v1/gyms/maps`); a member without a plan sees **Map ready for your gym · mapped by N members · X% agree → USE IT** (the plan other members agree with most; never who). **Live crowd**: each **Taken?** tap is also counted for the gym (`POST /v1/gyms/busy`, one per member and zone per 5 min, kept 8 weeks); the route card shows **Right now at your gym** (zones members found busy in the last 20 min) and the quietest coming hour (`GET /v1/gyms/crowd`), and Taken? lists quiet zones first. Privacy policy and data export cover maps and busy taps; tables `gym_maps`, `gym_busy`, `gym_scan_usage`. **Gym map** (was Floor plan / Gym layout): tiled floor, thick outer walls, an entrance you drag along the walls, zone blocks with icons of the equipment that sits there, a **2D / 3D** tilt view; tap a zone to see its pieces, then drag one onto another zone (or touch and hold → Move to…). **Your route today** on the workout page: numbered stops from the entrance on a mini plan, stops, walks and rough metres walked, each stop's exercises; tap the plan to edit it. **Taken?** in the player's Change sheet: swaps to the same muscles in another zone (quiet zones first) and remembers the zone was busy at that time; the route card warns when the first zone is usually busy around now (two taps within 45 min of the time of day in 8 weeks, same weekday counts double; kept on the device only). Player: Up next shows a mini plan with the walk to the next zone, and a **Zone cleared** banner + haptic when you leave a zone for good. Engine `GymLayout.door/route`, `GymRoute`, `GymBusy`; tests `GymRouteTests`.
- **Remove an exercise, and swap or remove during a workout** (#391): long-press on an exercise of a planned workout only offered Swap (Remove was only in Edit), and once a workout had started an exercise couldn't be swapped (only **Not here?** for missing equipment). Design A + C from `remove-exercise/proposals.png`. Workout page: the long-press menu gains a red **Remove from workout** (before the workout starts, not on the last exercise); it goes at once with the Undo bar, no confirmation. Player: a lime **Change** chip on the exercise card, in **Not here?**'s slot, opens **Swap for similar** / **Remove from workout** / **Not at this gym**; swap and remove apply before the exercise's first set (after it, Skip ⏭ moves on, and the sheet says so). Remove moves to the next exercise and shows an Undo banner that puts it back on the same spot. Both are saved as the workout's edit, so the session page and the Watch follow. `AppModel.removeFromSession` / `restoreSessionEdit`, `ChangeExerciseSheet`.
- **Coach voices: studio voices, female or male, 7 accents** (#395): the in-workout coach (#379) sounded robotic because it used the iPhone's basic voice for the language. Design B + A fallback from `workout-coach/voices.png`. Settings → Workout coach → **Coach voice**: **Female / Male** and an accent (🇺🇸 🇬🇧 🇦🇺 🇮🇪 🇮🇳 🇿🇦 🇳🇿). **Studio voices** (Amazon Polly neural: Joanna, Ruth, Matthew, Gregory, Stephen, Amy, Arthur, Olivia, Niamh, Kajal, Ayanda, Aria): **Get** downloads the voice's recorded coach lines once (fully offline afterwards), ▶ plays a sample, tap to use it, long-press to remove. Studio lines are written without numbers or exercise names so one recording fits everyone; the bubble shows the same words. Without a studio pack the coach uses the **best installed iPhone voice** for the pick (premium, then enhanced, never novelty voices; no pitch shift) and reads the full line with numbers; a hint says where to download free premium voices. Server: `GET /v1/me/coach-voices` (voices + lines), `GET /v1/me/coach-voices/:voice/:line.mp3` (recorded once per voice with Polly, kept on the volume under `coach-voices/`); Polly uses the SES IAM keys unless `POLLY_ACCESS_KEY_ID`/`POLLY_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY` are set (`POLLY_REGION`, default eu-central-1; `POLLY=off` disables). Tests `coachVoices.test.ts`, GymCore `studioKindsMatchTheServerGroups`.
- **Exercise position guide in 3D** (#399): members couldn't see the right position for an exercise from every side. Design A + B from `exercise-positions/`: **Position guide** on the exercise page and in the player's **Form** sheet opens the exercise's **Start / Middle / End** positions on a 3D clay figure (headband head, worked muscles tinted, equipment around it) that turns with a drag or **Side · ¾ · Front · Back** snaps. Numbered pins mark the technique's body-position checkpoints (matching colour cue cards below), arcs measure the two joints that move most with the range that counts as right ("Knee 85°", 75–95°), and **Show the common mistake** draws the exercise's first drawable "Avoid" item (knees caving, rounded or arched back, hips sagging or too high, elbows flared, shrugging, head forward, half range) as a red ghost over the right position. Data: `ios/Tools/illustrations/positions.py` lifts the animation's own key poses to 3D (434 exercises, `ExercisePositions.json.z`, 179 KB) with real stance width (squat, hinge, jump, carry, throw) and barbell / pull-up grip width, and checks every key pose for a knee bent backwards (4 reviewed exceptions). Demo screens `position`, `position-mistake`, `position-press`; GymCore `ExercisePositionTests`.
- **Apple Watch lift sensor (beta)** (#383): the Watch counts reps and measures bar speed with its motion sensors, like a clip-on velocity sensor but with no extra hardware. Design A + B from `watch-gpath/proposals.png`. During a loaded set the Watch shows a **big rep count** (counted automatically, the Digital Crown corrects it), the **speed of the last rep** (m/s) with its zone (Max strength / Strength / Power / Speed / Explosive) and one dot per rep. When the reps slow down past the member's limit it buzzes twice and shows **Rack it**. Once the bar is racked (no rep for 4.5 s) the set **logs itself**: a lime bar counts down 4 s with **Undo** (the set goes on), then rest starts as usual; the rest screen shows "8 reps · best 0.71 m/s · −21 %" and heart rate. Works on lifts where the wrist moves with the load (squat, bench, deadlift, presses, rows, pulldowns, curls, raises, cables). **Machine mode** (design A + B from `watch-gpath/machine-proposals.png`) covers lifts where the wrist stays still (leg press, leg extension and curl, calf raises, hip thrust, pull-ups, dips, abductor / adductor): the Watch asks to be strapped on the moving part ("Strap me on the shin pad"), waits for it to be still, learns the direction from one slow test rep, then counts and measures like on the wrist; the rest screen reminds to put it back on the wrist when the next lift needs it. **Log by hand** skips it for that exercise. While the iPhone runs the workout and the Watch tracks it, the Watch measures too: the counted reps fill the iPhone set card's reps, a lime **⌚ 7 reps · 0.62 m/s** pill shows on the card, and racking the bar completes the set on the iPhone. iPhone **Settings → Lift sensor (beta)**: Off / On, no stop buzz / buzz at 10 % (power), 20 % (strength, default) or 30 % (muscle) slower. Measured sets are kept on the iPhone only (wiped on sign-out); the exercise page shows a **Bar speed** card (speed per rep of the last measured day, speed loss, range of motion, lowering time, power, e1RM from load and speed over 30 days). Maths in the new `LiftSensor` package (rep detection with drift correction, test-rep axis, load–velocity 1RM) with `swift test` in CI. Watch demo screens `lift`, `lift-stop`, `lift-log`, `strap`, `testrep`. Accuracy needs real-Watch calibration: the Watch app has not run on a device yet.
- **Ask Coach: chat for today's workout** (#381): a member couldn't just say what they want today. Design A from `workout-chat/proposals.png`: an **Ask Coach for today's workout…** bar in the Coach card on Train (a lime chat button in the card's corner on short phones, so Start workout stays on screen) opens a full-screen chat with Coach Monkey. The member types or dictates ("45 min, legs, sore shoulder") or taps a starter; Coach answers in a line and the workout lands in the chat as a card (length, first exercises, **Start now** / **Add to day** / **Tweak** in the builder), with quick chips after it (Shorter, Harder, Easier, No barbell, Swap the first exercise). The server (`GET/POST /v1/me/coach-chat`) asks Claude Opus 5.5 (low effort, structured output, server-side refusal fallback) only for generator settings: minutes, muscle groups, rest, grouping, and groups / equipment / exercise-name words to avoid; the app's muscle generator builds the workout from catalog exercises that fit the member's gym and level (new `MuscleWorkoutOptions.avoidGroups/avoidEquipment/avoidKeywords`, `CoachWorkoutSettings`). Only chat text and training settings are sent (no name, email or id); the conversation stays on the device for the day. Pro (`generateWorkout` gate, open while subscriptions are off; server answers 402 once enforced). Daily cap per member `COACH_CHAT_DAILY_LIMIT` (default 20; failed calls don't count). Off until the owner sets the `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` Fly secret (chat then says it isn't switched on). Privacy policy lists Anthropic. Demo screens `coach-chat`, `coach-chat-empty`.
- **Workout coach in the player** (#379): nothing motivated the member during a workout. Design A + C from `workout-coach/proposals.png`: Coach Monkey from Train follows into the player. **Voice** (A): lines spoken over the music (ducks it, iOS speech), a 🐵 pill in the header glows while it talks (tap = mute for this workout) and a short subtitle bubble shows over the picture. **Big moments** (C): a record burst over the exercise card (new best set: heavier than ever or higher estimated 1RM, once per exercise, tap or 3 s closes it), a **🔥 Final set** badge in the set card's source-chip slot, plus start, halfway and finish calls, each once. Lines are scripted on the device from the member's own sets (vs the same set last time), in three styles (`CoachLines`, WorkoutEngine): works offline, no delay, nothing leaves the phone. New Settings → **Workout coach**: on/off (default on), style **Chill / Hype / Drill**, voice **Off / Key moments / Every set** (every set adds the vs-last-time line after each set and "10 seconds" before a rest of 30 s or more ends), haptics on key moments, Apple Watch (the mirrored workout shows the line for 6 s with a tap), **Hear a sample**. Nothing moves in the player layout (#373): the coach only overlays. Demo screens `player-coach`, `player-record`, `coach-settings`; UI test `CoachUITests`; GymCore `CoachTests`.
- **Usual workout time** (#377): the plan didn't know when members train. Design C from `workout-time/proposals.png`: **Training profile → Workout time** is its own card (sunrise / sun / moon, the time large, "USUALLY"); its editor shows the time big on a gradient that follows the part of the day, with a slider from 05:00 to 22:00 in 15-minute steps and **It varies** to clear it. Before 11:00 counts as morning: the automatic warm-up starts with one more minute of easy cardio (#375 rules, still capped so the drills fit). Not set = plans as before. Saved with the training profile.
- **Lock screen and Dynamic Island: log sets and follow the workout** (#371): the Live Activity showed the set and rest countdown, but logging a set meant unlocking the phone, timed sets and the **Next** countdown didn't show, and the card vanished at the finish. Design B + C from `live-activity/proposals.png`: the lock-screen card and the expanded Dynamic Island show the same effort tiles as the player (0 · 1 · 2 · 3 · 4+): one tap logs the coming set with its effort and starts the rest, without unlocking; **−** / **+** beside kg × reps change the weight by the stepper's step. Timed work and other items get the player's lime button (START / PAUSE / NEXT / FINISH); timed sets and the Next countdown count down like rest. A session strip on both shows one bar per exercise (block colour, filled as sets are done), exercise n/N, workout time, live heart rate when an Apple Watch tracks the workout (sent every 10 s), kg lifted and the done-by time. Compact island: set n/N and the countdown, heart rate or exercise n/N. When the workout is finished the card turns into a summary (minutes, exercises done, kg lifted or sets, new PRs = exercises above their previous heaviest weight) and stays on the lock screen for 15 minutes. +15 s / Skip rest and the "Rest over" buzz are unchanged.
- **Climbing gym: Wall session and climbing exercises** (#366): MonkeyGrade's climbing warm-up and cool-down drills were missing here, and neither app had hangboard work. Design A + C from `climbing-exercises/proposals.png`. **Train → Climbing** now shows a **Wall session** card (replaces the climbing-day add-on, #47; on by default, switch in **Around climbing days**): four day tiles (**Bouldering**, **Hangboard**, **Rope day**, **Rest day**; last pick remembered) choose the bands below them, **Before** (wall warm-up), **Climb** (opens MonkeyGrade when connected, else Log climb; ticks when a climb is logged or synced today) or a **Hangboard** session (max hangs, 7/3 repeaters, open-hand hang, pinch block), and **After** (antagonists + forearm, shoulder and chest stretches).
