Each band starts on its own; a lime button starts the first. **Explore → Climbing** lists 25 exercises in Wall warm-up / Hangboard / Wall cool-down; drills with the same name in MonkeyGrade carry an **MG** tag (and "Same drill in MonkeyGrade" on the exercise page). 12 new exercises with animations and technique (finger & forearm ramp, forearm wrist waves, band external rotation, hangboard ramp, recruitment pulls, easy traversing, max hangs, repeaters, open-hand hang, no-hang block lift, pinch block lift, wrist flexor floor stretch); library-only, so generated plans don't change. New equipment **Hangboard**, **Climbing wall**, **Lifting block** in a new **Climbing** gym area (not guessed for any gym type, not in the Fitnesspark Puls 5 inventory). MonkeyGrade itself is unchanged. Demo screens `wall-session`, `explore-climbing`, `wall-hangboard`, `exercise-climbing`.
- **Workout player on its side** (#362): the iPhone app only worked upright, so a phone resting sideways on a machine or rack was unusable. The workout player now turns with the phone; every other screen stays upright (`OrientationLock`, app delegate orientation mask; iPad unchanged). Landscape uses design C "cockpit" from `landscape-player/proposals.png`: coming-up list on the left (the #363 Coming up strip stood on end: what's left and the end time, header opens the Workout map, tap a row for setup and technique), the exercise card (compact) and its one input in the middle, and on the right a ring (rest countdown that closes by itself, else sets done and time on the current set between back / skip) with the effort tiles as SET DONE (#357), the optional rating, +15 s / Skip and the **Next** countdown bar. The superset chip row (**Split**, #352) and the machine setup strip / add chip (#355) show on the landscape card too. No overlay covers the screen in landscape; values, rest and timers carry over when turning. New UI test `LandscapePlayerUITests`.
- **Machine setup notes per gym** (#355): members can keep their settings for a machine (seat, pin, pad…) at the gym they train in. Design B + C from `machine-notes/proposals.png`: in the player, a **Setup** chip on the exercise card opens a sheet with gradient quick-field tiles (name + value, presets Seat / Pin / Pad / Back / Handle / Foot plate / ROM / Height / Notch, up to 8), a note and one photo (camera or library). Once saved, the card shows the values as a strip (values that don't fit are dropped; the picture shrinks so the player still never scrolls) with a photo thumbnail that opens the photo full screen with the note. Saved per gym (a trip's gym on travel days) and exercise; the exercise page and Form sheet list **My setups** for that exercise and its machine at every gym, with **Copy here** / **Use** to reuse a setup where the same machine stands. Private: `GET/POST /v1/me/machine-notes`, `DELETE /v1/me/machine-notes/:id`, `PUT/DELETE /v1/me/machine-notes/:id/photo` (JPEG, metadata stripped), `GET /v1/me/machine-notes/photos/:id` (owner only, `Cache-Control: private`); in the data export (photos inline), deleted with the account, in member restore. Photos are kept on the device in their own files (data protection), removed on sign-out. Demo screens `player-setup`, `machine-setup`, `exercise-setups`.
- **Split a superset into straight sets** (#352): a superset whose machines aren't next to each other could not be separated. In the workout player the superset line is now a chip row, **Superset A1 + A2** and a lime **Split**: one tap turns the group into straight sets from the current exercise (its sets first, then the others; a member already done is passed over), with an Undo banner. Tapping **Superset A1 + A2** opens **Machines not next to each other?** (design A + C from `split-superset/proposals.png`): split for today, swap the partner (if no set of it is done yet; the swap list shows what's near), or set / edit gym zones (saved at once, the plan follows when the workout ends so the running one keeps its list; home gym only). Saved as this workout's edit, so the session page, history and the Watch follow, and a finish-early fit splits too. **Edit exercises** shows **Split** on a group's first exercise before starting. Removing an exercise from a pair also drops the stale "Superset: …" note from the one left.
- **Change a planned workout's focus** (#342): a member could swap single exercises but not what a workout trains. Design A + C from `workout-focus/proposals.png`: under the workout's title, **Change focus** opens the 14 session types (same as Goal → week strip, with the muscles each trains and a warning next to climbing days), for **This workout** (this week only; synced per session id like per-workout styles, dropped on a new week; the other workouts stay exactly the same) or **Every week** (becomes the plan's per-day pick, #200; not offered while a programme runs). **Swap day** lists this week's other workouts from today on, not done (past days stay as they are; nothing here starts a workout); picking one brings it to this day and moves this workout to its day (the #113 one-off move). "Something else" opens Change focus. Both show the Undo bar; exercise edits made on the old focus are set aside and come back with Undo. Suggested What's new card (written by the release owner). Demo screens `focus-sheet`, `swap-day`.
- **Apple Watch Ultra Action button** (#349): design C "Smart next" from `watch-action-button/proposals.png`. Pick MonkeyWorkout in Watch Settings → Action Button → Workout: the first press starts **Next up** on the Watch (`StartWorkoutIntent`; tracks the iPhone workout instead when one runs). Later presses during the workout run the donated `ActionNextIntent`: **Smart next** (default) logs the set, skips rest, starts/pauses a timed set, moves to the next exercise or finishes; **Log set** always counts the set; **Rest timer** starts/ends a rest countdown (sets logged on screen). A lime label + haptic confirms each press. While tracking an iPhone workout a press is the iPhone's main button (closes rest, advances, or completes the set). Action + side button pauses/resumes (`PauseWorkoutIntent` / `ResumeWorkoutIntent`); the Watch Pause tile follows it. New Settings → **Action button** (iPhone): Smart next / Log set / Rest timer. Only verifiable on a real Apple Watch Ultra.
- **Watch offers to start an iPhone workout** (#347): starting a workout on the iPhone did nothing on the Watch unless its app was already open. The iPhone now wakes the Watch app (`HKHealthStore.startWatchApp`) with a haptic and a start prompt in the Watch style (Full colour / Tiles / Game mode). Design A + B from `watch-start-prompt/proposals.png`: the workout card with a lime **START ON WATCH** whose ring counts down 5 s (tick each second), then tracking starts by itself; tap to start now, **Not now** to skip. **Start on Watch** tracks heart rate and calories while following the iPhone player (supersets included) with its controls; the Watch saves the one Apple Health workout and sends heart rate back (the iPhone saves it itself if the Watch doesn't answer within 3 min, or if tracking is stopped). Phone pause pauses tracking (dropped after 30 min); discard discards it. New Settings → **Start on Apple Watch**: Ask on Watch (default) / Start automatically / Never. Watch demo screen `phone-start`. Wake-up and tracking are only verifiable on a real Watch.
- **Daily database backups and a restore path** (#310): there was no backup job and no restore procedure, only Fly's automatic volume snapshots, unchecked. The API now writes `/data/backups/app-YYYY-MM-DD.db` once a day (`VACUUM INTO`, checked with `quick_check` before it is kept, files 0600, newest 7 kept; `backend/src/backup.ts`, `BACKUP_DIR`/`BACKUP_KEEP_DAYS`). Restore: a backup is staged as `restore.db` and swapped in on the next start, the replaced database kept as `backups/pre-restore-<time>.db`; a corrupt copy is rejected and the live database left alone. New **Fly backups** workflow: `status`, `backup-now`, `snapshot-now`, `verify` (restore drill, changes nothing), `retention`, `restore` (needs the app name as confirmation), and a daily 04:30 UTC check that fails when an app has no volume snapshot from the last 36 h. Dependabot for backend npm packages and GitHub Actions.
- **Encrypted off-site backup copy** (#313, Fly Tigris picked by the owner): after each daily backup the API gzips it, encrypts it (AES-256-GCM, key from the `BACKUP_KEY` passphrase via scrypt; the provider only stores ciphertext) and uploads it to a Tigris bucket (`<app>-backups`), deleting copies older than 30 days (`backend/src/offsite.ts`, SigV4 reused from SES). **Fly backups** workflow: `offsite-setup` (creates the bucket, sets `BACKUP_KEY` from the GitHub secret), `offsite-list`, `offsite-fetch` (download + decrypt into `/data/backups`, then `verify` / `restore`).
- **Restore or delete one member's data on request** (#315): the database restore (#310) rolls back every member, so a support request ("my workouts are gone") had no answer. **Fly backups** → `member-restore` (email + backup day): dry run lists that member's rows missing or changed since the backup; apply puts back only the missing ones (`overwrite` also replaces changed rows, `profile` the training profile) with a fresh `updated_at` so devices pick them up, saves a JSON of what it did, and never touches other members; a deleted or re-created account is refused (`backend/src/admin/restoreMember.ts`). `member-delete` deletes an account for a member who can't use the app (dry run lists their rows; apply needs the email typed again; same path as Account → Delete account) (`admin/deleteMember.ts`). Privacy Policy (**Your rights**: restoring lost data, deletion by email) and Support page (**Some of my data is missing**, deletion by email, backup expiry) say how to ask.
- **Privacy Policy updated** (2026-10-15): new section **How we protect your data** (HTTPS, encrypted disk, hashed/encrypted secrets, on-device cache with iOS Data Protection and offline sync, account security, access, daily backups: 7 days on the server, disk snapshots up to 14 days, encrypted Tigris copy 30 days); Tigris Data added to service providers; climbing sessions listed with training data; backup retention wording; support page lists everything in the data export.
- **Climbs are saved to the server** (#312): logged climbs lived only on the phone (lost on sign-out or reinstall, missing from backups and the data export). New `GET/POST /v1/me/climbs`, `DELETE /v1/me/climbs/:id` (upsert by client id, owner-checked, deleted with the account, in `/v1/me/export`); the app queues climbs like PR attempts and uploads climbs logged before this once.
- **Finish a workout early** (#300): a member who had to leave early could only stop, or skip exercises one by one and lose the cool-down. Designs A + C from `finish-early/proposals.png`: tap the clock in the player's top bar, or pause → **Short on time? Finish early**. Coach Monkey offers three ready plans with their end time (**Express**: main lift + short cool-down; **Balanced**: accessories kept with fewer sets; **Wrap up now**: finish this exercise, then cool-down), or the member's own **10′ 15′ 20′ 30′ 45′** chips or **Done by** time; a list shows each exercise left as **Kept**, **Shorter**, **Less rest** or **Skip** before **Fit to 15 min · done 19:45**. After the fit the clock counts down in a pink capsule to the end time, a banner ("Done by 19:45 · 2 skipped · 3 sets less") offers **Undo** for 8 s, and the sheet has **Back to the full workout**. Engine `FinishEarly` (`WorkoutEngine/FinishEarly.swift`, unit tested): done sets and the current exercise stay, items behind the member count as done; trim order mobility → cardio (shorter, then skipped) → warm-up not started → rest between sets −25 % (min 45 s) → accessory sets to 2 → cool-down down to 3 min (flush and later stretches go; first stretches and breathing stay) → accessories skipped → accessory sets to 1 → main lift sets to 2 → 1. Supersets and circuits change together. The fit survives pause/resume (`ActiveWorkout.fitted/fitEnd`); Live Activity, Watch mirror and the saved record follow it. Demo screens `finish-early`, `player-fitted`.
- **Home Screen and Lock Screen widgets** (#297): MonkeyWorkout had only a Live Activity, nothing to put on the Home Screen. Now there is one **MonkeyWorkout** widget in **Small**, **Medium** and **Large** plus Lock Screen sizes (circle, rectangle, inline), in three looks from `home-widgets/proposals.png`, all built: **Gradient cards** (Explore style: icon tile, big number, vivid gradient), **Monkey coach** (the monkey with its mood and rank gear, level, XP bar, weekly chest and a nudge) and **Data tiles** (dark tiles, colour-coded numbers). Topics: **Next up** (with **Start**), **This week**, **Streak**, **Readiness**, **Level + XP**, **Top lifts**, **Last workout**, **Volume** (4 weeks + trend), **Climbing** and the large **Week dashboard** (week strip, next up, streak, top lift). Tapping opens the session, Train or Progress (`monkeyworkout://` links). **Account → Home Screen widgets** (design 3 from `home-widgets/config.png`): presets **Classic**, **Coach Monkey**, **Data nerd** and **Climber** with live previews, and **Customise sizes** (look + topic for Small, Medium, Large and Lock Screen, with a preview). Each widget's own **Edit Widget** can override topic and look. The app writes `home-widgets.json` to the App Group (`HomeWidgetSync`, same changes that refresh the Watch) and reloads widgets only when it changed; widgets also turn over at midnight. Code in `ios/HomeWidgets/` (shared by the app and the widget extension) and `MonkeyWorkoutWidgets/HomeScreenWidgets.swift`; the iPhone app and the widget extension now carry the App Group (registered in the developer portal on 2026-10-14). CI checks the extension and App Group keys are in the build; demo screens `home-widgets`, `home-widgets-custom`, `home-widgets-gradient`, `home-widgets-coach`, `home-widgets-data`.
- **The app learns what each member likes** (#299): swapping an exercise away, removing one before starting, or passing over it in a workout now teaches the app that the member doesn't like it; swapping one in, adding it and finishing all its sets teach the opposite. Each exercise gets an explainable score (`MemberTaste`, `Taste.swift`): swap in +1, swap out −1 (kept swaps ±1.5), added +1, removed −1, skipped −0.5 (only when a later exercise was done, so stopping early says nothing), finished +0.15 (capped); signals fade with a 60-day half-life, undoing an edit cancels it. Plans use it from the next new week (frozen per week in `SyncedProfile.weekTaste`, so the week in progress never reshuffles): liked exercises get up to +0.9 in the generator's pick score (variety still comes first), disliked ones down to −3 (two swaps away already outweigh freshness) and lose a template's favourite bonus; quick sessions and plan variations follow it too, variations leave disliked exercises out when anything else fits. Learned data syncs with the profile through the backend. Nothing learned = plans exactly as before (unit tested, `TasteTests`). Design A + C from `member-learning/proposals.png`: **What Monkey learned** (Training profile, last card) lists **You like** and **You avoid** with the reason ("Swapped out or removed 2× · skipped 1×") and a liked/avoided bar; tap one for **Forget this**, **Never suggest again** (out of every plan when anything else fits, never fades) or **Actually, I like it**, which apply to the rest of this week at once; **Learn from my choices** switch (off: nothing recorded, plans ignore it) and **Reset** with Undo. On Train, a new week made with learned tastes shows a coach card: "New week, made for you! I left out 2 moves you keep swapping and added more of what you like." with each change (**OUT** Leg press → Hack squat, **MORE** Incline DB press, 2 workouts), its reason and **Undo** (forgets that exercise and puts this week back), **Got it** and **What Monkey learned ›** (`TasteRecap`, `WeekPlan.tasteChanges`). Demo screens `taste`, `train-taste`.
- **Customise warm-up and cool-down** (#293): in **New workout** (Session flow) and **Quick session**, warm-up and cool-down were always auto-picked; only their length could be dragged. Design A + C from `warmup-cooldown/proposals.png`. In New workout, the **Warm-up ›** and **Cool-down ›** chips now open a sheet (Leave out is in the sheet); Quick session gets **Warm-up** and **Cool-down** cards under Muscle focus. The sheet has **style tiles** (warm-up: Auto, Pulse raiser, Dynamic, Activation; cool-down: Auto, Stretch, Foam roll, Easy cardio, Breathe) and the block's **moves**: swap, remove, add a move (picker opens on warm-up drills or stretches) or change a move's minutes, which turns the block into your own moves (their time sets the block's length; **Back to style** undoes it). **Use as my default** saves the style or moves to the profile (`Profile.warmupStyle` / `cooldownStyle`, own moves as the block's only picks), so new workouts and the plan use them. Per-workout choices are kept on the workout (`CustomWorkout.flowTweaks`) and applied through `Profile.applying(_:)`; quick sessions take them in `Generator.quickSession(…, tweaks:)`. Quick sessions for picked muscles now also get a warm-up and cool-down (4 + 3 min up to 20 min, else 5 + 4, taken from the duration). Auto = plans exactly as before (unit tested). Demo screens `flow-warmup`, `flow-cooldown`, `quick-flow`.
- **Edit a workout's exercises before starting** (#295): only exercises of the generated plan could be swapped; added library workouts, quick sessions, extras, example and shared workouts were fixed lists, and no workout could drop, add or reorder exercises. Design B from `edit-before-start/proposals.png`: the **Exercises** tile (pencil) and a lime **Edit** button above the exercise list open **Edit exercises**, a compact sheet grouped by block: drag ≡ to reorder, red − to remove (rest-time drills go with their exercise, a superset left with one exercise ends, letters renumber), a − sets + stepper, ⇄ swap (same Swap sheet, picks for this workout), **Add to <block>** (exercise picker, starting prescription) and **Back to the workout as planned**; **Save · 66′ · 18 exercises** shows the new length; closing with changes shows the Close + Undo bar (#285). Works for plan days, extras, quick sessions and library workouts; this workout only (plan days for this week, a library workout until it's finished), locked once started. The session page shows **Edited** with **Reset**; card Swap on a library workout or an edited one changes that workout's list. Edits sync with the profile (`SyncedProfile.sessionEdits`, `SessionEdit`; a re-planned session drops its edit). Engine: `SessionEdit.swift` (unit tested). Demo screens `edit-exercises`, `session-edited`.
- **Close + Undo** (#285): closing a screen with unsaved input (X, Cancel, swipe down or back) lost it without a word. Now the screen still closes at once, and a gradient bar (design A from `cancel-confirm/toasts.png`: icon tile, what was closed and what was in it, lime **Undo** in a 6 s countdown ring) brings it back with every value. Untouched screens close silently; saving never shows it. Covers New programme, Edit programme / Make it mine, Build your own exercise, Exercise picker, Gym setup, Log a missed workout, PR attempt (once tries are logged), Log measurements, Away period, Log a climb, Training profile sections, Body & Health, Community profile, Share a win, 2-step setup / new recovery codes (one-time codes come back so they can be saved) and Change email (code sent). The bar sits in its own window above sheets, swipe down to hide it, VoiceOver announces it with an Undo action. `UndoClose.swift` (`UndoOffer`, `CloseGuard`, `.undoableClose`); demo screen `undo-bar`. Audit in `cancel-confirm/audit.md`.
- **New workout: Cancel did nothing** (#285): after Generate, Cancel on New / Edit workout tried to show a "Discard changes?" dialog that never appeared on iOS 26, and swipe-down was blocked, so the sheet couldn't be closed. It now closes at once and shows the same Undo bar.
- **Gym zones: supersets stay in one spot** (#274): members can group their gym's equipment by where it sits, so a superset or circuit no longer sends them across the gym. Designs A + B from `gym-zones/proposals.png`. **Equipment page**: a **Group by where it sits** card starts from the usual layout (Free weights, Machines, Functional, Cardio, Stretch); pick a zone chip and tap pictures to move them there (each picture shows its zone's colour dot); **+ Zone**, rename / recolour / delete (touch and hold a chip), back to the usual layout or zones off. **Floor plan** (optional, `FloorPlanSheet`): drag zone blocks onto a rough plan and resize them; blocks that touch count as next to each other. **Workouts**: supersets and circuits only pair exercises from one zone or neighbouring zones (exercises needing only mat, bands and other carried items fit anywhere), groups in the same zone follow each other after the main lift, letters stay A, B, C in order; same for Generate-and-do-today workouts. Session cards show a zone tag; the player's Up next says **Same spot** or **Walk to <zone>**; Swap lists moves near the rest of the group first, tagged **here**, and warns when one would split the group. Zones are kept per saved gym (`GymLayout`, `Profile.gymLayout`, `SavedGym.layout`; a redo in swipes keeps them) and don't apply on trips. No zones = plans exactly as before (unit tested). Demo screens `equipment-zones`, `floor-plan`, `session-zones`, `swap-zones`, `player-zones`.
- **Programmes: every workout, your length, 2 more, and your own** (#268, #269), after build 32 feedback. **Every workout**: a programme's page only listed its signature workouts; it now has an **All workouts** grid (design B from `programmes/details-b.png`), one row per week and one cell per workout (signature workouts in the programme's colours, easy week green, test week gold, weeks done faded, this week outlined), built for the member's gym, days and focus; tap a cell for its exercises with sets × reps. **Length** (design C): **Start** opens **Pick your length** (Quick / Standard / Extended / Long haul, e.g. 6 / 8 / 10 / 12 weeks for Strength foundation): Base and Build stretch or shrink in the programme's ratio, Peak, Ease and Test stay one week each; a running programme shows **Length · Change**, the new length shapes the weeks from next week (lengths that would end it now are off) so nothing done this week is lost. **2 more programmes**: **Athletic engine** (6 weeks, 3×, jump + trap-bar day and a kettlebell / slam circuit, tests trap-bar deadlift and swing) and **Fresh start** (4 weeks, 3×, for new or returning members, full-body basics, tests goblet squat, dumbbell bench and lat pulldown). **Your own programme** (designs A + B from `programmes/custom-*.png`): **Make it mine** on any programme copies it into **My programmes** and opens its editor (name, length chips, sessions, goal, split, method, signature workouts to remove or add from My workouts or the library, test lifts); **New programme** in Explore → Programmes is a 4-step builder (what for, how long and how often, a workout or Auto per day with **Heavier as the phases go**, test lifts; no lifts = ends with an easy week). A member's programme runs, pauses and finishes like the others and keeps running from its saved copy if edited or deleted; **Edit** on its page. Stored in the synced profile (`customProgrammes`; the running one in `programme.custom`, its length in `programme.length`; older profiles decode as before); no server change. Engine: `Programme.lengths`, `resized(to:)`, `previewWeek`, `phases(weeks:test:)`, `defaults(for:)`, `copy()`, `ProgrammeSignature.from(...)` (strength / power exercises of a workout, with phase progression), programmes are `Codable` (unit tested). Demo screens `programme-length`, `programme-grid`, `programme-grid-running`, `programme-mine`, `programme-builder`, `programme-builder-week`. What's new `2026-10-14.1` (4 cards; `2026-10-13.4` moved to the history as build 32).
- **Customisable Train screen** (#260): Train was the same for every member. Designs A + B from `train-widgets/proposals-full.png`: Train is now made of widgets on a 2-column grid; today's screen stays the default for everyone (Quick actions, Coach monkey or Level bar per game look, Week + readiness, Next up, Climbing for climbers, This week). The new grid button next to **LV** opens **Edit Train**: widgets wiggle, **−** removes one, the lime corner handle cycles its size (**Small** half width, **Wide**, **Large**), the white **Look** menu switches its look, drag a widget onto another to move it; **Add widget** opens a gallery of 14 widgets, **List** opens **Customise Train** (reorder with handles, size and look per widget, add, swipe to remove, **Reset to default**). One-tap presets: **Classic** (default), **Minimal**, **Data nerd**, **Climber**. New widgets, each opening its screen when tapped: **Calendar** (looks: week strip, next 4 weeks grid, agenda; planned, done, climbing, can't-train and today marked; opens the training calendar), **Streak**, **Training load** (kg lifted, minutes or sets this week with the 8-week trend and change vs last week), **Records**, **Body weight** (latest and 4-week change), **Muscles worked** (28-day body map, front and back), **Last workout**; Progress widgets switch to the Progress tab and scroll to their section. Small versions of Coach monkey, Week + readiness, Level, Next up (with Start) and Climbing (log a climb); This week's sessions as a list or the Quest map. The **Programme** card (#255) is a widget too (shows while a programme runs; in the default layout after Coach monkey). With Quick actions removed, a **⋯** menu next to LV keeps Calendar, Variation and Edit plan. Layout saved with the synced profile (`SyncedProfile.trainLayout`, nil = default, so the default keeps following the game look); unknown widgets from a newer app are skipped. Engine: `TrainLayout`, `TrainWidget`, presets and row packing (unit tested). Demo screens `train-edit`, `train-gallery`, `train-customise`, `train-data`, `train-climber`, `train-minimal`, `train-looks`. What's new `2026-10-13.4`.
- **Training programmes** (#255): members can follow a multi-week programme toward one goal (design C hybrid + integration A with pause and a Progress section, from `programmes/` in project files). **Explore → Programmes** (4th tab) lists **Strength foundation** (8 weeks, 3×: Base 5×5 → Build triples → Peak doubles → Ease → PR test on squat, bench, deadlift), **Muscle builder** (6 weeks, 4×, upper / lower, Volume method, ends with 8-rep tests) and **Climber's power** (6 weeks, 2× around climbing days, back + core focus, weighted pull-up and row tests). A programme page shows the phase bar, phase notes, its **signature workouts** (pinned each week: the generated session of the closest type keeps its warm-up and finishers, its strength block becomes the signature's exercises with the phase's sets × reps; exercises the gym lacks are swapped for the closest one it has), what it locks and the finish line. **Start** replaces the plan from this week: goal, split, method and focus come from the programme (locked in Training profile, shown with a lock), days, gym, time and climbing stay the member's; one programme week = one plan week, built as the matching cycle week (Base / Build / Peak / Deload) with a fresh seed each week. The member's own plan is saved and comes back on **Pause** (programme waits at that week, **Resume** picks it up), **Leave** or finish. Train shows a programme card (week n of N, phase, weeks bar with the test week in gold) above the week card, whose week bar no longer switches weeks while a programme runs; the test week lists **Attempt** buttons for each lift (PR attempt screen). Calendar shows the phase bar and plans the coming programme weeks. **Progress → Programmes** section (page map card + section bar chip): running programme with % done and lifts **start → now** (best estimated 1RM in the 8 weeks before the start vs since), finished and left programmes. Finishing shows a trophy sheet with the lifts. Stored in the synced profile (`programme`, `programmeHistory`); no server change. Engine: `Programme`, `ProgrammePhase`, `ProgrammeSignature`, `ActiveProgramme`, `ProgrammeRecord`, `ProgrammeLift` (unit tested); `PlanCalendar.weeks(future:)`. Demo screens `explore-programmes`, `programme`, `programme-running`, `train-programme`, `programme-test`, `progress-programmes`, `profile-programme`, `calendar-programme`, `programme-finished`; the CI screenshot job limit goes from 60 to 100 minutes for them and the security screens. What's new `2026-10-13.4` (the two library cards of #252 merged into one to stay at 5 cards).
- **Account security: confirm it's you, 2-step sign-in, passkeys** (#257): designs A + A from `account-security/proposals.png`. **App**: a **Confirm it's you** sheet (passkey, Continue with Apple, Email me a code, Current password, 2-step code; only the ways the account has) pops up when saving a password, an email, deleting the account or changing security settings, then the change goes through; the old current-password fields are gone. **Account → Security** card: Passkeys (add with Face ID, rename, remove), 2-step sign-in (QR code, Open in authenticator link, key, first code, 10 recovery codes to copy or share), Recovery codes (left, make new), Sign in with Apple (skips 2-step), Signed-in devices. Sign in gets **Sign in with passkey** and, when 2-step is on, a **2-step code** step (or a recovery code). Entitlement `webcredentials:` for both API hosts. Demo screens `account-security`, `account-reauth`, `account-2step`, `account-recovery`, `account-passkeys`, `signin-2step`. What's new `2026-10-13.4`. **Server**: setting or changing the password, changing the email, deleting the account and changing security settings now need a fresh confirmation, valid 5 minutes (`POST /v1/me/reauth` → `X-Reauth-Token`): passkey, a new Sign in with Apple, current password, 2-step code or a 6-digit code mailed to the verified email. Before, an account without a password (Apple sign-in) could set a password, move its email or be deleted with only a signed-in session. Older app builds keep working for members with a password (they send the current password). **2-step sign-in** (optional): authenticator app (TOTP, RFC 6238, secret stored encrypted, codes can't be replayed) with 10 one-time recovery codes; asked only after email + password sign-in (`403 mfa_required` + `POST /v1/auth/mfa`); Sign in with Apple and passkeys skip it. **Passkeys** (WebAuthn via `@simplewebauthn/server`): add, rename, remove (`/v1/me/passkeys`), sign in without email (`/v1/auth/passkey`); relying party `workout.monkeygrade.cloud` / `workout-staging.monkeygrade.cloud` (`WEBAUTHN_RP_ID`), `/.well-known/apple-app-site-association` lists the app for `webcredentials`. Email notice on every change (2-step on/off, passkey added/removed, recovery code used). `GET /v1/me/security` for the Security page. Expired grants, codes and challenges are purged.
- **10 more built-in workouts, goal filter and search in Explore → Workouts** (#252): the library had 2 built-in workouts. New: **Push power** (chest, shoulders, triceps), **Pull strength** (back, biceps), **Posterior chain** (glutes, hamstrings), **Dumbbell full body**, **Bodyweight anywhere** (no kit, circuits), **Machine starter** (beginner, machines), **Core crusher**, **HIIT circuit** (kettlebell, slams, rower rounds), **Climber's strength** (pulling plus antagonists and finger extensors) and **Mobility reset**, all catalog exercises, with warm-up and cool-down blocks, per-exercise rest, supersets/circuits and timed moves in seconds/minutes. Built-in workouts carry a goal (Muscle, Full body, Cardio, Core, Mobility), kit and level (shown on the workout page); a copy keeps warm-up/cool-down as session flow and the circuits. Explore → Workouts gets **goal chips** with counts and a **search field** matching name, any muscle or muscle group worked, goal or kit (also used by the full library's search). Fixed: card subtitles were cut ("7 exercises · Chest · Front…"); cards now show two lines, the muscles worked, then the facts (design A from `library-workouts/proposals.png`). What's new `2026-10-13.4`.
- **Level ladder: what each level brings** (#242): levels had no meaning beyond the number. Six ranks, same on iPhone and Watch (`WatchShared/GameLadder.swift`): **Rookie** L1–2 (lime headband monkey, confetti), **Regular** L3–7 (cap monkey, Regular title on the Community profile, bronze ring), **Iron** L8–14 (dumbbell monkey, fireworks, Iron app icon), **Steel** L15–24 (sunglasses monkey, silver ring, pick the XP colour for Train and Watch: lime, aqua, pink, sun, violet), **Titan** L25–39 (lightning monkey, gold ring, crown rain, Titan app icon), **Legend** L40+ (crowned monkey, animated ring, Legend app icon, Legend seal next to your name on Community posts). Levels unlock looks only; no training feature is locked by level. Progress gets a compact **level row** (design A from `iphone-gamification/levels-a.png`: rank monkey, level, rank, XP bar, XP to the next rank and its first unlock, badge count) replacing the trophy shelf / quest log / level card; tap → **Levels** sheet with every rank (levels, XP threshold, unlocks, locked/you/unlocked), **Share my level** card, app icon and colour pickers. Every level-up shows a full-screen celebration with the rank's effect on both devices. Every level display opens the ladder: the **LV** chip on Train, the level bar card, the session banner, the finish level card and, on the Watch, any level / XP bar (Watch **Levels** list). The monkey wears its rank gear on Train, finish screens and the Watch Monkey coach home. Server: `PUT /v1/community/me/level` stores the level; profile, member page, member search and post authors return `level` (backend test). Engine rank names follow the ladder thresholds (unit tested). Demo screens `game-ladder`, `game-levelup`, `game-levelup-legend`, `game-level-row`, Watch `levelup`. What's new `2026-10-13.3`.
- **All past updates in What's new** (#245): Account → **What's new** (card shows the number of updates) now lists every release as one timeline, newest first: the latest notes, then each earlier release under its day and build (13 Oct · Build 28 …), with the same gradient cards. History rebuilt from the notes shipped in TestFlight builds 19–30 into `ios/MonkeyWorkout/Resources/WhatsNewHistory.json` (`releases`: id, build, date, items). The page shown on first open after an update stays latest-only. Fixed: the last card was hidden under **Let's train** and the tab bar when opened from Account (Account now opens the timeline without the button; the first-open button sits on a solid bar). Release rule: starting new notes (new `id`) moves the last shipped notes on top of the history; `check_whats_new.sh` (CI, with tags, and the TestFlight workflow) fails otherwise.
- **Game looks on the iPhone, matched to the Watch style** (#227): the style picked for the Watch (#219) now also sets the iPhone's look, so both devices match; **Account → Style** (was Account → Apple Watch) shows the pair per card, and the Watch gear still changes both. **Monkey coach** (Watch Monkey coach / Game mode): the monkey on Train with the Watch's moods and tips (tap to hop and get the next one, streak and week chips), a dancing monkey with confetti when a workout ends, a trophy shelf in Progress. **Quest map** (Watch Crown deck / Full colour): Train's week list becomes a trail of stages (one per session: cleared, today glowing, upcoming), each with its XP and "bosses" (exercises), ending in the week's chest, which opens (+200 XP) when the weekly session goal is met; touch and hold a stage to move, add or remove as before; finish says **Stage cleared** or **Chest opened**; Progress shows a quest log (chests, stages this week). **Level bar** (Watch Charge & go / Tiles): level card with XP bar on Train, trophy + XP finish, a pink level card in Progress. In every look: **LV** chip next to the Train title, **+XP** on Next up and on each session page, and the finish screen shows XP gained, level-up, week bonus and new badges. **One game for both devices**: XP = 50 per workout + 5 per set + 200 per week with the goal met, 500 XP per level (`GameProgress`, `GameReward` in the engine, unit tested); badges are the iPhone's 21 tiered achievements on the Watch too (replaces the Watch-only 8 badges; Watch shows the earned ones and new ones on its Done screen once the phone syncs). Watch looks also gained the game layer: Crown deck day cards show XP and **CLEARED ✓**, its stats page the week chest; Charge & go shows the XP bar and a level stat in the rings; Full colour and Tiles Progress / Done show level, XP and new badges. Mascot drawing shared (`WatchShared/MonkeyFace.swift`). Demo screens `game-{week|session|done|progress}-{coach|deck|rings}` in CI screenshots (`-appStyle` launch argument). What's new `2026-10-13.2`.
- **Exercise movement plays as real motion** (#229): every exercise picture moved as 3 cross-faded stills (start / middle / end). It now plays 9 frames: the hand-drawn start, middle and end poses plus eased in-betweens, each fading over the last at the exercise's tempo. While it plays: the worked muscles heat from amber to full red towards the end pose; a dotted line traces the path of the bar / hand / foot (or hips for jumps) with a yellow dot riding it; a chip at the bottom left names the half being played with its tempo (lifting: working half 1 s in green, e.g. **UP 1s**, return 2 s in yellow, e.g. **DOWN 2s**; power: **UP FAST** / 1.4 s back; mobility, stretches, cardio and warm-ups move evenly without tempo), a bar filling over that half and the rep (**2/3**). Unchanged (#188): plays 3 times when a new exercise shows, tap replays 2, Reduce Motion = no auto-play. The exercise page strip still shows Start / Middle / End. **New head** (design 3 from `exercise-animation/heads.png`): a sculpted head (skull, jaw, nose, chin, ear) with a pink-to-orange sweatband replaces the bottle-shaped head and grey cap. **Coherence check of all 422 animations**: in-betweens bend each joint within its real range (no arm swinging through the body), pinned legs are kept above the floor (mountain climbers, spiderman push-ups), joints moving between different pins travel in a straight line, and hand-made in-between poses were added where interpolation could not show the move (power / hang clean second pull, Turkish get-up half-kneel, man maker squat clean); archer push-up now seen from a raised three-quarter view (was head-on, the body hidden behind the shoulders); burpee jump arms no longer clip the top edge; `ios/Tools/illustrations/check.py` checks every animation (jumps, stretching limbs, floor, picture bounds) and writes the motion table. Engine: `ExerciseMotion` (path, phase labels, tempo; generated table `ExerciseMotion+Table.swift`), `ExerciseIllustrations.keyFrames`. In-betweens are drawn at 540×405 (keys stay 720×540); illustrations grow from about 26 MB to about 65 MB. What's new `2026-10-13.1`.
- **3D body map on Explore** (#225): Explore → Muscles can show the body in 3D. A **2D | 3D** switch sits on the map (same setting in Account → Body & Health → **Body map**); 2D stays the default. The 3D figure is built from our own 2D body map art (no third-party model), for the member's M/F figure: the silhouette is inflated into a rounded body with muscle relief, front muscles on the front, back muscles on the back, coloured with this week's heat. Drag to turn, pinch to zoom, double-tap to reset, **Front / Side / Back** snaps; tap a muscle to light it up and show a card (name, main mover / assist, exercises, sets this week), tap the card to open the muscle page. Engine: `BodyMesh` (mesh + hit test via `BodyMapLayout`), SceneKit view `BodyMap3DView`, design A "heat clay" from project files `3d-body-map/`: matte dark figure, violet rim light. Demo screens `explore-3d`, `explore-3d-back`, `explore-3d-side`. What's new `2026-10-12.6`.
- **Help with every measurement** (#220): Log measurements gives help for each value (designs A + C from `measurement-help/`). **A**: a **?** on every card (body weight, body fat, each girth, each side when logging left and right) opens a how-to page: the member's body figure (M/F choice) with a glowing tape line where the tape goes (or standing on a scale for weight, calipers at the waist for body fat), 3 numbered steps and a tip. **C**: a lime **Measure with me** card at the top starts a guided walk-through, one measurement per screen: figure, steps, one big number (last value as placeholder and under it), **Next** / **Skip**, progress bar; values land in the sheet's fields, then **Save** as before. Neck hint now says "larynx (Adam's apple)". Steps/tips in `BodyMetric.guide`, tape positions from the body map outline (`BodyMapLayout.tapeBand`). Demo screens `measurement-howto`, `measurement-guide`. What's new `2026-10-12.6`.
- **Pick your Apple Watch home: Monkey coach, Crown deck, Charge & go** (#219): the Watch's first screen (was the Hero card) now comes in three styles, picked on the iPhone (**Account → Apple Watch**, gradient cards with a check) or on the Watch (gear top left on the home → **Home style**); both stay in sync, the latest change wins. **Monkey coach** (default): a cartoon monkey on a full-screen gradient whose face follows your week (party when the week is done), readiness (sleepy on "Take it easier") and streak (headband when fired up); it bobs and hops on open, tap it to hop and cycle tips in its speech bubble (pep talk for the next workout, streak, best lift to beat, sessions left this week) with the background colour following the tip; next workout with **START** below. **Crown deck**: one full-screen card per plan day in its colour, the Digital Crown flips through them (vertical pages, selection haptic), opening on the next workout; big lime play button to start, done days get a **DONE ✓** stamp that slams in, tap a card for its exercises, last page holds the stats. **Charge & go**: three gradient rings (week goal, streak vs best, readiness) sweep in on open (the week ring spins a lap once the goal is met); tap the middle to cycle next workout / week / streak / readiness, press and hold it: rings charge to full with a 3‑2‑1 countdown and haptics, then the workout starts (let go to cancel). All three keep the This week, Workouts, Streak and Progress rows below; Reduce Motion turns the animations off. **Every other Watch screen follows the pick** (This week / Workouts lists, workout detail and form cues, Progress, Done, iPhone mirror, Pro lock; workout player pages unchanged): Crown deck → **Full colour** (screen in the workout's colour, white glass cards, huge numbers), Charge & go → **Tiles** (black, Explore gradient tiles with icon tile left and big number right, 2×2 stat tiles on Progress), Monkey coach → **Game mode** (workouts as a quest path with numbered steps and the XP they earn, Progress shows level, XP bar and 8 badges (first workout, 10 / 50 workouts, iron week, on fire, 100 / 1,000 sets, PR hunter; locked ones greyed), Done has a dancing monkey with confetti, **+XP**, level-up and new-badge chips; the monkey's tips add level and XP to go). XP = 50 per workout + 5 per set, 500 XP per level, computed on the iPhone from the whole history and sent in the snapshot (`WGame`, `WBadge`); a workout finished on the Watch counts at once until the next sync. Snapshot fields `homeStyle` / `homeStyleChangedAt`, Watch event `homeStyle`. DEBUG Watch demo (`-demo -homeStyle coach|deck|rings -watchScreen list|detail|progress|summary|mirror|pro`) and best-effort Watch simulator screenshots in PR CI; iPhone demo screen `watch-home`. Mockups: project files `watch-home/proposals.png`, `watch-home/other-screens.png`. What's new `2026-10-12.6`.
- **My picks per block** (#204): optional pinned exercises for **Warm-up**, **Mobility**, **Cardio** and **Cool-down**, e.g. 7 min treadmill as every warm-up. **Edit plan → Session flow → My picks** opens a sheet per block: add up to 6 exercises, set minutes (1–30), **Only my picks** (block is just them) and **Also in my own workouts**. In a workout, long-press an exercise in one of those blocks → **Always use in …** / **Remove from my … picks**. Pins go first, keep their time and are skipped when the gym lacks the equipment; the rest of the block is auto-filled as before. Nothing pinned = plans unchanged (default). Engine: `BlockPick`, `BlockPicks`, `Profile.blockPicks`, `Generator.withPicks`. Demo screen `flow-picks`.
- **Choose your split, each day's focus and focus muscles** (#200): Training profile → Goal (and onboarding) shows a panel under the picked goal. **Split**: Auto (the goal's own split, plans unchanged), Full body, Upper / Lower, Push Pull Legs, Upper / Lower + Full, PPL + Upper / Lower, Body part (chest & triceps, back & biceps, legs, shoulders & arms, glutes & hamstrings), Arnold (chest & back, shoulders & arms, legs) and Strength + cardio; each repeats its day types to fill the sessions per week. **Your week**: a strip of the week's days (weekday names when gym or climbing days are set) in the day's colour; tap a day to pick what it trains from 14 session types (sheet with gradient tiles). Per-day picks show as **Custom**, stay on their days (no re-ordering, no weekly rotation) and reset when the goal or sessions per week change or a split is picked. **Focus muscles**: any number of Shoulders, Chest, Arms, Back, Core, Hips & glutes, Legs; their exercises get +1 set ("Focus muscle: +1 set") and come right after the main lift. Training profile hub shows the split next to the goal. Engine: `SplitStyle`, `Profile.split` / `splitDays` / `focusMuscles` (optional, older profiles decode), `Rules.split(profile)`, `Generator.weekPreview`, `Generator.dayFoci`, new `Focus` cases `chest-tri`, `back-bi`, `shoulders-arms`, `chest-back`, `posterior`; history stores the closest older session type so earlier app versions still read it, and unknown session types decode as full body. Demo screens `profile-goal`, `profile-goal-focus`, `day-focus` in CI screenshots.
- **Choose your session flow** (#198): pick which blocks a workout runs (Warm-up, Explosive, Strength, Mobility, Cardio, Cool-down) and how its time is split. **Edit plan → Session flow** sets it for every workout: presets **Full** (the goal's mix, unchanged), **Lift focus** (no explosive, no cardio), **Lift + mobility** (longer mobility), **Express** (warm-up + strength), block chips to add or remove a block, and a large time bar of a typical workout whose dividers drag to move minutes between neighbouring blocks (linked: the session length stays the same). A block removed gives its time to the remaining work blocks in proportion; a block added takes its minimum from strength; at least one work block stays. Conditioning days keep their cardio-led split under a dragged plan split. **On a workout**: Session flow → **Edit** opens the same bar and chips for that day, applied to **This workout** or **All workouts**, with **Back to plan flow**; exercises are re-picked for the new time. **Custom workouts** (builder, Generate from muscles, Add workout): a Session flow section adds warm-up, explosive, mobility, cardio and cool-down around your own exercises, picked for them and your gym; your exercises keep their time ("YOURS") and dragging beside them resizes the extra block. New workouts start with a warm-up and a cool-down. The flow is saved with the workout and kept when shared in the library, copied or added to a day. Plans without a flow are unchanged. Engine: `SessionFlow` (presets, `setting`, `sanitized`), `Profile.sessionFlow`, `SessionStyle.flow`, `Generator.flowMinutes` / `flowBlocks` / `referenceFocus`, `CustomWorkout.flow`. API: shared library workouts return `flow`. Demo screens `profile-flow`, `flow-session`, `flow-builder` in CI screenshots.
- **Apple Watch complications** (#190): 8 MonkeyWorkout complications, each available in every slot (circle, corner, rectangle, inline line), on any face including analog ones with numbers (Meridian, California, Utility). **Next workout** (today / rest day + next, minutes, exercises, plan week), **Weekly goal** (ring / corner gauge / lime pills), **Streak** (vs best), **Week strip** (Mon–Sun: done ✓, today, planned, missed), **Readiness** (Apple Health headline, green / amber / red), **Top lift** (best lifts, rotating hourly), **Last workout** (when, minutes, sets) and **My stat** (pick weekly goal, streak, next workout minutes or readiness). App colours on full-colour faces: filled Explore-style gradient cards with icon tile, gradient discs and rings; tinted faces fall back to one colour; Smart Stack shows a gradient card. Watch app shares the iPhone snapshot with the new widget extension `<app id>.watchkitapp.Complications` through App Group `group.<app id>` and reloads complications on every sync; hourly timeline, week resets on Monday. Tap opens the Watch app. Mockups: project files `watch-complications/` (v2 colours, v4 analog faces).
- **Exercise movement plays on its own** (#188): in the workout player and on the exercise page, the picture now plays the movement (start → middle → end → start) **3 times automatically** each time a new exercise shows, then stops on the start position (or the frame picked on the exercise page). Not an endless loop. **Play movement** (tap the picture) replays it twice; picking Start / Middle / End stops a running play. With iOS Reduce Motion on, nothing plays on its own. Replaces the tap-only rule from #170.
- **Body measurements** (#185): Account → Body & Health → **Measurements** (also from Progress). Quick log sheet, every value optional: body weight, body fat, and neck, shoulders, chest, waist, hips, upper arm, forearm, thigh, calf (arms and legs left/right if wanted), with how-to-measure tips and the last value as a hint. Each measurement has its own progression chart and entry list (tap to edit or delete); Progress shows the two biggest changes. **Apple Health**: reads and writes body weight, body fat, lean body mass and waist circumference (Health values merged into the charts without duplicates); other girths stay in the app. **Suggested weights**: for exercises never logged (and no related lift), lean mass from body fat (±15 %) and arm girth for arm work / thigh girth for knee-dominant leg work (±8 %) refine the first-time estimate; logged sets always decide, training methods and plans unchanged. Synced via API `/v1/me/body-measurements` (in export, removed with the account); privacy policy updated.
- **What's new page after each update** (#183): the first time the app opens after an update, a **What's new** page lists the release's top improvements as Explore-style gradient cards (icon tile, heavy title, one line each), closed with **Let's train**. Shown once per release; new accounts skip it (onboarding marks it seen); Account → Legal & support → **What's new** reopens it. Content is the bundled `ios/MonkeyWorkout/Resources/WhatsNew.json` (`id` + 1–5 items: SF Symbol, category tint, title, body). Release rule: every release writes new notes with a new `id`; CI validates the file (`.github/scripts/check_whats_new.sh`) and the TestFlight workflow fails when the `id` is unchanged since the last uploaded build (tagged `testflight-<build>` after each upload; `same_whats_new` input for rebuilds). First notes: the Add workout improvements (#178) and saved gyms / swipe setup (#180).
- **More exercises, machines, calisthenics and mobility from a big club's library** (#182): compared with the Fitnesspark app's exercise list (1,150 strength + 210 mobility names) and added what the catalog lacked: **76 exercises** (346 → 422) and **12 equipment items** (seated row machine, seated back extension machine, squat machine (pendulum / V-squat), belt squat, glute kickback machine, multi-hip machine, pullover machine, sandbag, sliders, BOSU / balance pad, massage ball, stall bars / pole), each in the Your gym setup areas with search names and a picture. **Calisthenics**: muscle-up (+ weighted), front lever and back lever (+ tuck progressions), human flag, iron cross (+ band-assisted), one-arm and archer pull-ups, one-arm / shoulder-tap / Spiderman / wall / deficit push-ups, pistol (+ weighted), free-standing and kipping handstand push-ups, bar static hold, dip-bar leg raise, monkey bar traverse, hanging shoulder circles. **Free weights / bodyweight**: thrusters (barbell, dumbbell, kettlebell), overhead squats, upright rows, sumo / kettlebell high pulls, man maker, kettlebell windmill and halo, dumbbell bench pullover, sit-ups, wall sit, bear crawl, donkey kick, frog pump; sandbag, slider and BOSU moves. **Mobility**: foam rolling for upper back, glutes, lats, hamstrings and adductors, massage-ball releases, hip windshield wipers, straddle stretch, downward dog, warrior 2, Brettzel, adductor rock-back, boat pose. All drawn in the 3-frame style with technique notes, library-only (plans unchanged) and folded under their base exercise where they are variations. **Rotate variations** (Training profile → Variety, off by default, not with Same every week): from week 2, accessories switch to another member of their family that fits your equipment and level (main lifts stay; a kept swap always wins).
- **Switch between your gyms, one item count, swipe setup from Equipment** (#180): Training profile → Your gym (also from Account) opens on the gym your plan is built for, with **your own list's count** everywhere (card, Equipment row, Account card; was 56 on the card = Puls 5's full inventory vs 48 in your list). When a scoped gym's full inventory has items your list lacks (e.g. added by the equipment audit after you picked it), a card names them with **Add all**. **Your other gyms · tap to switch**: switching keeps the previous gym saved with its own list (also when it was never set up, e.g. Puls 5 since onboarding), so switching back restores it; trips and holidays stay in the calendar. **Account → Your gym** shows your other gyms as one-tap chips to switch (with a confirmation; the week is rebuilt for that gym's equipment). **Set up in a few swipes** is now offered when picking any new gym, scoped ones included (first card: **Full inventory**, one tap), and from the selected gym's Equipment page (**Set up this gym in a few swipes**).
- **Add workout: custom workout, short library, quick session options** (#178): Train → Add workout gets a **Custom workout** card (builder with a blank workout, also Generate from muscles; **Add** saves it to your workouts and places it on the picked day; Pro like building workouts). **From your library** shows 3 workouts (yours newest first, then built-in) and **Browse library**: the full searchable library (mine, built-in, members) in pick mode, tap one to add it. Quick session tiles open an options step: **duration** 15 / 20 / 30 / 45 / 60 min (default 30) and **muscle focus** preset from the tile (e.g. Push = shoulders, chest, arms); keep the preset for the usual session with warm-up and cool-down at that length, or pick other muscles to get a strength workout for them sized to the duration (Pro, like Generate from muscles). Long-press a tile to add it at 30 min in one step. Engine: `Generator.quickSession(…, minutes:)`, `Generator.quickWorkout`, `Generator.quickDurations`, `Focus.muscleGroups`; quick sessions under 30 min get a short warm-up (2-min raise, 2 drills), 2 stretches and accessory work only, no heavy main lift with ramp-up, so 15 / 20 min really fit (plans unchanged). Demo screen `add-workout-quick` in CI screenshots.
- **Catalog extension and variation families** (#174): 131 exercises added (215 → 346) after an audit against standard gym libraries, all using existing equipment and drawn in the 3-frame style with technique notes. Missing staples (front raise, straight-arm pulldown, machine-assisted pull-up, dumbbell fly, triceps kickbacks, wrist curl, cable glute kickback, cable woodchop, Russian twist, bird dog, hanging leg raise, Turkish get-up, sumo deadlift, rack pull, power clean, push press, burpee, lateral lunge, incline / kneeling push-up…) and common variations (Smith / decline / floor bench, cable fly angles, rope pushdown, Bayesian / spider / concentration / Zottman curls, grip variants of pulldowns, pull-ups and rows, single-leg machines, box / Zercher / landmine squats, curtsy / forward lunges, cable abduction / adduction…). **Variations fold under their base exercise** (`Exercise.variationOf`, 141 links incl. existing exercises): Explore muscle and category lists and the workout builder show the base once with **+N variations** (the builder's chip opens them in place; search lists everything); the exercise page has a **Variations** section and a **Variation of …** link; **Swap exercise** offers the exercise's variations first. Generated plans and Generate from muscles are unchanged: the new exercises are library-only (`Exercise.libraryOnlyIds`), picked by members through swap, the builder or Explore.
- **Realistic exercise pictures** (#170): every exercise (215) now has the app's own picture in three frames (start, middle, end of the movement) instead of stock photos and stick figures: a shaded anatomical figure, the worked muscles in the load colours (primary red, secondary amber), the machine or equipment drawn in the scene (frames, pads, weight stacks that rise as you pull, cables and pulleys, bars and plates, dumbbells, benches, cardio machines), and a small front/back muscle map in the corner so hidden muscles stay clear. Exercise page: large picture with a **Start / Middle / End** strip to pick a frame; tap the picture to play the movement once (start → end → start, twice; Reduce Motion switches frames without the dissolve). Player and thumbnails use the same art; the player's next/up-next row shows the picture for every exercise. No more downloads from free-exercise-db (removed `ExerciseImages`, image cache); all art is bundled. Generator: `ios/Tools/illustrations/art.py` (renderer), `kit.py` (equipment), `scenes*.py` (poses + machines), `generate.py` (palette PNGs + `Exercise+Illustrations.swift` + `ios/EXERCISE_MEDIA.md`), `sample.py` (contact sheets). Custom exercises unchanged.
- **Visual muscle cards in Explore** (#163): the muscle list under the body map is now one gradient card per muscle (colour per body region, same as Generate from muscles) instead of grey rows. Each card shows a close-up of that muscle on your body figure (M/F), lit in its load colour from this week's plan (white when the plan doesn't load it), the muscle name, how many exercises train it, when you last trained it (Today, Yesterday, 3d ago…), and this week's sets on the right. On a Volume-progression plan the tracked muscles show hard sets against this week's target (e.g. 10/13) with a progress bar. Without a plan the sets are hidden. Group headers, the New exercise card and tap → muscle page are unchanged. Engine: `BodyMapLayout.focus(_:figure:)`. Demo screens `explore-list`, `explore-volume` in CI screenshots.
- **Bigger, easier muscle picker in the exercise builder** (#168): New / Edit exercise shows the front and back figures across the full width (was ~2/3, 240 pt high) and adds **Full screen**: one large figure with a Front / Back switch, legend and picked-muscle chips. Taps just outside a muscle pick the nearest one (`BodyMapLayout.muscle(at:…tolerance:)`), so small muscles are easy to hit; selection haptic. Header: round X top left and a lime **Save** button, no longer sharing one capsule. Demo screen `muscle-picker` in CI screenshots.
- **Gyms near me when picking your gym** (#165): onboarding, Training profile → Your gym and Account → Your gym get a **Gyms near me** button opening the map of gyms around you (until now only reachable from Calendar → Travelling); tap a gym, then **Use this gym**. Search by name works as before.
- **New gym in a few swipes, saved gyms, "Not here?" and gym memory** (#162). Setting up a gym (Training profile → Your gym, picking a gym found by search, or a trip in the calendar): pick the kind of place (Hotel gym, Home, Budget chain, Big club, CrossFit box, Park), then one swipe card per area of the gym (Barbells & racks, Dumbbells & bench, Cable tower, Machine circuit, Pull-up & dip bars, Functional zone, Cardio) instead of ticking items one by one; each card shows three pictures, what it covers, how many planned exercises use it and the usual answer for that kind of place, ordered by what your plan uses most. Yes / No / Not sure (swipe or tap); not-sure items stay on. The last step shows the item count and which planned exercises get another pick there, with **Fine-tune with pictures**. **Gym memory**: a gym other members already set up shows "Known gym · set up by N members" (items at least half of them have) or the chain's template from other branches, one tap to use; your list is shared as counts only, never who reported, and corrections replace your earlier answer. **Saved gyms**: every gym you set up keeps its own list; Your gym lists your other gyms to switch in one tap, and a trip can use a saved gym. **Not here?**: in the workout player (before the first set) and in Swap exercise, tell the app the gym lacks an item; it is removed from that gym (the trip's list on a travel day) and this week's remaining exercises there that need it are swapped for the best match, done sessions untouched. API: `PUT/DELETE /v1/gyms/reports`, `GET /v1/gyms/known?id=&name=&lat=&lng=` (new table `gym_reports`, in data export, deleted with the account); `savedGyms` on the synced profile (optional, older profiles decode). Demo screens `equipment`, `gym-setup`, `gym-swipe`, `gym-result`, `not-here` in CI screenshots.
- **Equipment page with pictures and search** (#162): the equipment list (Training profile → Your gym → Equipment, trips, new gym setup) is now a grid of pictures of each item in use, grouped by area with All / None per area, a search field that also knows other names (swiss ball, assault bike, roman chair, stairmaster…) and the count ticked. Tap a picture to tick or untick it.
- **Gym equipment audit** (#162): 13 items often found in commercial gyms added (43 → 56): EZ curl bar, preacher curl bench, shoulder press machine, standing calf raise machine, T-bar row, glute-ham developer (GHD), stability ball, jump rope, assisted pull-up / dip machine, reverse hyper, biceps curl machine, triceps extension machine and dip belt / weighted vest. Each comes with an exercise (EZ-bar curl and EZ-bar skull crusher, dumbbell preacher curl, machine shoulder press, standing calf raise machine, chest-supported T-bar row, glute-ham raise, stability ball crunch, jump rope, machine-assisted dip, reverse hyper, machine biceps curl, machine triceps extension, weighted dip) with free-exercise-db photos (public domain) and technique notes. Like other single-machine exercises they are offered in Swap and search, not generated into plans. Existing equipment lists keep their items; tick the new ones on the equipment page.
- **Your gym on Account and your profile** (#160): Account → You gets a **Your gym** card: the gym your plan is built for and its item count, opening Training profile → Your gym. While a travel period covers today it shows the trip (place or preset, equipment count, "until" date, home gym) and opens the calendar; a trip in the next two weeks shows as "Trip: … from …". Community → Your profile shows your gym with the **Show my gym** switch (was only in Find members), and the gym under your photo while shown. Your own member page shows your gym, marked hidden when not shown. Trips stay private: members only see the home gym, and only with Show my gym on.
- **iPad and iPhone Duo support** (#120): the app now runs on iPad (iPhone + iPad, every orientation on iPad; iPhone stays portrait) and adapts to the unfolded iPhone Duo. On wide screens (iPad, Duo inner display) every scrolling screen centres its content in a 720 pt column with the background still full width, the workout player and the bottom action bars (Start workout, Generate plan / Save, Apply variation, Add exercises) keep the same column, and tabs use the sidebar-adaptable style (iOS 18+). iPhone layouts are unchanged; every iPhone that runs iOS 27 (iPhone 11 and later, SE 2nd / 3rd gen) is covered by the iOS 17 minimum. Live Activity (iPhone only) and the Watch app are unchanged. CI, PR screenshots and TestFlight archives move to `macos-26` and select the newest Xcode installed (26.6 today; Xcode 27.x and the iOS 27 SDK are picked up automatically once GitHub's runner image ships them, #158); PR screenshots add an iPad set. New manual workflow **App Store screenshots** captures full-resolution JPEGs (no alpha) for iPhone 6.9" (1320×2868), iPhone 6.3" (1206×2622) and iPad 13" (2064×2752), plus iPhone Duo once its simulator is available, to the `app-store-screenshots` branch.
- **Training methods** (#156): Training profile → **Training method** (also from Account) picks how the plan progresses, separate from the goal. **Block periodization** (default, plans unchanged): Base → Build → Peak → Deload with double progression. **Daily undulating**: each session type rotates Heavy (main 3–5, accessories 6–8, long rests) / Moderate (the goal's range) / Light (main 10–12, accessories 12–15) days; the Peak extra set skips light days. **RIR autoregulated**: logged reps in reserve set the next load (≥ 1 above target → +1 increment, ≥ 2 → +2; ≥ 2 below → −5 %); on a "Take it easier" readiness day today's session drops 1 RPE and one set on accessories and explosive work (main lift kept), shown on the readiness card. **Volume progression**: hard sets per major muscle (chest, lats, mid back, side delts, biceps, triceps, quads, hamstrings, glutes) aim at 10 → 13 → 16 → 6 per week (beginner −20 %, advanced +2) by adding or trimming accessory sets (2–5 per exercise, ≤ 10 per muscle per session, ≤ 115 % session time); exercises unchanged. **Minimal dose**: sessions ≤ 40 min, ≤ 4 strength exercises, 2 hard sets each (RPE 9, beginners 8), supersets by default, also for Generate from muscles. All methods: a lift not logged for ≥ 14 days eases back in at −10 %. The chosen method shows its evidence in short; **Sources for each method** opens the full references per method (authors, title, journal), each linking to a PubMed search for the title. Optional `method` on the profile (older profiles decode). Engine: `TrainingMethod` (+ `sources`), `MethodSource`, `DayZone`, `VolumeTargets`, `Session.easedForReadiness()`, `LoadAdvisor.suggest(method:now:)`. Demo screen `profile-method`.
- **Find members in Community** (#152): new **Find members** card in the Community feed (shows how many you follow) opens a search by nickname (contains, prefix matches first) or gym name. With no search it lists members at your gym, who you follow, then suggested members (most followed, then most shared, not yet followed). Each row is an Explore-style gradient card with the member's photo, nickname and about; tap to open their public page, or **FOLLOW** / **FOLLOWING** right on the row. **Show my gym** (opt-in, off by default): your gym from the training profile appears on your rows and member page, and you see members at the same gym (same place id, or same name within ~300 m) who also show theirs; **Hide my gym** turns it off. Never lists you, anyone blocked either way, or members hidden after reports from 3 different members. API: `GET /v1/community/members?q=&gym=mine&limit=`, `showGym` on `PUT /v1/community/me` (new column `community_profiles.show_gym`); `GET /v1/community/follows` rows now also carry bio, shared, followers and `followedByMe`. Demo screen `community-members` in CI screenshots.
- **Pro test mode on staging** (#154): test subscriptions without Apple. With `SUBSCRIPTION_TESTS=on` (set in `fly.staging.toml` only; `SUBSCRIPTIONS` stays `off`), Account → MonkeyWorkout Pro shows a **Staging test** card: Pro for this account only as Server / Off / Preview / Enforced, and plan buttons make fake purchases (subscription with free week, Lifetime) with **Cancel auto-renew (test)** and **End test purchase now**. Other accounts and older TestFlight builds see nothing. The Staging test card sits at the top of Pro and says what the mode does (e.g. "Free: Pro features locked"); the Account card shows it too, since Server / Off / Preview lock nothing. API `PUT /v1/me/subscriptions/test`, `POST /v1/me/subscriptions/test/purchase`, `POST /v1/me/subscriptions/test/cancel` (404 unless enabled); table `subscription_tests`.
- **New exercise from Explore** (#142): Explore → Muscles and Explore → Categories get a **New exercise** card opening the same custom exercise builder as when building a workout; on a muscle page it starts with that muscle as main muscle, on a category page with that category. Your custom exercises now show in Explore's muscle and category lists and counts (`Exercise.working` / `inCategory` include active custom exercises; plan generation unchanged). Pro (`ProGate.customExercise`, PRO badge), like in the picker.
- **Start a generated workout now or on a day** (#138): after **Generate from muscles**, the builder shows **Start now** (saves the workout and opens the player) and **Add to a day** (saves it and opens Add to plan: replace a day of this week or add an extra session, optional weekday). Work one body part in a single session without changing your plan. Pro (`ProGate.generateWorkout`, PRO badge; open to everyone while subscriptions are off).
- **Watch main screen redesign** (#134): Today opens on a big gradient card for the next workout (day, title, length, exercises, plan week) with **▶ START** under it and week progress / readiness; turn the crown for Explore-style gradient rows with an icon tile and a big number: This week, Workouts, Streak, Progress (PR count).
- **Undo a workout** (#133): a finished workout can be undone as if it never happened, from History (**Undo workout**, was Delete from history) or from the session page this week (**Undo workout**, replaces Mark as not done when the session was recorded). Removes the history entry and the sets logged during it (progression, PRs, starting loads, progress), its auto-shared community post, and sets the session back to not done so it can be done again; removed on the server too. Apple Health keeps its copy. On the Watch, **End & save** now counts down 3 s with **Cancel** before ending.
- **Watch workout on one screen** (#131): while an exercise runs, the Watch shows one page with elapsed time, heart rate, kcal, exercise and set, kg / reps (crown), timer, main button and next (was 3 vertical pages). Swipe right for controls: Pause / Resume, End & save, Back / Skip, Discard workout (asks to confirm). Swipe left for Now Playing (system player) to skip a song or change volume. Rest / effort overlay stays on the workout page, so music and controls are reachable during rest. New look in the app's style: the block's colour wash and category pill like the phone player, exercise progress bar, big tinted kg with round − / +, set dots, glowing lime button, Next pill; controls as Explore-style gradient tiles; effort buttons as colour gradients; gradient rest ring with the next set as a card.
- **Ops: undo a mistaken workout** (#135): manual GitHub workflow **Fly undo workout** (staging / production) runs `backend/src/admin/undoWorkout.ts` on the Fly machine. Dry run lists an account's workouts started, ended or saved in a time window with what each would remove; with a workout id + apply it writes a JSON backup to `/data/backups`, then deletes the workout, its set logs (same session, its start to end + 6 h), PR attempts made during it and its community posts (reactions cascade). Nothing else touched.
- **Generate a workout from muscles** (#119): New workout → **Generate from muscles** picks muscle groups (shoulders, chest, arms, back, core, hips & glutes, legs), duration (20–90 min, default from your session length), **Rest between sets** and **Group exercises** (straight / supersets of the same muscle group / circuits per body area), both preset from the training profile with the goal's choice marked REC. Strength exercises fit your gym's equipment and your level, cover each muscle of the group, sets × reps follow your goal (up to 2 main lifts on the goal's main dose, straight sets with ≥ 90 s rest; core, calves and prehab light and last). The result fills the builder (name from the muscles) to edit before saving; **Generate again** gives another pick. Custom workouts keep superset / circuit groups (optional `group` on each item, older records decode; shown as A1 / A2 badges) and the player runs them back to back with rest after the round; a group left with one exercise after edits plays as straight sets. Engine: `MuscleWorkoutOptions`, `CustomWorkout.generate`, `CustomWorkout.groupLabels`. Pro feature (`ProGate.generateWorkout`, PRO badge on the card; open to everyone while subscriptions are off). Demo screens `generate`, `generated` in CI screenshots.
- **Muscle audit** (#112): 19 → 28 muscles. New: upper traps, serratus, brachialis, forearm extensors (forearms now = flexors / grip), lower abs (lower part of the rectus, pelvis-curl moves), deep core (transverse abdominis), glute med, tibialis, soleus (calves now = gastrocnemius); "Upper back" renamed **Mid back** (mid/lower traps, rhomboids). All 195 exercises re-tagged. Each new muscle is drawn on the front/back body map and listed in Explore → Muscles with its role. 6 exercises added so every muscle has a direct one (all `generator: false`, plans unchanged): barbell and dumbbell shrug, dumbbell reverse curl, lying leg raise, band monster walk (free-exercise-db photos, public domain) and wall tibialis raise (own illustration), each with technique notes. Stretches cover the new muscles too (wall biceps → brachialis, sphinx → lower abs / deep core, figure-4 → glute med, lat stretch → serratus, couch stretch → tibialis). Additive raw values: saved data decodes unchanged.
- **MonkeyWorkout Pro: subscriptions, Lifetime and free/Pro split** (#126): CHF 2 / week, CHF 6 / month, CHF 60 / year with a 1-week free trial, and **Lifetime CHF 100** one-time (all future updates and features included). Approved split ([proposal](https://claude.ai/code/artifact/b7e5fd97-3b10-4a70-a91c-4a6fc5201054), `docs/subscriptions.md`): training stays free; New variation, kept swaps, workout style, building / sharing own workouts, custom exercises, Attempt a PR, progress beyond 28 days and the muscle heat map, starting workouts on Apple Watch, climbing-day rules / add-on and MonkeyGrade are Pro, with a lime PRO badge and a paywall sheet headed by the tapped feature. One skippable offer after the first plan ("Your week is ready"), Account → MonkeyWorkout Pro (status, trial days, auto-renew, manage, restore, redeem code, upgrade to Lifetime), PRO chip on the profile, reminder 2 days before a free week ends, Terms / Privacy links and renewal terms on every purchase screen. API: `GET /v1/me/entitlement` (`lifetime`), `POST /v1/me/subscriptions/transactions` (Apple-signed JWS verified against the pinned Apple Root CA - G3 chain), App Store Server Notifications V2 at `POST /v1/app-store/notifications`. Free access: Actions → **Free access** (grant / revoke / list / grant-all for early testers). Actions → **App Store subscriptions** creates group, products, Lifetime, CHF prices, trial and notification URLs (dry run by default). Server switch `SUBSCRIPTIONS=off|preview|enforced`, default `off`: nothing shown or locked until launch. Demo screens `paywall`, `pro`, `pro-offer`, `pro-gate`.
- **Move a workout or add an extra one** (#113): one-off changes to this week, kept in the app and synced with the profile. **Move to…** from a Calendar day (this week) or by touching and holding a Train session card: pick any day of this week (days off locked); a day that already has a session swaps with it. Moves apply to that calendar week only (`moves`, optional, older profiles decode); next week follows the plan again; days off and travel still re-plan around moved sessions. **Add workout** from a Calendar day or Train → This week: a quick generated session (upper, lower, full-body power, push, pull, legs, conditioning; built for your equipment and this week's phase), a workout from your library, or log a climb. Extra sessions show on their day in Calendar and Train, count in the week's progress and are recorded in history like any session. Train lists this week's sessions by day. Missed-session check-in gets **Pick a day this week**. Demo screens `day`, `move`, `add-workout` in CI screenshots.
- **Privacy policy, Terms of Use, Support** (#125): public pages `/privacy`, `/terms` (EULA incl. subscriptions, free trial, complimentary access, zero tolerance for objectionable content, Apple minimum terms) and `/support` (contact, delete account, export, cancel subscription, report) on the backend, for App Store Connect and the app. Operator, address, contact, governing law and venue from `LEGAL_OPERATOR` / `LEGAL_ADDRESS` / `LEGAL_EMAIL` / `LEGAL_LAW` / `LEGAL_VENUE`, set in `fly.staging.toml` / `fly.production.toml`. Account gets a **Legal & support** section opening them in-app; sign-up and Sign in with Apple link Terms + Privacy Policy. In-app privacy notice corrected (session heart-rate summary is synced; community data). Privacy manifest adds photos, user content and user ID. Checklist for App Store Connect in `docs/app-store-review.md`.
- **Anatomical body map + body figure M / F** (#110): every body map (Explore → Muscles, muscle and exercise pages, sessions, player, progress, builder) now draws smoothed front/back anatomical figures with real muscle shapes (own vector art, generated by `ios/Tools/bodymap/generate.py` into `BodyMapGeometry.swift`), heat glow on the most-worked muscles and faint anatomy lines. Two body figures, **M** and **F**: asked at the end of onboarding (prefilled F for female sex, otherwise M) and changeable in Account → Body & Health. Display only, stored as optional `figure` in body metrics (synced with the profile), separate from the optional sex / gender fields. Explore → Muscles gets a gradient "This week" hero card (working sets, most-worked muscles) above the figures. Taps hit-test the muscle polygons (top-most wins). Demo screens `onboarding-figure`, `explore-female`, `muscle-female` added to CI screenshots.
- **Starting load for new exercises** (#115): the player no longer starts at an empty kg. Exercises you logged before keep prefilling from last time (double progression, labelled "From last time"); a never-logged exercise starts from a related lift you did log (e.g. bench press → incline bench, barbell ↔ dumbbell converted), else from an estimate based on body weight, sex and age (Apple Health or Body & Health) and your experience level, with reps and reps-in-reserve from the plan. Estimates are conservative, rounded to the equipment step and labelled "Estimate"; kg and reps stay editable.
- **Swap an exercise** (#101): on a session, each planned exercise has a **Swap** button (also long-press → Swap exercise, and **Swap exercise** on the exercise page). The swap sheet lists best matches (same movement or same main muscles, with your equipment, closest muscle overlap first) and **Search all exercises** (full library incl. your custom exercises).
