Applies to this session, or turn on **Keep for <day> in future weeks**: kept swaps are stored per plan day in the synced profile (`exerciseSwaps`, optional, older profiles decode), survive New variation and week changes, and can be undone from the sheet (Back to …). Engine: `ExerciseSwap`, `WeekPlan.applying(swaps:)`, `Exercise.swapOptions(for:)` (shared with plan variation). Demo screen `swap` in CI screenshots.
- **Profile on Account, linked with Community** (#108): Account → Profile sets nickname, photo and about you (also before joining; nickname optional until then). Set once, shown in both places: the Account header shows the photo, and editing in Account or in Community updates the other. Joining the community is prefilled ("You'll appear as" card, Edit to change) and only asks for visibility and the guidelines. Leaving the community now keeps nickname, photo and about on Account (posts, cheers and follows are still deleted). API `GET/PUT /v1/me/public-profile`, `PUT/DELETE /v1/me/public-profile/avatar`; nickname unique across members and account-only profiles; `PUT /v1/community/me` nickname optional when the Account profile has one. New table `user_profiles` (account-only profiles; existing members keep theirs in `community_profiles`), in data export and account deletion. Demo screen `account-profile` in CI screenshots.
- **Apple Watch companion app** (#56): browse this week's sessions, session details and progress (PRs, recent workouts) on the watch; start a workout on the watch (HealthKit workout session: heart rate, calories, active time) with the same player as the phone: hero card, crown to adjust kg / reps, one-tap effort, rest ring with haptics, up next, pause / resume, summary. While the phone player runs, the watch mirrors it (set, rest countdown, next) and can complete sets, skip rest and add 15 s. Sets logged on the watch sync back to the phone (WatchConnectivity). New watchOS target (bundle id `<app id>.watchkitapp`, watchOS 10).
- **Faster logging in the workout player** (#55): current-set card with kg / reps steppers prefilled from the load suggestion (no keyboard); after each set a one-tap effort picker (Fail / 1 / 2 / 3 / 4+ reps left, logged as RIR) on the rest screen; gradient "Up next" card (next exercise with image, sets × reps) in the player and next set or exercise on the rest screen.
- **Live Activity** (#55): lock screen + Dynamic Island show the current set or rest countdown and what's next, with +15 s / Skip rest buttons; "Rest over" notification when the app is in the background. New widget extension target (bundle id `<app id>.LiveActivity`).
- **Pause and resume a workout** (#57): the player's pause button offers Pause (resume later at the same exercise, sets done and active time, also after closing the app), Finish & save now, or Discard. "Resume workout" on the session and week cards.
- **Workout library** (#62): Train's "My workouts" + "Example workouts" become one library: your workouts, built-in (Lowerbody, Arms + Shoulder) and workouts shared by members, as Explore-style gradient cards (All / Mine / Built-in / Members filters, search). From a workout's page: **Start workout** runs it as a standalone quick session; **Add to plan** replaces a day of this week's plan or adds an extra session (optional weekday), kept on a new variation, cleared on a week change, synced with the profile; planned ones can be removed again. Share your own workouts as Only me (default) / Members / Public, same model as Community (#61); members' workouts show the author, can be saved as a private copy, reported (auto-hidden after 3 reports) or their author blocked. API: `visibility` on `/v1/me/custom-workouts` (needs community profile, text filter), `GET /v1/library/shared`, `/:id`, `/:id/save`, `/:id/report`; migration adds `visibility`, `shared_at`, `hidden_at`, `saves` to `custom_workouts`. Demo screens `library`, `library-plan` in CI screenshots.
- **Notifications** (#69). Account → Notifications, Explore-style cards with toggle and time per type:
  - **Session reminder** on the morning of each planned session (default 07:30); tap opens the session.
  - **Missed session** check-in the next day (default 09:00) with **Train today** / **Pick another day** (moves it to the next free day via the training calendar, or opens the calendar). Cleared once the session is recorded.
  - **Your achievements** (#76): notification when you unlock a badge (Progress → Achievements); tap opens the badge. Own toggle.
  - **People you follow**: APNs push when they set a PR (PR attempts) or unlock a badge (sent once per badge; device announces new badges via `POST /v1/me/achievements`, old badges never sent). Followers come from Community follows (blocks respected); only members whose default visibility isn't private announce, shown by nickname. Tap opens their community page.
  - **Quiet hours** (default 22:00–07:00): local reminders move out of the window, remote pushes arrive silently.
  - Send test (local + server push). Reminders follow the training calendar's dated sessions (moves, days off, travel).
  - Backend: push device registry, notification prefs, APNs HTTP/2 token-auth sender, dead tokens removed; secrets `APNS_KEY_ID` / `APNS_PRIVATE_KEY` via **Fly setup**; skipped until set. Device unregistered on sign-out; data deleted with the account.
- **Training style** (#78): training profile → Rest between sets (long 2–3 min / standard / short 30–60 s) and Group exercises (straight sets / supersets of the same muscle group / circuits of 2–4 per body area). Goal defaults marked REC (Max strength, Explosive, Build & define: long rests; Build & define: supersets; Endurance: short rests + circuits); picking REC keeps following the goal. Generator honours both: main lifts stay straight with ≥ 90 s rest, grouped accessories labelled A1/A2 share the rest after the round and get extra rounds for the saved time (not in deload). Session cards show the group badge. Optional profile/plan fields, older data decodes unchanged.
- **Supersets and circuits in the workout player** (#78): grouped exercises play back to back, A1 → A2 with no rest, then the group's rest before the next round; after the last round the player moves on to the next exercise. Up next / rest screens show the next member or round.
- **Per-workout rest and grouping** (#106): workout detail → Rest & grouping card opens the same Rest between sets / Group exercises cards for that workout only (PLAN badge = your plan's setting, picking it follows the plan again; Back to plan default). The workout is re-planned for its new rests and grouping (main lift stays, time fits), other workouts and the training profile are unchanged. Overrides sync with the profile and reset on a week change; not offered for conditioning circuits or done / started workouts. Demo screen `workout-style` in CI screenshots.
- **Exercise coverage** (#77): 14 staples fill catalog gaps found in an audit (patterns with 1–3 options): Smith machine squat, barbell good morning, reverse nordic, TRX hamstring curl, leg press calf raise, incline barbell bench, pec deck fly, chin-up, band-assisted pull-up, close-grip bench, dumbbell overhead triceps extension, reverse crunch (grip-free core for climbers), Copenhagen plank (adductors), side-lying dumbbell external rotation (rotator cuff without a cable). Each has technique notes and media: 11 free-exercise-db photos (pinned, public domain), 3 new own illustrations. All generator-eligible (177 exercises).
- **Community tab** (#61): Train / Explore / Progress / **Community** / Account. Community profile with nickname, profile photo and short bio. Share a win (recent workout, personal best or badge) as a vivid card with caption; per post visibility **Only me / Members / Public** (public adds a web link `/share/:id`), default set in the profile (Only me unless changed); change or delete any time. Optional auto-share of finished workouts. Follow members; feeds: **Members**, **Following** and **Mine** (private progress log). Cheers on others' posts: ❤️ like, 💪 strong, 🔥 on fire, 👏 keep going. Member pages with shared wins and cheers count. App Store 1.2: community guidelines acceptance to join, objectionable-text filter (nickname, bio, captions), report post or member (mailed to `MODERATION_EMAIL` when set), post hidden after 3 reporters, block both ways, leave community deletes profile/photo/posts. Photos re-rendered on device (square, ≤512 px) and stripped of EXIF/GPS on the server. API `/v1/community/*`; included in data export and account deletion. Moderation helpers (`community/moderation.ts`) reusable for other shared items; `followerIds(db, userId)` for push fan-out.
- **Week to week** choice in the training profile (onboarding + Train → edit) (#64): **Same every week** (identical exercises per day all cycle, only sets/effort change), **Fresh each week** (default: main lift per day stays for progression, accessories/warm-up/mobility/cardio change weekly) or **Rotate muscle focus** (day order and each day's lead lift rotate weekly, same patterns and weekly volume). Explore-style gradient cards. Stored as optional `variety` in the profile (older profiles → Fresh). Main lifts now stay stable across weeks in every mode (previously re-rolled weekly). Train → New variation still reshuffles with a new seed. Demo screen `variety` added to CI screenshots.
- **Find a gym** (#70): gyms near you (location, when in use) or near a city/hotel/address, from Apple Maps (fitness centres, nearest first) on a map + list. Tapping one shows details in the app first: distance, address, map, phone (tap to call), website host, Directions (Apple Maps), Hours & photos (Apple Maps place card), then Open website. From Calendar (toolbar) and travel mode (Full gym nearby → "Find gyms near <place>", Use this gym). Demo screen `gyms` added to CI screenshots.
- **Training calendar** (#68): Train → Calendar shows this week and the next 5 with dated sessions, climbing days and cycle phase (Base/Build/Peak/Deload); tap a day for its session (exercises, sets × reps). **Can't train**: mark a date or range (optional reason); sessions move to free days of that week (climbing/spacing rules kept), with too few free days the week switches to the smaller split (fewer, fuller sessions), days left over are listed as skipped. **Travelling**: date range, place, and what's there (hotel gym, dumbbells only, bodyweight only, bands, full gym via Apple Maps search) + fine-tune equipment; sessions on those days are rebuilt for that equipment. Climbing days are paused while away. Current week: done sessions stay, only today onward is re-planned; Train shows travel days on session rows. Synced in the profile (`away`, optional, older profiles decode). `RescheduleSheet` / `AppModel.reschedule(sessionId:)` move a session to the next free day; `AppModel.plannedSessions(from:to:)` lists dated sessions (for reminders). 18 travel-kit exercises with full technique (bodyweight squat/split squat/lunge/single-leg RDL, single-leg hip thrust, table row, prone floor pulldown, pike push-up, single-leg calf raise, dumbbell bent-over row/floor press/standing press/floor pullover, band row/pulldown/chest press/overhead press/good morning), picked only when no barbell, cable or machines are available, so full-gym plans are unchanged. Travel exercises have media: 8 free-exercise-db photos (public domain) and 10 own two-frame illustrations (`ios/Tools/illustrations` now also reads `Exercises+Travel.swift`). Demo screens `calendar`, `away` added to CI screenshots.
- **Custom exercises** (#52): build your own exercise, e.g. for a machine in your gym, from **New exercise** in the Add exercises picker. Name, category, machine name, equipment, main/assist muscles on a body map, reps or seconds, up to 3 cues. Picture: a photo (camera or library, compressed to ≤300 KB) and/or a pose drawn in the **pose designer** (start/end frames, drag joints, templates, props such as seat, pad, cable, bench). Custom exercises appear in search and the picker, open in the exercise page (Edit), work in custom workouts and history, sync across devices (`GET/POST /me/custom-exercises`), and are archived rather than deleted so past logs keep resolving.
- **Attempt a PR** (#60): exercise page → "Attempt a PR" (Library and from a session's exercise) for strength moves. Choose 1RM, rep max (3 or 5) or max reps (only option for bodyweight moves; load = added kg). Target from history (best for that rep count + one increment, or est. 1RM via Epley), adjustable; warm-up ramp (40–90 %, empty-bar floor, rounded to equipment steps); safety cue per lift (spotter / safety arms for bench, pins for squat, drop-don't-grind for deadlift). Up to 3 attempts (made → +1 increment, missed → same load). Result screen celebrates a new record (+Δ vs previous best) and makes a shareable image card (1080 × 1350: exercise, load, +Δ, date, MonkeyWorkout) with caption text for Instagram, Messages, WhatsApp etc., PR history per exercise (long-press deletes). Made attempt sets are logged into the exercise history but ignored by load suggestions. Synced via API `/v1/me/pr-attempts` (in export and account deletion) for the upcoming community feed.
- Every exercise now has a picture (#46): 1 more free-exercise-db photo match (battle rope slams → 114 photos) and the app's own two-frame illustrations (start/end, primary muscles highlighted, on the category gradient) for the other 49. Generated as vector SVG assets by `ios/Tools/illustrations/generate.py`; shown in the picker, session thumbnails, exercise page and player. No more endless two-frame flip: exercise page and player show start and end side by side (labelled); shared `ExerciseMotionView` plays the movement once on tap with a slow dissolve (instant switch with Reduce Motion). `ios/EXERCISE_MEDIA.md` records source + license per exercise; test enforces media for every catalog exercise. No video: no free-to-use video set covers the catalog.
- New app icon: lime kettlebell-monkey on purple gradient (source `ios/Design/AppIcon.svg`) (#35).
- **Connect MonkeyGrade** (#48): Account → Connected apps → MonkeyGrade. Sign in with MonkeyGrade email + password (+ 2FA code) via its native API (`api.monkeygrade.cloud`, Bearer token in this device's Keychain, never sent to the MonkeyWorkout server). Read-only import of your home-gym climbing sessions: duration, sends, flashes, hardest grade (Font/V), RPE. Shown in Recent climbs (MonkeyGrade's own Health copies are de-duplicated) and RPE feeds the hard-climb note. No MonkeyGrade changes.
- **Around climbing days** (#47): choose how the gym day before and after climbing is planned (keep it light, strong push or legs, rest, no adjustment) and whether gym sessions may share a climbing day. Schedule, grip/explosive rules and session notes follow the choice; defaults keep the previous behaviour. Optional **climbing-day add-on** (≈ 28 min: push, shoulder health, finger extensors, core, no pulling/grip) shown on Train. Settings from the Train climbing card.
- **Log climbs** (#44): Train tab card for climbers (goal climbing or climbing days set) with climbs this week; log sheet with type (boulder, lead, top rope, outdoor), start, duration, effort 1–10, optional top grade and notes. Stored on device and written to Apple Health as a Climbing workout (estimated kcal) when Health is connected. Recent climbs strip merges in-app climbs with Health climbs from other apps (e.g. MonkeyGrade), long-press deletes in-app ones (and their Health workout). A hard climb yesterday or today shows a keep-grip-light note before the next session.
- Train tab explains itself (#41): Readiness card shows its source (Apple Health, on-device), your 30-day averages, and opens a sheet on tap: how the verdict is computed (HRV > 15 % below average, resting HR > 5 bpm above, sleep < 6 h; 0 / 1 / 2+ off = Ready to push / Train as planned / Take it easier), what HRV, Rest HR, Sleep and VO₂max mean (VO₂max is context only), and RPE in plain words. Week chip ("Base · RPE −1") opens the 4-week cycle explained. Workout player: info button on "kg × reps · RIR" explains RIR/RPE and where to log it. Demo screens `readiness`, `cycle` added to CI screenshots.
- **TestFlight distribute** workflow: waits for Apple processing, then adds the newest build to the internal TestFlight group "Workout" via the App Store Connect API. Runs after each TestFlight upload; also manual (group name input).
- Manual **Fly logs** workflow: prints recent staging/production API logs (errors by default) without local tools.
- Custom workouts (#28): build your own (name, catalog exercises with search + category/muscle filters, sets × reps, rest, optional kcal, reorder), edit/duplicate/delete, "Duplicate & edit" on example workouts; "My workouts" on Train, played and recorded like examples, synced with offline queue. API `/v1/me/custom-workouts` (upsert, `since`, delete; in export and account deletion).
- Per-set logging in the workout player (kg × reps, optional RIR), prefilled from a load suggestion; synced (`setIndex`/`rir` on logs, backward compatible; backend migration adds nullable columns) (#5).
- Load suggestions: double progression within the prescribed rep range, equipment-aware increments/rounding, −5 % below range, −10 % deload week; shown in player and on exercise page (#5).
- Per-exercise history (best e1RM trend, sessions with sets) from the exercise page (#5).
- Exercise illustrations from free-exercise-db (public domain, pinned commit) for 113 exercises: thumbnails on session cards, animated header with credit on exercise page, animated image in the player; disk-cached for offline use (#27).
- Sign in with Apple credential check on launch/foreground; signs out when the Apple ID link is revoked (#12).
- Account deletion revokes the Sign in with Apple token: server exchanges the authorization code at sign-in (refresh token stored encrypted) and calls Apple's revoke endpoint on deletion. Needs GitHub secrets `APPLE_KEY_ID`, `APPLE_PRIVATE_KEY`, pushed to Fly by the **Fly setup** workflow; skipped until set (#12).
- Change email: Account → Change email, 6-digit code to new address, old address notified (`POST /v1/me/email`, `/v1/me/email/confirm`) (#13).
- Breached-password check (Have I Been Pwned, k-anonymity, fail-open, 2 s timeout) at sign-up, reset and change; `HIBP_CHECK=0` disables (#13).
- Purge job for expired codes, pending email changes and expired/revoked refresh tokens (`PURGE_INTERVAL_MIN`, default 60) (#13).
- Climbing schedule (#6): pick climbing weekdays (and optionally gym days) in the training profile; sessions are placed on weekdays around climbing: no heavy pull/grip or explosive block the day before climbing, push and leg days adjacent to climbing. Session cards, next-up card and session screen show weekday + "climbing tomorrow / day after climbing" hint. Climbing days/week follows picked weekdays; Health climbing hint hidden then. Profiles without weekdays generate unchanged plans.
- **Staging smoke test** workflow (#26): after each staging deploy, checks `/healthz`, that mail goes via SMTP, sign-up email accepted by SES (mailbox simulator), unverified-login refusal, forgot-password and auth guards. `/healthz` now reports the mail transport.
- Example workouts on the Train tab: **Arms + Shoulder** (10 exercises) and **Lowerbody** (10 exercises), 3 × 10 each, with source-plan calories and activity points; open, tick off and play like any session, recorded to history. 12 new exercises with full setup/technique (barbell split squat, barbell & incline curls, lying barbell/dumbbell French press, standing reverse fly, diamond push-up, seated calf raise, hip adduction/abduction, seated crunch, torso rotation) and 4 new machines; example-only exercises are never picked by the generator.
- **TestFlight (Staging)** workflow (#12): archives the Staging scheme on macOS and uploads to App Store Connect using an App Store Connect API key (cloud signing); skips until `ASC_KEY_ID`/`ASC_ISSUER_ID`/`ASC_KEY_P8` are set. Setup steps in `ios/README.md`.
- Staging + production hosted on Fly.io (#3): apps `monkeyworkout-staging` → `https://workout-staging.monkeygrade.cloud`, `monkeyworkout-prod` → `https://workout.monkeygrade.cloud` (`backend/fly.*.toml`, region `fra`, 1 machine + `/data` volume, `/healthz` check). **Deploy staging** deploys the CI-built image on every merge to `main`; **Promote to production** (manual) deploys the same digest to prod. Both skip with a warning until `FLY_API_TOKEN` is set. Manual **Fly setup** workflow bootstraps app, volume, secrets and TLS cert per environment without local tools. Email via Amazon SES SMTP (eu-central-2, dedicated send-only IAM user; SMTP password derived in CI) from `no-reply@monkeygrade.cloud` when repo secrets `SES_ACCESS_KEY_ID`/`SES_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY` (or `SMTP_URL`) are set, else codes logged. iOS Staging build → staging host, Release → prod host.
- Progress tab (#18): totals, week streak vs target, 12-week consistency calendar, sessions-per-week chart, estimated-1RM trend per lift, 28-day muscle balance map, personal records, 22 achievements (bronze/silver/gold with progress), full session history with per-exercise sets/weights, heart rate, kcal and muscle map.
- Apple Health (#18): writes each session as a workout (energy estimate + metadata) and reads weight, height, age, sex, resting HR, HRV, VO₂max, sleep, in-session heart rate and climbing workouts; readiness card (sleep, HRV and resting HR vs 30-day baseline); climbing-days suggestion from Health; body weight & VO₂max trends. Health data stays on device.
- Body & Health screen (#18): height, weight, birth year entered in-app when Health lacks them, optional write-back to Health; prompt on Train tab until known. Inclusive, optional identity: gender (woman, man, non-binary, genderfluid, agender, Two-Spirit, questioning, self-describe, prefer not to say) kept separate from optional sex for estimates (female, male, intersex, other, prefer not to say).
- API: workout history sync `/v1/me/workouts` (upsert, `since` delta, delete; included in export and account deletion) (#18).
- Equipment & correct positioning for every exercise (#16): equipment cards (icon, Puls 5 zone, adjustment advice), and per-exercise technique — numbered set-up steps (machine settings + start position), head-to-feet body-position checkpoints, common mistakes, breathing. Shown on exercise pages, as a "Setup & technique" sheet and inline checkpoints in the workout player, and as an equipment line on exercise cards. Covered for all 151 exercises by tests.
- Muscle visibility (#14): front/back body heat maps per session, per exercise and for the week; primary/assist muscle chips on every exercise; per-part "session flow" showing which muscles each category targets.
- Explore tab (#14): browse by muscle (tappable body map, muscle groups, weekly sets) or by category; muscle pages list related exercises grouped by category (main vs assist); exercise pages with map, cues, equipment and swap.
- Workout player (#14): full-screen step-through with category progress bar, set tracking, auto rest countdown (+15 s / skip), weight logging, next-up preview and a finish summary of muscles hit.
- DEBUG demo mode + CI screenshot job (≤ 800 px, branch `screenshots/pr-<n>`) for visual review (#14).
- Bold dark design system (#14): heavy rounded type, lime accent, gradient per category, progress rings, animated welcome screen.

### Changed
- **Explore top menu: live hero card + colour dock** (#459, design B3 from `explore-menu/proposals-b.png`): the five equal tiles (Muscles, Machines, Categories, Workouts, Programmes) crowded the width since Machines was added, clipped "Programmes" and looked flat. Now a big gradient hero for the picked section shows something live, with its count on the right: Muscles = this week's sets and top muscles (replaces the "This week" header over the body map, so the map moves up), Machines = most used machine (last 30 days), Types = most trained type, Workouts = last workout, Plans = programme week (or goals). Below, a dock of five round colour icons (short labels Types and Plans); the picked one grows with a white ring and lime label. Each section has its own colour. `ExploreMenu` / `ExploreHero` in `Views/Library/ExploreMenu.swift`.
- **Log climb looks like Log sport** (#460, design C from `log-climb/proposals.png`): the climb sheet was a long grey form (type grid, When, Duration, 1-10 effort, grade, notes) that needed scrolling to reach Save. It now uses the Log sport layout: sport strip on top (Climbing picked; another sport or All turns the sheet into Log sport, same start time), one card in the climb type's colours with Boulder / Lead / Top rope / Outdoor chips, start, duration stepper, Easy / Solid / Hard / Wrecked, **top grade as chips from your recent climbs** (tap again to clear, Other to type one; the field shows when there's no history) and + Notes. Save is pinned: "Save Boulder · 1h30 · 7". Draft and Undo work as before. Shared parts `SportStrip`, `CardDuration`, `FeelChips`, `CardChip`, `AddChip` in `SportViews.swift`.
- **Release bundle 2026-10-18.2** (What's new `2026-10-18.2`, 2 cards): Daily Move (#454) and Explore by machine (#456). The 2026-10-18.1 notes moved to the history (build 44).
- **Auto save on edit screens, drafts kept for new ones** (#437, design A + C from `auto-save/proposals.png`): screens that edit something that already exists had a Save button, and closing without it dropped the changes. They now save on their own: **Body & Health** (height, weight, birth year, sex, figure), **Around climbing**, **Edit workout**, **Edit measurements**, **My setup** (machine note, photo included), **Edit away**, **Gym zone**, **Edit exercise** and **Edit programme**. **A**: a lime pill in the navigation bar reads *Saving…* a moment after a change, then *Saved*. **C**: leaving the screen after a change shows the gradient Undo bar ("Body & Health saved · 78.5 kg") and Undo puts back the values the screen opened with. Changes wait while they're incomplete (a workout without a name or exercises, an exercise missing its basics). Delete / Remove / Archive never get written back by a pending save. Apple Health gets Body & Health values once, on leave. **New** workouts, exercises, climbs and measurement entries keep their Save / Add button, but what's typed is kept on the device: closing the screen or the app being killed keeps it, and reopening shows **Draft restored · Start fresh**. Security, consent and one-shot screens stay explicit. Component `Views/Components/AutoSave.swift` (`.autoSave(_:title:detail:save:committed:)`, `SavedPill`, `DraftStore`, `.keepsDraft`, `DraftRestoredStrip`).
- **Release bundle 2026-10-18** (What's new `2026-10-18.1`, 12 cards, one per shipped stream): MonkeyFit (#449), sport maps (#445), Track live (#439), Log sport (#435), History (#442), Progress board (#444), Hyrox ready (#431), Chest & Back (#451), auto save (#437), one profile page (#438), gym search (#447), review fixes (#432). What's new no longer caps cards at 5 (`check_whats_new.sh`, ios/README). The 2026-10-17.3 notes moved to the history (build 43). All streams merged in one integration PR so CI runs once.
- **Start sport tracking from the iPhone** (#439, A + C from `other-sports/start-watch-proposals.png`): live tracking (#418) could only start on the Watch, and Hyrox's **Start race on Watch** (#426) was only in Progress → Hyrox once Hyrox was in My sports. **A**: Log sport gets a **Track live / Log past** switch for sports the Watch records (with a paired Watch): Track live shows what the Watch dashboard records, **Full GPS / Saver** (Hyrox: full race, first half, second half) and a pinned **Start on Watch** that wakes the Watch app and starts the session there (`WatchSnapshot.sportStart`, the Watch takes the newest start asked in the last 5 minutes). **C**: a round lime Watch button next to Log sport on Train (Next up and rest days) opens it in Track live mode; Explore → Categories gets a **Hyrox** card that opens the Hyrox page for everyone. Fix: a Hyrox start from the iPhone now reaches the Watch before Hyrox is in My sports.
- **Log sport: details right under the sport** (#435, layout B + card C from `other-sports/log-sheet-proposals.png`): after picking a sport, When / Duration / How hard sat below the 28-tile grid and members did not see they had to scroll. Picking a sport now folds the grid into a strip (picked sport first, your sports, **All** reopens the grid), and one bold card in the sport's colours holds start time, duration stepper and how hard (Easy / Solid / Hard / Wrecked); **+ Distance** and **+ Notes** open inside the card. A lime **Save Hyrox · 2h · 6** bar stays pinned at the bottom.
- **One profile page for everything about you** (#438, design A from `unified-profile/proposals.png`): name and @nickname were edited on the Settings header card, photo and about in Settings → Community profile, gender and pronouns in Body & Health, and visibility / auto-share in Community → Edit profile. They are now all on one **Your profile** page, opened by tapping your photo and name on the Settings header card or **Edit profile** on your Community profile: header card (photo, name, @nickname, email), About you, **How we talk to you** (gender, pronouns, coach calls you, show pronouns in Community), and for members the Community options (default visibility, auto-share, show my gym). Auto saves (#437): Saved pill while editing, Undo bar on leave puts the opened values back (photo changes save at once). The Settings **Community profile** row is gone (Settings search for nickname, photo, pronouns or gender still finds **Your profile**); Body & Health keeps body data only (Health, figure, height, weight, birth year, sex for estimates, measurements). The join form stays as it was.
- **Auto save on edit screens, drafts kept for new ones** (#437, design A + C from `auto-save/proposals.png`): screens that edit something that already exists had a Save button, and closing without it dropped the changes. They now save on their own: **Body & Health** (height, weight, birth year, sex, figure), **Around climbing**, **Edit workout**, **Edit measurements**, **My setup** (machine note, photo included), **Edit away**, **Gym zone**, **Edit exercise** and **Edit programme**. **A**: a lime pill in the navigation bar reads *Saving…* a moment after a change, then *Saved*. **C**: leaving the screen after a change shows the gradient Undo bar ("Body & Health saved · 78.5 kg") and Undo puts back the values the screen opened with. Changes wait while they're incomplete (a workout without a name or exercises, an exercise missing its basics). Delete / Remove / Archive never get written back by a pending save. Apple Health gets Body & Health values once, on leave. **New** workouts, exercises, climbs and measurement entries keep their Save / Add button, but what's typed is kept on the device: closing the screen or the app being killed keeps it, and reopening shows **Draft restored · Start fresh**. Security, consent and one-shot screens stay explicit. Component `Views/Components/AutoSave.swift` (`.autoSave(_:title:detail:save:committed:)`, `SavedPill`, `DraftStore`, `.keepsDraft`, `DraftRestoredStrip`).
- **Home-screen name MonkeyFit + new colourful icon** (#449, name B + icon C from `app-name-logo/proposals.png`): "MonkeyWorkout" (13 characters) was cut to "MonkeyWo…" under the icon. The home-screen and Watch label (`APP_DISPLAY_NAME`, Release + Staging) is now **MonkeyFit**; the App Store name stays MonkeyWorkout and the bundle id is unchanged. The icon keeps the kettlebell monkey but moves to a vivid blue → purple → magenta gradient (Strength → Mobility card colours) with a red headband, matching the Explore cards. iPhone and Watch icons stay identical. Source SVG `ios/Design/AppIcon.svg`.
- **Lift sensor: rep floor per lift** (#424): one 8 cm minimum for every lift was small next to real ranges (bench 30–45 cm, squat and presses 40–70 cm), so the first reps of a set could still count wobble. The minimum travel now follows the lift: 15 cm for squats and overhead presses, 12 cm for bench, rows and deadlifts, 10 cm for curls and raises, 6 cm for shrugs, calf raises and short machine moves. Machine mode uses its machine's family, so leg press needs 15 cm. Relaxed needs ×0.85 and Strict ×1.25. Practice mode counts like a bench. The Counting hints in Settings give the new numbers.
- **Gym map editor: smooth blocks + swap** (#422, design A + swap from C, `gym-zones/v4/proposals.png`): editing the map felt choppy and messy (resize jumped on a 5 % grid while dragging, blocks overlapped, icon clutter, a block could cover the door). Blocks now follow the finger 1:1 and spring onto the grid on release, with a light tick for each grid cell crossed. A block that moves or grows pushes the blocks it lands on aside (never overlaps), and the floor in front of the entrance stays clear (also after moving the door). Drop a block with its centre on another and the two **swap** places (lime dashed outline + **Swap** pill while hovering). The resize handle shows only on the selected block (bigger, lime). Blocks show the zone name and the number of pieces, no icon grid. Engine `GymLayout.settle`, `swapPlaces`, `zone(at:)`, `doorClearance`; tests `GymMapEditTests`.
- **3D position guide: a real athlete, real grips, real machines** (#408): the guide's clay mannequin (#399) looked like a cartoon, machines stopped halfway (the cable tower ended at 1.1 m, no cable or handle in the hand), arm positions were off, bodies floated above benches and dumbbells sat at the wrist with open hands. Look B from `exercise-positions/v11-looks.png` (anatomy on the dark stage, picked over a white anatomy chart and natural skin): a lean athletic male body (MakeHuman CC0 base mesh with muscle targets, subdivided, abs / pecs / serratus carved, shorts; no genitals) as a grey sculpted figure, every body vertex mapped to a muscle (quads, hamstrings, calves, forearms and triceps split into heads), a shallow groove and a dark line along each muscle border, fine fibres on every muscle, and the worked muscles in orange-red tissue with fibres running the way the muscle pulls. The body is posed by its full skeleton (spine and neck share the trunk's bend, shoulder girdle follows the arm, two-bone IK for arms and legs, delta-mush skinning); hands close into a fist with the thumb folded over the fingers on anything they hold, the fist's hollow sits on bars and handles, dumbbells and kettlebells move into the hand (one weight between both hands for goblet / two-hand lifts), free hands lie flat on the floor and toes bend flat in push-ups and planks. A contact pass over all 434 exercises fits benches, seats and blocks to the body, lowers or lifts floating or sunk bodies (hanging and standing kept), gives cable towers their full 2 m with a top cap, guide rods, plate stack and pin, puts uprights under free-hanging pull-up bars, turns short bars in dumbbell exercises into dumbbells and balls in kettlebell exercises into kettlebells, adds the ball crunch's ball, flattens wall-sized "mats" and recentres scenes exported far from the camera. Legs trailing under a bar (pull-ups, chin-ups, hangs, knee raises at the bottom) hang the way a person's do, knees bent back with the feet together and toes pointed, instead of the 2D kneeling silhouette with the feet on the floor; bilateral poses keep both legs the same. Grips follow the exercise: curls hold dumbbells and bars palms up, dumbbells sit across the hand for curls, presses and rows and along it for hammer curls and carries, a cable handle held by both hands becomes a straight bar with the cable clipped to its middle, and dumbbell exercises found through the catalog's equipment get their dumbbells. Tooling: `ios/Tools/illustrations/body.py` (build + `ExerciseBody.bin.z`, 1.1 MB), `rig.py` (poser), `contact.py` (equipment pass, report of every change); app: `BodyRig` (binary reader, same solver, 12 mush passes off the main thread, posed meshes cached per exercise and key) and the look-B shader, studio lights and dumbbell / kettlebell props in `PositionGuideView`. GymCore `BodyRigTests`.
- **Release notes for the 2026-10-17 bundle** (What's new `2026-10-17.1`, 5 cards): cleaner Apple Watch (#393), coach voice picker (#397), 3D position guide (#400), change or remove an exercise on the fly (#396), Settings regrouping (#398) with the climbing Train bars (#392) in one card. `2026-10-16.1` moved to the history as build 39.
- **Settings regrouped, clearer options** (#394): Settings had about 25 cards in one long list, the Apple Watch and workout-flow options opened pop-up menus over the cards, and subtitles were long arrow chains ("iPhone workout → Ask on Watch"). Design B + C with option layout 2 (enhanced) from `settings-regroup/`. Below the profile header: a **Search settings** field (every row, with keywords: "face id", "passkeys", "velocity"…) and a sticky **chapter bar** (You, Training, Apple Watch, Look & apps, Account, Help) that jumps to a chapter and lights up the one on screen. Each chapter is one gradient card with short rows: icon, title and the plain current value right ("Ask", "Smart next", "Stop at −20 %", "5 s"). **You**: Pro, Community profile, Your gym, Body & Health. **Training**: Training method, After last set, Workout coach, Reminders & alerts. **Apple Watch**: Start from iPhone, Action button, Lift sensor. **Look & apps**: Style, Widgets, MonkeyGrade. **Account**: Password, Email, 2-step & passkeys, Signed-in devices, Face ID lock, Export, Sign out, Delete account. **Help**: What's new, Privacy, Terms, Support. Pop-up menus are gone: **Apple Watch** and **Workout flow** pages show every option as an icon chip (picked one lime with a tick) and a hint saying what the picked option does. Demo screens `watch-options`, `workout-flow`.
- **Train → Climbing: Log climb and Wall session on one line** (#390): the Wall session options filled most of the Climbing section. Design B "slim bars" from `climbing-train-cards/proposals.png`: **Log climb** (climbs this week) and **Wall session** (last day type picked) sit side by side as slim gradient bars; Wall session opens its own page with the day tiles, Before / Climb / After bands and the start button. **Around climbing days** sits just under the two bars, on the Wall session page and in **Settings → Training** (climbers, searchable). Demo screen `wall-session` shows the new page.
- **Release notes for the 2026-10-16 bundle** (What's new `2026-10-16.1`, 5 cards): Ask Coach chat (#382), Coach Monkey in your workout (#380), bigger player picture + lock-screen logging (#373, #371), warm-ups that fit you (#375, #377), Watch rep counting beta (#384). Quest map Today label fix (#369) in the ChangeLog only. `2026-10-15.3` moved to the history as build 38.
- **Warm-up and cool-down cardio timed by the evidence** (#375): generated warm-ups often had 4 min (or less) of cardio. Most of what a general warm-up does (raise muscle temperature) comes in the first ~5–10 easy-to-moderate minutes, and drills plus ramp-up sets do the specific part. The automatic warm-up now starts with 5 min of easy cardio, 6 before a lower-body day (lower, legs, full lower, posterior), one more minute from age 50 (birth year in Body & Health); conditioning days keep 4 min (their interval block opens with its own easy build-up); the cardio never takes more than the warm-up minus a minute, so at least two drills still fit (ramp-up sets follow in the strength block). Quick sessions under 30 min keep their short raise. Cool-down: the flush after hard cardio goes from 2 to 3 min when the cool-down has 8 min or more, and the easy-cardio cool-down stays between 3 and 5 min (longer adds little for recovery). Members can still change any time. New tests in `WarmupStyleTests`.
- **Workout player: bigger movement picture, everything in a fixed place** (#373): the movement animation had become small, the Coming up strip took space and moved depending on what else showed, and it repeated the Up next bar. Design C upright and A + C mix on its side from `player-layout/`. The Coming up strip (upright) and the coming-up column (landscape) are gone; the exercise picture takes their space (about twice as large). One **smart Up next bar** stays in the same place above the controls in both orientations: the next set or exercise with its target, the two after it ("then …"), exercises left and the done-by time; tap or swipe up opens the Workout map. Upright order never changes: exercise card → set card / timer → Up next → controls. Landscape: exercise card at full height on the left (Form / Not here? / Setup stacked beside the picture), set card, effort tiles with ‹ › and Up next on the right; during rest the ring with +15 s / Skip, the optional effort and the paired drill replace only the set card and tiles. Fixed: **Not here?** and **Form** overlapping each other and the rep counter, **Attempt PR** cut to "At…", kg shown as "…" and "reps" wrapping in landscape. UI test `WorkoutMapUITests.testUpNextBarOpensMapAndButtonsDontOverlap`.
- **Release notes for the 2026-10-15 evening bundle** (What's new `2026-10-15.3`, 5 cards): one tap per set + workout reopens after iOS closes the app (#358, #360), Coming up strip / workout map / landscape player (#363, #364), Watch joins in + Ultra Action button (#348, #350), machine setup notes (#356), change focus / swap day / split superset / Skip fix (#344, #353, #341). `2026-10-15.2` moved to the history as build 36.
- **What's new `2026-10-15.3`: climbing card** (#366, #367): Climbing gym days card added; machine setup notes and change focus / swap day / split superset / Skip fix share one card ("Your gym, your day") to stay at 5.
- **See what's left without leaving the player** (#361): mid-workout there was no way to see the rest of the workout, and the progress dots looked tappable but did nothing. Designs A + C from `workout-overview/proposals.png`: the free space under the exercise card shows a **Coming up** strip (block-coloured tiles of every exercise still to do, swipe sideways, tap one to see its form; shown only when it fits, so the player never scrolls), with exercises left and the estimated end time. Tapping the progress dots, **Map** on the strip, or **See what's left** in the pause menu opens the read-only **Workout map**: every block with done / now / skipped / left, sets × reps, exercises left, sets left and done-by time. Nothing there edits the workout. New demo screen `player-map`, UI test `WorkoutMapUITests`.
- **Smoother workout flow: 1 tap per set** (#357): a loaded set took 3 taps (SET DONE, effort, Continue after the rest). Mix of designs A + B from `smoother-flow/proposals.png`: the effort tiles (0 · 1 · 2 · 3 · 4+) replace **SET DONE**, so one tap logs the set and its effort; **Done, no rating** logs it without one and the rest screen then offers the picker as optional. The rest ends by itself (3-2-1 taps, then the player is back, no Continue). After an exercise's last set a **Next: …** bar counts down 5 s and moves on (tap it to go now, tap the ring to stay); a timed next item is shown but never started for you. Settings → **Workout flow**: straight on, 3 / 5 / 10 s countdown, or wait for NEXT. Watch: an effort press with no rest running completes the set with that rating; the main button during the countdown moves on. RIR + readiness load suggestions now use the rated working sets when some are unrated (reps rules when none are). New demo screen `player-next`.
- **Name and @nickname both on the Settings card** (#335): Settings showed two "nicknames": the header card edited the account name, the **You** card showed the Community nickname. Design B from `nickname-sync/proposals.png`: the header card shows the name with the **@nickname** under it, both edited in place (the nickname saves to the same record as Community, with the same rules; taken or invalid nicknames show an error and revert). The **You** card is now **Community profile** (photo and about) and its page no longer has a nickname field.
- **Release notes for the next bundle** (What's new `2026-10-15.2`): Start in one look (#337) and name + @nickname (#335). New UI test `TrainFitUITests` checks Start workout sits above the tab bar on Train without scrolling. `2026-10-15.1` moved to the history as build 35.
- **Release notes for the 2026-10-15 bundle** (What's new `2026-10-15.1`): 5 cards covering #309 animation realism, #310-#313 data protection, #305 Settings rename, #304 Log climb on rest days and #303 Coach Monkey card.
- **Account is now Settings; What Monkey learned moved to your profile** (#305): the fifth tab and its page are called **Settings** (gear icon), and every "Account → …" hint in the app, widgets, Watch and legal pages now says "Settings → …". **What Monkey learned** left Training profile: design B from `learned-settings/proposals.png` puts it as a strip inside the profile card at the top of Settings, with the top liked (lime ♥) and avoided (red ✕) moves and the count; tap it for the same page as before.
- **Release notes for the 2026-10-14 evening release** (What's new `2026-10-14.2`, 5 cards): Home Screen widgets (#297); Monkey learns what you like (#299) with edit before starting (#295) and custom warm-up / cool-down (#293); finish a workout early (#300); Train does more: Coach Monkey chips (#278), tap any day + Log climb (#277), Close + Undo (#285); gym zones v2 (#289), programme look fixes (#280, #281, #282) and the Progress section bar fix (#286). `2026-10-14.1` moved to the history as build 33.
- **Gym zones: see each item's zone, By zone view, items without a zone** (#289), after build 33 feedback (designs A + B from `gym-zones/v2/proposals.png`). The zone dot on each picture blended into it, zone chips slid sideways and hid zones, and an item couldn't be left without a zone. **Pictures**: each ticked picture now has a ribbon with its zone's name in the zone's colours and a border in those colours (grey dashed with **NO ZONE** when it has none). **Zone chips** wrap on as many lines as needed, followed by a **No zone** eraser chip: with it on, tapping pictures clears their zone (`GymLayout.unassign`, unit tested); items without a zone fit any zone in a workout. **By type / By zone** switch: By zone shows one bar per zone in its colours with its items, then **No zone**. Touch and hold any picture to move it to another zone or **No zone**. Demo screen `equipment-by-zone`.
- **Release notes for the 2026-10-14 morning release** (What's new `2026-10-14.1`): 5 cards covering #268/#269 programmes (4 cards merged into 2), #266 missed workouts + Next up, #274 gym zones, #271/#273 gym save + Add a gym and #264 calendar day sheet.
- **Your gym: one Add a gym button** (#273): the separate "Gyms near me" button, "Set up a gym in a few swipes" card and search field are now one lime **Add a gym** button (design C from `gym-setup-entry/proposals.png`) opening three choices: **Near me** (map), **Search by name** (search field with suggestions), **Home or hotel** (swipe setup, no map place). Picking a new gym from the map or search still opens its swipe setup; redoing this gym stays on the Equipment page. Demo screen `profile-gym-add`.
- **Release notes for the 2026-10-14 night release** (What's new `2026-10-13.4`): the Train widgets card (#260) is folded into the Style picker card (#251) as **Make Train and the app yours**, keeping 5 cards: Training programmes, Make Train and the app yours, 10 new workouts, Fluid motion, Safer account.
- **Style picker shows each look** (#251): Account → **Style** had text-only cards, so members couldn't see what a style looks like. Now (design C, `iphone-gamification/style-preview-c.png`) one full-height card per style to swipe through, each with real iPhone and Watch screens (the Watch overlapping the iPhone; images `StylePreview-Phone/Watch-<style>` in Assets, ~190 KB), page dots, and **Use <style>** (or **✓ Your style**) below; tapping a card still applies it. Fixed: **Monkey coach** was cut to "Monkey co…" next to the DEFAULT badge (badge now above the title). What's new `2026-10-13.4`; `2026-10-13.3` moved to the history (build 31).
- **Compact week + readiness card** (#246): the Train week card with the readiness pill inside (#208) took about 280 pt. Now one slim card (about 80 pt, design C from `week-readiness/compact-proposals.png`): sessions done as a ring on the left, the phase line (e.g. EASE IN · W1/4 · RPE −1, tap → training cycle sheet), today's readiness verdict as the headline with HRV / RHR / sleep (or "Today's session eased") below (tap → readiness sheet), and the slim week bar (tap a week to switch). The card fades from the phase colour into the readiness colour. Without readiness the phase is the headline, with **Connect Health for readiness** when Health isn't connected, else sessions and minutes. `ReadinessPill` removed; `WeekHero` + `DoneRing`, `Readiness.markers`. What's new `2026-10-13.3`.
- **Progress page structure: page map + pinned section bar** (#243): the level card and the big streak hero pushed everything else below the fold, so members couldn't tell what Progress holds. Designs A + B from `progress-structure/proposals.png`: the streak hero is now a slim lime row (streak, best, this week vs goal ring) under the level card; then a **page map** of 6 bold cards (Training this week vs goal, Body weight / featured measurement, Records, Badges, Muscles most worked, History count + last session), each jumps to its section; then a **section bar** (Training · Body · Records · Badges · Muscles · History) that pins under the title while scrolling, lights the section on screen and jumps on tap. Each section opens with a bold colour header. Muscle balance is its own section. Replaces the 3 top layouts of #238 (Account → Progress layout removed). Demo screen `progress-records` (replaces `progress-strip`, `progress-tabs`, `progress-layout`). What's new `2026-10-13.3`.
- **Progress: highlights at the top, 3 layouts to choose from** (#238): measurements, personal records and achievements were buried below the charts. The top of Progress (under the streak) now shows them in the member's layout, picked in **Account → Progress layout** (proposals `progress-top/proposals.png`, all three built). **Hub grid** (default): 4 big gradient tiles: Measurements (featured value with the biggest change, tap → Measurements), Records (count + top 2 PRs), Achievements (unlocked/total, ring to the next badge), Body (VO₂max and body weight with change). **Shortcut strip**: a swipeable row of tiles (featured measurement, top PR, badges, body weight, VO₂max, history). **Section tabs**: a Training · Body · Records · Badges · History pill bar switches the page; Body shows Measurements plus a tile per logged measurement (tap → its chart and entries). In Hub and Strip, tiles scroll to their section. Live tiles (#218) unchanged under Training. Preference stored on the device. Demo screens `progress-strip`, `progress-tabs`, `progress-layout`. What's new `2026-10-13.2`.
- **3D body map looks like a body and only turns around itself** (#225): the 3D figure was the 2D outline inflated like a balloon (blobby limbs, dark specks at the edges) and dragging up/down tilted it. It is now a sculpted body (head, neck, ribcage, abdomen, pelvis, shoulders, arms, hands, legs and feet as smooth joined volumes fitted to the 2D outline, F figure with its own proportions), generated once by `ios/Tools/bodymap/generate3d.py` and shipped as compressed meshes (`Resources/body-male.bm3d`, `body-female.bm3d`, ~0.85 MB each). Dragging only turns it around its vertical axis; vertical drags scroll the page. Designs in project files `3d-body-map/v2/`.
- **Live progress tiles** (#218): the Progress stat tiles were static totals. Design A from `progress-tiles/proposals.png`: a lime **Week / Month / All** switch (remembered) above the tiles; each tile (Sessions, Trained, Lifted, Sets, new **Days active**) shows that period's value, a trend line (last 8 weeks / 6 months / up to 12 months) and the change vs last week / month. **Tap a tile** to flip it into a full-width bar chart of that metric with the best week / month crowned; tap again to close. Climbs tile stays when you climbed. **Consistency calendar**: tap a lit day to open that workout. Free still charts the last 4 weeks. Engine: `Progression.periodSummary`, `StatPeriod`, `StatMetric`. Demo screen `progress-flipped`. What's new `2026-10-12.6`.
- **CI checks Watch complications are shipped** (#190): the iOS build and the TestFlight archive now fail if `MonkeyWorkoutWatchComplications.appex` is missing from the Watch app, and print its bundle id, extension point and App Group entitlements.
- **Week and readiness in one card** (#208): Train showed the week card (Week 1 of 4 · Base, Ease in, 0/3 done, week bar) and a separate Readiness card below it. Readiness is now a bright pill inside the week card, above the week bar (design B from `week-readiness/proposals.png`): verdict colour and icon, HRV / RHR / sleep (or "Today's session eased" on eased days), info icon; tap opens the readiness explanation as before. Phase header still opens the training cycle sheet, week bar still switches weeks. Health not connected → a **Connect Health for readiness** row in the same place asks Apple Health; connected without recovery data or still loading → no pill. `ReadinessCard` replaced by `ReadinessPill`. What's new `2026-10-12.5`.
- **Gradient tile switchers** (#192): Explore's Muscles / Categories / Workouts switcher is no longer the grey system segmented control. New `GradientSegments` (Theme.swift): equal-width tiles with an icon tile, heavy rounded title and optional count (Explore: muscles, category types, workouts); the picked tile is a vivid gradient, the others dark cards; selection haptic. Same control replaces every other grey segmented picker: Community feed (Members / Following / Mine), workout visibility (Only me / Members / Public), Pro staging test (Server / Off / Preview / Enforced, icon over title), custom exercise builder (Reps / Seconds, Front / Back), pose designer (Start / End). What's new item in `2026-10-12.4`.
- **Community header: round profile picture** (#151): the toolbar capsule that covered the Community title is gone. The feed has its own header like Train: **Community** title, a lime **+** (Share a win) and your round profile picture, which opens your profile directly.
- **Train: tap the Next up card to open the session** (#150): tapping anywhere on the Next up card (title, muscles, body map) opens the session overview, same as the list button. Start workout stays a separate button.
- **Sheets close with a round X** (#149): the "Cancel" capsule that covered the title (New variation and others) becomes a small round **X** at the top right on every dismiss-only sheet: New variation, Add exercises, Generate from muscles, Swap exercise, Log climb, Around climbing, Add to plan, Away, Move to, Add workout, Share a win, New exercise.
- **Train: Plan button split into Variation and Edit plan** (#147): the "Plan · Variation · edit" menu button becomes two gradient buttons, **Variation** (opens New variation, Pro badge when locked) and **Edit plan** (opens the training profile). Calendar, Variation and Edit plan now sit in one equal-width row under the Train title.
- **Smoke test covers legal pages and subscriptions**: staging / production smoke checks `/privacy`, `/terms`, `/support` return 200 with no "to be completed" placeholders, `GET /v1/me/entitlement` refuses requests without a token and `POST /v1/app-store/notifications` rejects an unsigned payload.
- **Training profile in sections** (#99): Train → Training profile is a hub of gradient cards (Goal, Your week, Week to week, Training style, Your gym), each showing its current value and opening its own screen with Save. Saving a section keeps the current week and variation. **Your gym** is also on top of Community (same screen). Onboarding keeps the full guided form. Demo screens `profile`, `profile-gym` in CI screenshots.
- **Train: week header as a gradient card** (#116): replaces the plain ring + text header from #104 with an Explore-style card: icon tile left, "Week 1 of 4 · Base" + the week's job (Ease in / Build / Peak week / Recover) and rule, big "0/3 done" right, week bar inside. Colour follows the cycle phase. Tap for the phase explainer; no truncated text. Calendar and Plan (new variation / edit training profile) move from plain nav-bar icons to gradient buttons next to the Train title (icon tile, name, "13 planned" / "Variation · edit").
- **Whole exercise card opens the exercise page** (#101): on a session, tapping anywhere on an exercise card (muscle chips, equipment line, padding) opens its details, not only the name or picture. Long-press offers Exercise details / Swap exercise. The exercise page's bottom "Swap for" list is replaced by the Swap exercise button.
- **Train: today's workout first** (#104): compact week header (small progress ring left, week / phase chip on one line, week bar), Readiness as one compact row (verdict + HRV / resting HR / sleep, tap for details), then Next up. Calendar moves to a top-right icon next to the settings button (calendar card removed).
- **Workout library moves to Explore** (#95): Explore → Muscles / Categories / **Workouts**. Workouts shows New, your workouts and the built-in ones as gradient cards, and "Browse all workouts" (search, members' workouts). Start now / Add to plan unchanged. Train keeps the week plan only. Demo screen `explore-workouts` in CI screenshots.
- **New variation is member-controlled** (#98): Train → ⋯ → New variation opens a sheet instead of re-rolling the whole week. Pick the days (done sessions and library workouts locked), what changes (Accessories and Warm-up & mobility on by default; Main lifts, Explosive, Cardio machine opt-in), preview every swap (old → new), lock any exercise to keep it, Shuffle the rest, Apply. Swaps keep the movement pattern/regions (same muscle focus per day), unit, sides, level, equipment, and never add heavy grip work; no duplicates within a day; sets/reps unchanged. Exercises swapped and kept (#101) start locked. Stored with the week in the synced profile (`variation`, optional; survives re-plans), Undo card on Train, Reset in the sheet. No longer resets done sessions. Demo screen `variation` added to CI screenshots.
- **Smoke test on production**: the "Staging smoke test" workflow takes a `target` input (staging | production) so the same checks verify prod after a promote.
- **Explore style everywhere (#88)**: sign-in, sign-up, email verification and password reset use gradient headers, dark rounded fields and the lime button; Body & Health shows height, weight, birth year, gender and sex as tappable gradient cards; History, Add to plan, Share a win, community profile, technique and exercise pages, custom workout editor, readiness, PR and muscle pages, session and workout-complete stats use gradient cards instead of grey tiles and lists. Disabled buttons and unselected cards stay readable; titles wrap instead of truncating; one title per screen; image credit line removed. CI screenshots `signin`, `signup`, `verify`.
- **Community join (#90)**: joining asks who sees your wins, Members preselected; new profiles default to Members so followers get notified of your records unless you pick Only me. Existing profiles unchanged.
- **Workout player redesign** (#59): one gradient hero card per exercise in its block's colour (name, big target, start-position picture that plays the movement once on tap instead of the looping two-frame animation, muscle chips, Form button for setup & technique); body map and inline form checkpoints removed from the player; set card, Up next and controls always visible. Timed work (rower, planks, cardio, intervals) gets START / PAUSE / RESUME with a countdown ring that completes the set at 0 (interval "easy" part used as rest). Rest screen shows the paired mobility drill as its own card. Completing an exercise's last set moves straight to the next exercise (after the one-tap effort when the set is logged), no extra NEXT tap. Main button label always on one line. **Attempt PR** button on weighted strength exercises opens the PR attempt flow (#60).
- Exercise completion circles in the session overview are status only; exercises complete only when done in the player after Start workout (#57). Removed "Mark session done (ticked items)".
- **Account redesign** (#71): Account tab in the Explore card style. Profile hero (initials, tap-to-edit name, email, Apple/verified chips, session count), gradient cards for Body & Apple Health (current weight), MonkeyGrade (session count), password, email, signed-in devices (active count), Face ID lock as a toggle card, Export my data, Sign out / Sign out everywhere pills, outlined Delete account. Sub-screens (Body & Health, password, email, devices, delete account) get a gradient header, card fields and a lime action button; Body & Health pickers become menus. Same behaviour: Save pops back, keyboard Done, gender self-describe. Reusable `AccountCard` for new Account rows. Demo screens `account`, `account-password`, `account-email` added to CI screenshots.
- **Progress tab redesign** (#82) in the Explore card style: lime streak hero (week streak, best streak, this-week ring vs goal), gradient stat tiles (sessions, time trained, tonnage, climbs in 12 weeks with top grade, or total sets), gradient weekly bars (lime when goal hit), strength and Apple Health trends with big latest value and change, PR attempts as gold cards plus top lifts, climbing card (logged, Health, MonkeyGrade), achievements header with next badge. New users get a "Day one" hero, a First milestones card (first session, first PR, first climb), today's square outlined, ghost weekly bars and a next-badge hint instead of zeros. Badge ids and `BadgeSheet` unchanged (notification deep links). CI screenshot `progress-empty`.
- **TestFlight upload is manual only** (#53): merges to main no longer archive/upload a TestFlight build; run **TestFlight (Staging)** via workflow_dispatch when a batch is ready. Staging API deploy on merge unchanged.
- **Training profile redesign** (onboarding "Your training" + Train → edit) (#37): vivid Explore-style goal cards, each showing recommended gym sessions/week, session length and training mix (power/strength/mobility/cardio); picking a goal applies its recommended sessions. Session count, experience and length as big tap targets with a "REC" marker; per-session / per-week totals card. Climbing days and weekdays behind an **I climb** switch. Gym days as large day tiles. Gym: scoped gyms first (Fitnesspark Puls 5) or search any gym via Apple Maps; selected gym saved in the profile (optional field, older profiles unchanged); equipment editor in a sheet.
- **Add exercises** picker redesigned in the Explore look (#46): gradient category tiles with live counts, muscle-group chips (then individual muscles), two-column picture cards with category pill and lime tap-order badge, sticky "Add N exercises" button. CI screenshots add `picker`.
- App renamed **MonkeyWorkout** (#21): Xcode project/target/schemes, display names, bundle IDs `Com.app.MonkeyWorkout` (Release + TestFlight staging) and `Com.app.MonkeyWorkout.dev` (DEV); App Store Connect SKU `Monkeyworkout`, Apple ID `6819971090` documented; signing team `U7VAR53G86` set.
- API: Sign in with Apple accepts the new bundle IDs, matched case-insensitively; emails sent as MonkeyWorkout (#21).
- Every session now ends with mandatory static stretching (≥ 3 stretches + breathing, never dropped by the time budget); block renamed "Stretching & cool-down" (#14).
- Climber prehab (shoulder + forearm antagonist) both supersetted into long main-lift rests (#14).
- Added side-delt stretch so every muscle has at least one stretch (#14).
- iOS app (SwiftUI, iOS 17+, #9): welcome + Sign in with Apple, email sign-up with 6-digit verification, sign in, forgot/reset password, privacy notice; account screen with name, change/set password, signed-in devices, Face ID app lock, data export, sign out / sign out all devices, account deletion.
- On-device workout engine (`GymCore/WorkoutEngine`, Swift port of the validated generator, #1): session length from goal × frequency, weekly splits, warm-up → explosive → strength (supersets, paired mobility) → climber mobility → cardio → load-derived cool-down, 4-week mesocycle, Puls 5 equipment profile, 150 exercises.
- Plan UI: training profile, week view with mesocycle navigation and variations, session view with tick-off, swap, rest timer with haptics, weight logging with last weight.
- `GymCore/APIClient`: Keychain token storage, single-flight token refresh, profile + log sync with offline queue.
- XcodeGen project with Debug/Staging/Release configs, privacy manifest, macOS CI (package tests + simulator builds).
- Auth & sync API (`backend/`, #8): email/password registration with emailed 6-digit verification, login with lockout, Sign in with Apple, rotating refresh tokens with replay detection, forgot/reset & change password, logout / logout all devices, account deletion, data export, profile + workout-log sync, per-IP rate limiting.
- API Docker image, CI (typecheck, tests, image build), staging auto-deploy and manual "Promote to production" (same image) workflows.
- Repository bootstrap (README, CHANGELOG, .gitignore).

### Fixed
- **Signed out for no reason** (#469): refresh tokens rotate and replaying a used one revokes the whole session. When the server rotated a token but the answer never reached the app (app suspended, backgrounded or offline mid-refresh), the app kept the old token and its next refresh signed the member out. A replayed token whose replacement was never used is now treated as a lost answer: the unused replacement is revoked and a new pair issued. A token claimed less than 10 s ago (parallel refresh still rotating) gets 401 without ending the session. Real reuse (replacement already used) still revokes the family. Backend only. Tests in `api.test.ts`, `account-security.test.ts`.
- **Stray Undo bar over Log climb** (#460): in Log sport, changing a value then tapping Climbing showed "Trail run closed · Undo" over the climb sheet, covering its fields. Switching sport no longer counts as closing.
- **Full review #432: sign-in and account security**
  - Rate limits key on the address Fly saw (`Fly-Client-IP`), not a client-supplied `X-Forwarded-For`.
  - One-time codes (verify, reset, re-auth, email change): a code burned by wrong guesses isn't replaced inside the resend minute, at most 5 codes per account per hour, at most 20 wrong guesses per account per day (also for 2-step codes, recovery codes and re-auth passwords). Mails to one recipient ("already have an account", email-change attempts) capped at 3 per hour. New `throttle_hits` table, purged with its window.
  - Verify email takes the password typed by the inbox owner (app sends it), so a stranger who registered the same unverified email first never keeps access; Sign in with Apple linking an unverified account drops its unproven password.
  - A refresh token is claimed before rotation: two parallel refreshes can't both succeed. Notice mails after a committed change (password, 2-step, delete, email change) no longer turn the request into an error when mail fails.
  - Login lockout is per account and client IP: 10 wrong passwords from one IP lock only that IP out of the account for 15 min (a stranger can no longer lock the owner out), and a correct password clears it. 100 wrong passwords a day across all IPs lock password sign-in for a day (same 429 `account_locked` with `lockedUntil`); a password reset reopens it at once.
  - Sign in with Apple and Apple re-auth check the token's `nonce` claim: the app sends the raw `nonce` (16–128 chars) whose SHA-256 hex must match, and each nonce works once. Tokens without the claim (older app builds) are still accepted.
- **Full review #432: app and Watch fixes**
  - Sync keeps set and heart-rate edits made while an upload is in flight; after a reinstall the profile is pulled before onboarding (leftover sign-in from a previous install is cleared), and the profile is pulled again on sync so two devices don't overwrite each other.
  - A workout running across midnight Sunday survives the week roll; a workout finished on the Watch is still recorded when its session left the plan; Watch recovers its Health session after a crash; a Hyrox race can't be saved twice.
  - Server lists with one unreadable item no longer break history sync; unknown moves in shared workouts can't crash the app.
  - Watch upload key no longer copied to the App Group; MonkeyGrade link cleared on sign-out; Gym zone "Taken?" learning kept across launches; Watch privacy manifest, purchase history declared; Body & Health says which Health data is synced.
- **Full review #432: backend fixes**
  - Coach chat and gym scan take the day's slot atomically before the model call (given back if it fails) and run at most 2 calls per member at once, so parallel requests can't pass the daily cap.
  - Production refuses Sandbox / TestFlight purchases and ignores sandbox notifications (`SUBSCRIPTION_SANDBOX=on` allows them, e.g. for App Review); an App Store notification is marked seen only after it was applied, so Apple's retry of a failed one is processed.
  - Sync pulls (`?since=`) compare in the stored ISO form and include rows at exactly `since` (a since without milliseconds skipped rows of that second); `saved` counts rows actually written.
  - Community feed and workout library page by (time, id): `nextBeforeId` + `beforeId` continue through items of the same moment; `before` alone still works.
  - Leaving the community makes your shared workouts private again; the library lists only authors with a community profile. Auto-hide counts only reporters whose account was at least 7 days old.
  - Coach voice calls: at most 20 different names per member per day, memory cache bounded to 200 recordings, Polly limit holds at 2. Achievement push texts go through the community filter. APNs signs a new provider token after a 403 Expired/InvalidProviderToken.
  - Gym lookups match names with non-ASCII first letters (Élan…) and no longer read the whole table for a name without words.
  - Backups: `BACKUP_KEEP_DAYS` / `BACKUP_OFFSITE_DAYS` that aren't whole numbers ≥ 1 fall back to the default (a typo pruned every backup); `offsite-fetch` writes `app-<day>.offsite.db` and never overwrites a file; member restore compares photo bytes, not references.
  - Storage quotas per member on sync uploads (100,000 logs; 20,000 workouts, climbs, sports, PR attempts, body measurements; 500 custom workouts; 1,000 machine notes; 200 gym reports and gym maps): past the cap new rows get 413 `quota_exceeded`, updates still pass.
  - Sync deletes reach other devices: a delete is kept as a tombstone (`sync_tombstones`, 180 days), every `?since=` pull returns `deleted: [ids]`; uploading a deleted id again (Undo) brings it back and clears its tombstone. Ops undo-workout records tombstones too.
  - Ops workflows: Free access and Fly undo workout mask the member email (undo prints a summary, `list` masks emails), Fly logs redacts emails and 6-digit codes, `setup-flyctl` pinned to a commit.
- **CI screenshots job timeout 100 → 150 min** (#443): iPhone, iPad and Watch captures ran past 100 minutes on busy Mac runners and the job was cancelled before publishing.
- **TestFlight archive fixed for the room scan** (#417): the device-only RoomPlan scanner (#406) never compiled on the simulator, so CI missed that its nested `Coordinator` (an `NSCoding` delegate) needs a stable Objective-C name; the TestFlight archive failed on it. It is now `@objc(MWRoomCaptureCoordinator)`.
- **Coach voice: quiet in silent mode, studio voices download** (#402): the coach spoke with the volume at zero and in silent mode, because its audio session used `.playback`. Without music it now uses `.ambient`, which follows the Ring/Silent switch. With music playing it still talks over the music and ducks it. It never speaks at zero volume. Studio voice downloads failed because Polly throttled 4 recordings at once (`Rate exceeded`). The server now records 2 at a time, retries with backoff, and in production records every voice ahead in the background. The app gives failed lines a second pass. **Coach live check** gains a `voice` option that makes one real Polly recording.
- **Train page sync error "Too big: expected string to have <=64 characters"** (#404): sets logged in a workout added to the plan carry a session id like `custom-<uuid>~plan-<uuid>` (85 characters); the server capped log `sessionId` at 64, so the whole log batch was refused and stayed pending, and the Train page showed the raw validation text. Server now accepts log `sessionId` up to 200 and `exerciseId` up to 128 (logs, PR attempts, custom workouts, machine notes); stuck logs sync on the next sync, no app update needed. The app's sync line never shows server text: "Some changes haven't synced yet. We'll keep trying." (or the offline / signed-out message). Test in `api.test.ts`.
- **Apple Watch layout** (#389): text sat on the screen edge (clipped by the round corners), the main button or last row was cut at the bottom, grey system titles were hard to read on colour, the settings gear crowded the clock, and the mirrored player's ring had tiny text with the button overlapping it. Mix A + B from `watch-layout/proposals.png`: every Watch screen gets 8 pt side margins; grey navigation titles are replaced by bold white titles in the screen; **Home style** moves from the gear next to the clock to a row under the home cards; the week list, workout, progress and done screens use the same Explore cards (icon tile left, big number right) in all three home styles, with XP and badges as card details in Monkey coach; START / GO! and DONE stay pinned at the bottom while the list scrolls under them; the mirrored player ring (Monkey coach) shows exercise and set above it, only the load inside, drawn within its frame. Small text enlarged (card titles 12 pt, details 10 pt, Next / coach line 11 pt). Home styles and the workout player keep their looks.
- **Ask Coach still failed after the API key was replaced** (#387): Fly setup only staged the new `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`; the running staging machine kept the old key after a redeploy, so the chat still answered "Coach is catching breath". Fly setup now applies staged secrets to running machines (`flyctl secrets deploy`) right away.
- **Ask Coach chat failed on every message** (#385): the chat always answered "Coach is catching breath". The Claude API refused the call (400): the API key isn't scoped to a workspace, so each request must name one. New optional `ANTHROPIC_WORKSPACE_ID` secret, sent as the `anthropic-workspace-id` header and staged by **Fly setup** (alternative: replace `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` with a workspace-scoped key). New manual **Coach live check** workflow makes one real call with the environment's key and prints the answer or the API error.
- **Quest map: "Today" only on today's stage** (#369): in the Quest map style, Train → This week labelled the next stage "Today" even on a later day (Friday showed "Today · Pull" on Sunday's stage). The next stage now reads **Today** on its own day (or when unscheduled), **Tomorrow** the day before, else **Next**.
- **Crash when coming back to the app from a notification** (#351): TestFlight build 36 aborted (`SIGABRT`, `-[UIApplication _performBlockAfterCATransactionCommitSynchronizes:]` on a Swift concurrency thread) when a member left a workout and returned by tapping a notification (e.g. rest over). The `async` `UNUserNotificationCenterDelegate` methods let Swift call UIKit's completion handler off the main thread; they are now the completion-handler forms, answered on the main thread. Also: the Face ID / Touch ID name is read once instead of a synchronous system call on every Settings render (it was blocking the main thread in the crash report); Watch and Home Screen widget snapshots no longer trap on a custom exercise not loaded yet mid-sync; a token refresh returns the new tokens instead of force-reading them back from the Keychain.
- New **TestFlight crashes** workflow (crash reports testers share, exception + crashed thread) and **Symbolicate crash** workflow (rebuilds a commit unsigned and maps crash-report offsets to code with `atos`).
- **Progress: Programmes card leads straight to picking one** (#345): with no programme running or finished, the **Start one** card on Progress scrolled down to the Programmes section, which only said to go to Explore → Programmes. The card now opens Explore → Programmes directly (`AppModel.openExploreProgrammes`, `exploreJump`), and the "No programme running" line in the section opens it too. With a programme running or finished, the card still scrolls to the section.
- **Workout survives iOS closing the app** (#359): the iPhone app runs no background work (no background modes; rest timer, Live Activity and "Rest over" alert are clock-based), so iOS can close it while the phone is locked mid-workout. The player now saves that it was running and any rest in progress when going to the background; the next launch (within 3 h) reopens the player at the same set, counts the time away as training and keeps the rest running. Otherwise a Live Activity left on the lock screen is cleared.
- **No starting or logging ahead** (#343): a later day picked in the Next up week strip offered **Start workout** and **Log climb**. A later day now shows **See workout** (its overview) instead of Start, and no Log climb; a later rest or climbing day keeps **Add workout** to plan one ("Climb planned. Log it on the day, or plan a workout."). Today and earlier days are unchanged.
- **Skip on a missed workout works** (#340): Skip only flagged the session, and every screen still listed it as missed, so the tap seemed to do nothing. Skip now lets it go: it leaves the catch-up sheet (which closes when nothing is left), the Calendar day sheet shows **Skipped** instead of the catch-up buttons, Next up says "skipped", and the missed chip, counts, Coach Monkey tip, Watch and Home widgets ignore it. The week strip keeps the day dimmed and tapping it follows it in Next up; it still counts as missed in history. New UI test `MissedSkipUITests`.
- **Offline saves are kept and retried silently** (#311): body measurements (and their queued saves/deletes) and skipped missed days were not read back from the device cache after an app restart, so measurements logged offline were lost. Sync steps now run independently (one failing step no longer holds back the rest, steps that succeeded are saved), a failed sync retries by itself after 5 s, 15 s, 1 min, then every 5 min while the app is open, and again as soon as the network returns. Undo workout and removing a PR attempt now queue their server deletes (an undone workout no longer comes back when the delete failed). Signing out first tries to send unsent saves (5 s at most), then also removes the cached Health readings, the data export file and cached HTTP responses, and clears the Watch and widget snapshots.
- **Personal data kept out of HTTP caches** (#310, #311): authenticated API responses carry `Cache-Control: no-store`, and the app's API client uses its own session without a URL cache. Watch and widget snapshot files and the data export are written with file protection.
- **Train: Start workout fits without scrolling** (#337): on iPhone 17 Pro the Next up card's **Start workout** sat behind the tab bar.
