Design A "Slim stack" from `train-fit/proposals.png`, same cards and order: Coach Monkey has a smaller monkey, a 2-line tip, the tip's action as a lime pill beside the chips (own row when it doesn't fit) and dots overlaid at the bottom; Ease In shows the readiness verdict and markers on one line with a smaller ring; Next up puts the top muscles beside a smaller figure and tightens its spacing; 14 pt between Train widgets. Short phones (SE) drop the figure, muscle chips and block stripe and keep the coach tip to one line.
- **Coach Monkey chips stay inside the card** (#303): on build 34 the **next tip ↻** chip ran past the card's right edge (4 chips wider than the card, row drawn outside it). Design B from `coach-monkey-actions/overflow.png`: next tip is a round **↻** button in the card's top-right corner; the stat chips (streak, this week, missed) keep their full labels when they fit and switch to short ones (`🔥 0 ›`, `0/3 wk ›`) on narrow phones. Swipe and monkey tap still show the next tip.
- **Exercise animations: realism pass** (#309): on build 34 the pec deck fly showed the hips below the seat and the arms crossing in an impossible way. Pec deck / reverse pec deck now sit on the seat with foreshortened seated legs, and the arms swing on a 3D arc projected to the view (`pec_arms`) instead of folding in the picture plane. All 422 animations re-reviewed (keys and in-betweens; joint scan for backward-bending elbows/knees and crossing limbs): ~88 fixes. 3D-projected arms (same technique) for cable fly, high-low / low-high cable fly, cossack squat goblet hold, tate press, wide-grip cable row, cross-body hammer curl, Pallof press (now side-on to the column, pressing out), landmine rotation, sumo / kettlebell high pull, prone pulldown, thread the needle, Turkish get-up post arm; knees no longer bend backwards on leg press (3 variants), tib raise, downward dog, foam-roll calves, single-leg back extension, figure-4; elbows bend the natural way on the deadlift family, dumbbell lunges / split squats / step-ups, hip thrusts, leg curl, seated calf raises, pullovers, preacher / machine curl, wall-sit, wall / freestanding / kipping HSPU, spiderman push-up, pistol, tuck jump; equipment contact: crunch machine seat, seated lateral raise seat and legs, seated cable row footplate, sit-up and frog pump on the mat, step-up trailing foot over the box, planted feet no longer slide in split squats; technique: dumbbell snatch close to the body, cable Y-raise / rear-delt fly start poses, door pec / biceps stretches step through, side-delt stretch, hip 90/90 feet on the mat, breathing elbows on the floor, meadows row plate, lateral bound arms. What's new `2026-10-15.1`; `2026-10-14.2` moved to the history as build 34.
- **Log climb on every rest day** (#304): with climbing on, any rest day picked in the Next up strip offers **Log climb** next to Add workout (was only on planned climbing days). A climb logged from an earlier day this week is dated that day at 18:00. Design A from `missed-sessions/rest-climb/proposals.png`.
- **Next up "Back to next up" label** (#277): an unscheduled next session no longer reads "(Mon)". Missed/day-strip demo screens now use a weekday-scheduled week so the strip, missed days and climbing days show in screenshots.
- **Programme look fixes after build 33** (#280, #281, #282). **Grey days** (#280, design 1A from `programmes/fixes/item1.png`): in a programme's All workouts grid, days that aren't signature workouts (e.g. Strength foundation's Day 3) were dark grey; they now take their week's phase colour (Base, Build, Peak, Ease) and signature workouts keep the programme colour with a ★. **Two-line picker rows** (#281, design 2A from `item2.png`): on Make it mine, Goal / Split / Method values like "Explosive / athletic" wrapped beside the label, giving uneven rows and clipping the Method row; they are now stacked (label on top, value large in lime under it; new `StackedMenuRow`); Body & Health Gender and Sex show the picked value under the title too. **New workout from a programme** (#282, design 3A from `item3.png`): Pick a workout (Make it mine → Add a workout, and the builder's days) starts with a **New workout** card that opens the workout builder (Pro, like Build a workout); on **Add** the workout is saved to My workouts and added to the programme (a workout with no strength or power exercise is saved but not added, with a note).
- **Progress section bar lights the section on screen** (#286): scrolled to the bottom of Progress, the pinned bar still lit **Badges** while Muscles and History filled the screen, because a chip lit only once its section's top reached the bar and the short end sections never get there. The reading line now slides from under the bar down to the screen bottom over the last screenful of scrolling, so Muscles and then History light as they come into view; a tapped chip stays lit until the member scrolls (so tapping **Muscles** at the page end no longer lights History); the bar still scrolls to keep the lit chip visible.
- **Coach Monkey card: every chip and tip leads somewhere** (#278): tapping the card only showed the next tip; its chips (streak, this week, missed, tap me) did nothing. Designs A + B from `coach-monkey-actions/proposals.png`: **🔥 wk ›** opens Progress → Training, **n/3 this week ›** opens the calendar, **n missed ›** opens the catch-up sheet, **next tip ↻** replaces "tap me". Each tip gets its own lime button: **Start <workout>** (opens the player), **Catch up**, **See streak** / **See progress**, **Open <exercise>** on the best-lift tip, **See levels**, **Open calendar**. Tap the monkey or swipe left/right for the next tip; dots show which tip is on.
- **Next up day strip: tap any day** (#277): tapping a planned or done day switches the Next up card to that day's workout (day turns white, eyebrow "<Day> · planned/done/today"); tapping a rest day shows **Rest day** with **Add workout** for that day; **Back to next up** returns. Missed days still open the catch-up sheet. Design A from `missed-sessions/day-strip/proposals.png`. **Log climb in Next up** (design A from `missed-sessions/log-climb/proposals.png`): climbers get an orange **Log climb** button beside Start workout (it takes the list button's place; the card still opens the overview), climbing days show orange in the strip, and a picked climbing day reads **Climbing day** with Log climb + Add workout. Demo screens `train-day-picked`, `train-rest-day`.
- **Missed sessions and Next up** (#266): on Wednesday Train's Next up still showed Monday's Push that was never done, and the coach said "0/3 this week. 3 to go!". Now a session of an earlier day this week that wasn't done is **missed**: Next up shows the first session from today on (eyebrow **Today**, **Tomorrow** or the weekday); with nothing left it shows **No more sessions this week** instead of Week complete. Design C from `missed-sessions/proposals.png`: Next up gets a **week strip** (missed days red, done lime, planned lit, today ringed); a red day opens a **catch-up sheet** with **Log it** (sheet dated that day: start time, length, sets done and kg per exercise, prefilled from the plan and last weights; the workout is saved on its day and counts for the week, XP and progress), **Mark done** (saved as planned), **Do today** (moved to today, next to today's session) and **Skip** (stays missed, day dimmed). Coach monkey says "0/3 done, 1 missed. 2 to go!" with a yellow **1 missed** chip and no "week done" party while a session is missed; the compact Next up widget gets a red **N missed** chip; This week rows and Calendar tiles show **Missed** (day sheet has the same four actions). Watch: Next up follows the phone, never an earlier missed session; missed days get the same red **MISSED** tag on home cards and This week lists (all three Watch styles), the Watch coach isn't in "week done" mode while one is missed, and complications no longer show a missed session as next (`WSession.missed`; Watch demo screen `missed`). Fixed too: without a programme the plan never moved to a new calendar week (last week's ticks stayed, Train stayed on the old cycle week while Calendar showed the next); a new week now starts on its own on Monday (next cycle week, ticks, moves, added workouts, variation and per-workout styles cleared, `SyncedProfile.weekOf`). Engine: `WeekProgress` (missed, next, remaining, weeks between; unit tested). Demo screens `train-missed`, `missed-catchup`, `missed-log`, `calendar-missed`. What's new: in the release's notes.
- **Calendar day sheet shows everything on open** (#264): tapping a day in Calendar opened the sheet at half height, so **Add another workout**, **Can't train** and **Travelling** were cut off below the screen. The sheet now opens sized to its content (design B from `calendar-day-sheet/proposals.png`), up to full screen, and can still be pulled to full height; a close **X** sits top right.
- **Your gym: added equipment no longer reverts** (#271): on build 32 items added to the gym list (ADD ALL or the Equipment page) fell back to 48 items; the Equipment page only has **Done**, and the edit waited for **SAVE** on Your gym. Gym and equipment edits on Your gym are now kept as soon as they're made (`AppModel.editGym`; SAVE and the back button both keep them). Leaving, pausing or finishing a training programme restored the whole profile saved at its start, gym list included; now only what the programme set (goal, split, method, focus, sessions per week) comes back and gym, equipment and days edited meanwhile stay (`ActiveProgramme.SavedPlan.restoring(onto:)`, unit tested). Profile sync no longer marks an edit as sent when it was made while an earlier save was still on its way. New CI job **UI tests (demo mode)** (`MonkeyWorkoutUITests`, demo screen `gym-missing`): ADD ALL, the Equipment page and SAVE keep the new list.
- **Exercise animations: no more flicker, figure fits the equipment** (#256): on build 31 some animations still flickered and some poses looked wrong. (1) Frames per exercise: each exercise now gets the fewest of 17 / 25 / 33 / 49 frames at which no joint moves more than 3.5% of the picture between two frames (`generate.frame_count`, user pick "up to 49, strict"): 245 exercises stay at 17, 177 get more; the app reads `ExerciseIllustrations.frameCounts`. (2) In-betweens: a foot or hand planted in both key poses stays planted (squat and lunge feet used to slide and snap back); equipment drawn by a pose's prop function (elliptical pedals, stability ball, sandbag) now moves with the in-betweens instead of jumping at the middle key; a barbell plate in front of the face fades to see-through. (3) Full review of all 422 animations at 5 frames: 89 fixes, among them muscle-up, archer pull-up and chin-up hands staying on the bar, Turkish get-up and burpee in-betweens, hip thrusts with the back on the bench, machine row / pullover / curl / triceps / crunch / chest press levers and cables connected, dead hang and hanging moves off the floor, bent-over reverse fly, good morning, pike push-up, downward dog, presses passing in front of the face, leg swings beside the pole, jump rope loop. Illustrations grow from 7,174 to 10,046 frames (PNGs about 122 → 166 MB). What's new `2026-10-13.4`.
- **Exercise movement no longer blinks** (#241): on build 30 the movement still blinked. Each step between the 9 frames was a cross-dissolve lasting the whole step, so two poses showed half-transparent on top of each other most of the time (doubled arm and bar, several times a second). Now every exercise has 17 frames (start, middle and end key poses plus eased in-betweens; option C of `exercise-animation/motion-options.mp4`), and the app holds each frame crisp and only dissolves to the next one over the last 20% of the step (`MotionPlayback.blend(window:)`). The in-between fixes found by checking all animations at 17 frames: a limb given without its hand / foot angle now interpolates instead of snapping at the key (shoulder tap push-up), a pose change between two pinned joints keeps the one that stays planted (shoulder tap), and the prone pulldown arms lift and sweep back instead of swinging through the mat. Illustrations grow from about 68 MB to about 122 MB; the CI screenshot job's limit goes from 45 to 60 minutes (it ran out installing the bigger app on the last Watch screens), and the job now boots the iPhone, iPad and Watch simulators while the app builds, so cold boots (about 10 minutes) no longer add to the run. What's new `2026-10-13.3`.
- **Way back after declining Apple Health** (#212): iOS shows the Health permission sheet only once, so a member who declined (e.g. body fat, lean mass, waist from Measurements) had no way back in the app. **Account → Body & Health** now has a pink **Manage Health access** row (design A, `health-access/proposals.png`) opening a sheet with 3 steps (Health → profile → Apps → MonkeyWorkout → Turn On All) and an **Open Health** button; the Measurements note points to it. What's new `2026-10-13.2`.
- **Exercise movement no longer flashes** (#236): on device the picture darkened for one screen frame at every frame step (measured on the build 28 recording: brightness 80 → 69–73 → 80), because each step started a new cross-fade before the last one had finished and hid the frame underneath. Playback is now one continuous frame position on a display-linked timeline (`MotionPlayback` in GymCore): the lower frame stays opaque and the next one blends over it, with no state changes between steps; the path dot glides with it; a tap mid-play glides on from where the picture is. Reduce Motion still shows whole frames. What's new `2026-10-13.2`.
- **Watch complications never appeared** (#235): the complications extension crashed as soon as watchOS asked for its list (`Formatted text for AppIntentRecommendation is not supported`: the My stat recommendations used interpolated `Text`), so no MonkeyWorkout complication showed in the face editor or Smart Stack. Recommendations now use plain text; iOS CI installs the Watch app on a Watch simulator and fails if the extension crashes. What's new `2026-10-13.1`.
- **Training profile cards show full titles** (#231): "Training method" read "Training me…" and subtitles wrapped ("Block / periodization", "Standard · / Supersets") because the big number's slot always took 96 pt since #223. The big number now takes only its own width, cards use slightly tighter spacing, and titles shrink a little before ever truncating. Same on the Training style editor and the workout style card.
- **Account header badges on one line** (#211): on the Account tab the Apple / Verified / PRO badges wrapped letter by letter ("Ap ple", "Ver ifie d", "P R O") because the sessions count squeezed them. Badges now sit on their own full-width row under the photo (PRO in lime) and the sessions count is a lime **2 SESSIONS** pill next to the name (design AB1 from `account-badges/proposals-mix.png`). Badges and pill never wrap. What's new `2026-10-12.6`.
- **Progress counted deleted workouts** (#213): workouts removed on the server (undo on another device, admin undo) stayed on the phone, so Progress showed e.g. 2 sessions while the account had 1. Sync now pulls the full workout list each time and drops records the server no longer has; workouts not yet uploaded are kept.
- **Training style card shows its full name** (#223): Training profile → Training style read "Training st…" with "Standard · Supersets" on two lines, squeezed by the big "60–120 SEC". Rest is now shown in minutes (**1–2**, **2–3**, **½–1 MIN REST**) on that card, the Training style editor and the workout style card, and the big number on these cards shrinks to fit a capped slot instead of taking room from the title (design B, `training-style-card/proposals.png`).
- **Log measurements: one Done button** (#215): the number keyboard above Log / Edit measurements showed **Done** twice (middle and right). The sheet added its own keyboard Done on top of the shared one; now only the trailing **Done** shows.
- **Workout player works in the gym** (#206): each item now shows only the input that fits it, in one place, pinned above the buttons so nothing hides under another card. **Loaded strength**: the set card is the only place to enter kg × reps (steppers, prefilled from last time or the estimate, with the reason under it before set 1); effort (RIR) stays one tap after the set; done sets show as chips with what was logged (e.g. 35×10), tap one to correct it and **Save**. The separate **Log sets** table (a second kg × reps × RIR entry that did not advance the set) is gone. **Timed work and cardio machines** (e.g. Cross-trainer 10 min, even inside a custom or example workout's strength block): labelled and coloured as cardio, no kg/reps, no Attempt PR, just the countdown card (Ready / Remaining / Paused) next to the lime Start / Pause button. **Other multi-set work** (body weight, mobility): set chips only. **No scrolling**: the whole player fits one screen on every iPhone; the exercise picture takes the height left (smaller on small phones), name and muscles stay on one line, Up next is a one-line card above the buttons (also on the rest screen), **Not here?** sits on the picture. The empty grey bars above the buttons (set dots) are replaced by those chips; **Attempt PR** sits beside the target instead of over the picture's muscle map. Demo screens `player-timed`, `player-logged` in CI screenshots.
- **App ready at launch, no Apple Health flash** (#202): Train showed **Connect Apple Health** and no readiness for a few seconds after launch (Account → Body & Health showed no weight) until Health was read. The last Health values are now kept on the device (Application Support, never sent to the server) and shown on the first frame, then refreshed in the background; the Connect card only appears once Health is known to have no weight. Health is read alongside sign-in and sync instead of after them, and its ~20 queries run concurrently.
- **New exercise: close button** (#196): the New / Edit exercise header showed a round **•••** button that did nothing and no close X (iOS 26 folded the leading X into an overflow menu next to the large title). The round **X** now sits top right next to **Save** and closes the sheet.
- **Changing goal now changes this week's workouts** (#194): the week's session types used to depend only on sessions per week, and exercise picks ignored the goal, so e.g. Balanced → Build & define (both 4×) kept the same Upper / Lower days and mostly the same exercises. Now each goal shapes its week: **Build & define** push / pull / legs at 3 and 5 sessions, **Endurance** conditioning days between full-body strength days (2 of 4), **Explosive / athletic** full-body power days leading the week, **Max strength** a power day instead of conditioning at 5; Balanced and Climbing keep their split. Strength picks lean to the goal's kind of exercise (Max strength: barbell compounds; Build: machines and cables for accessories; Endurance: bodyweight, dumbbells, kettlebells; Athletic: single-leg / single-arm), below the "not used yet this week" rule so variety stays. Saving a profile change re-plans the rest of the week: sessions already done and the workout in progress keep their content and stay done (were reset), moves, added workouts and kept swaps stay. Balanced plans unchanged. Engine: `Rules.split(_:_:)`, `Selector.goalBias`, `WeekPlan.keeping(_:from:)`.
- **Remove an extra workout from Train** (#176): a session added with **Add workout** (quick generated extra or library workout) can now be taken off this week from the Train list: swipe the card left, or touch and hold → **Remove from this week** (a library workout placed in place of a planned day shows **Back to planned session**), with confirmation. Done sessions stay. The session page's panel names generated extras "Extra session" (was "From your library"). Synced with the profile; no API change.
- **Explore muscle card names** (#171): on a Volume plan, cards with a target bar no longer shrink the muscle name.
- **Your week recap and card value** (#145): the Your week editor shows the recap (per session, gym / week, per week) at the top instead of above Save. The Training profile **Your week** card shows total hours per week (e.g. 3.5h PER WEEK) and no longer truncates to "…" with the label wrapped letter by letter when the subtitle is long.
- **TestFlight no longer runs out of Apple certificates** (#140): each TestFlight run revokes the development certificate Xcode created for it ("Created via API") once the upload is done, and the **prune_ci_certs** option revokes old CI-made ones before archiving. Certificates made on a Mac are never touched.
- **Similar exercises open their page** (#129): on an exercise page, each Similar exercises row (now with picture and chevron) opens that exercise, pushed in the same sheet; Back returns.
- **Join the community follows Account profile edits** (#122): after setting nickname, photo or about in Account → Profile, the Join screen now switches to the "You'll appear as" card right away (it kept the empty Nickname field until the app was restarted). The agree toggle + Join button footer sits below the scrolling content instead of over it, so the guidelines no longer show through.
- Workout player: the Attempt PR button no longer covers the "Play movement" tag on the exercise picture.
- **Train: day back on each "This week" card** (#96): plans without picked gym/climbing days show the day the training calendar places each session on (with climbing/travel note).
- Apple Watch app icon was still the old green diamond (TestFlight showed it for build 7); now the kettlebell-monkey icon, same as iPhone (#93).
- **Coherence (#89)**: one source for current cycle week and phase (Cycle, Calendar, Add to plan agree); Calendar session count matches its day tiles and hotel-gym item count matches Away; one volume format everywhere ("6.5 t"); Start workout bar no longer covers content; RPE legend includes 6; "−40%"; bottom padding on Cycle and Readiness; rest-drill card shows category icon instead of an unreadable thumbnail; Reschedule shows today's date instead of an emoji; Builder demo uses machine equipment.
- **Release integration check**: one-section-per-type ChangeLog (merges had duplicated Added/Fixed/Changed blocks); account export now includes notification preferences and registered devices (token shortened); backend test `integration.test.ts` runs one journey across all streams (profile, custom exercise, shared library workout, PR attempt + follower push, community post + cheer, notification prefs, export, block, account deletion leaves no rows). Staging deploy and production promote fall back to the flyctl installer when `setup-flyctl` leaves no `flyctl` on PATH (one staging deploy failed that way). Naming: built-in "Lowerbody" → "Lower body", Account row "Body & Apple Health" → "Body & Health" (matches the screen); library card duration reads "25 min" instead of a prime glyph that looked like a comma.
- Explore body map: tapping any muscle opened Calves (#79). Each muscle now opens its own page (front and back, mirrored sides too); same fix for the tappable maps on session and exercise pages, and display-only maps no longer swallow taps. Taps resolved by `BodyMapLayout` (WorkoutEngine, tested); muscle page exercise rows show the exercise picture; VoiceOver gets one action per muscle.
- Training profile: climbing no longer depends on the goal (#49). An independent **I climb** switch (any goal) shows climbing days/weekdays; changing goal never clears them (#40 cleared them for non-climbing goals); Climbing performance turns it on; new profiles start on when Apple Health shows climbing workouts. Health climbing-days hint back for all goals.
- Home Screen name of TestFlight (Staging) builds now "MonkeyWorkout" instead of "Monkey STG" (#35).
- Body & Health: Gender and Sex picks were reset on return from the picker list (form reloaded on every appear); Save now returns to the previous screen (#38).
- Keyboard in forms (Account, Body & Health, password/email screens…) can now be dismissed: Done button above the keyboard and swipe-down on the form. Account name saves when you leave the field, not only on Return (#42).
- **TestFlight distribute** no longer fails on internal groups with automatic distribution (Apple rejects manual assignment there; those groups already get every build).
- TestFlight uploads after the first failed ("bundle version must be higher than 1"): generated Info.plist hard-coded version 1.0 build 1. It now uses `MARKETING_VERSION` (set to 1.0, matching App Store Connect) and `CURRENT_PROJECT_VERSION` (CI run number).
- Email on staging/prod: SES Zurich (eu-central-2) has no SMTP endpoint, so sign-up failed with 500 (`ENOTFOUND email-smtp.eu-central-2.amazonaws.com`). API now sends via the SES HTTPS API (`SendRawEmail`, SigV4, same send-only IAM user); **Fly setup** pushes `SES_*` secrets and drops the old `SMTP_URL`. Smoke test accepts `ses` (#26).
- iOS local state decodes leniently: state saved by older builds no longer resets to empty when new fields are missing.
- API container: entrypoint fixes ownership of root-owned `/data` mounts (Fly volumes) before dropping to the `node` user (#3).

### Security
- **Dependency updates** (Dependabot #317–#326): passkey library `@simplewebauthn/server` 13 → 14 (type `AuthenticatorTransportFuture` → `AuthenticatorTransport`), `@fastify/rate-limit` 11.2.1, `nodemailer` 10.1.0, `zod` 4.6.7; GitHub Actions `checkout`, `setup-node`, `upload-artifact` v4 → v7, `docker/login-action` and `docker/setup-buildx-action` v3 → v4. `@types/node` stays on 22 to match the Node 22 runtime (#324 not taken).
- **Fly backups: member emails masked in the log** (#333): `member-restore` / `member-delete` printed the member's email in the public Actions log (job env). Email and confirm are now read from the event file and masked before any step prints, as is the encoded restore request.
- **Fly backups: Tigris keys kept out of the log** (#327): `offsite-setup` printed the new Tigris bucket keys (from `flyctl storage create`) into the public Actions log on its first staging run; the log was deleted within minutes. Its output is now hidden, a new `offsite-rotate` action destroys and recreates the bucket to issue new keys (run on staging), and setting `BACKUP_KEY` retries on Fly API errors. A failed bucket create shows its error with key lines removed; `backup-now` also uploads the encrypted off-site copy; rotate destroys every `<app>-backups*` bucket and creates one under a fresh name.
